*Sait Furkan Teke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文描述了ufakzeka-1，一个从零开始预训练的151M参数土耳其语解码器模型，总成本约286美元，并详细记录了构建过程中的关键发现：包括数据安全检查门被"修复"但实际未修复的问题、训练种子方差与配方差异相当、以及仅靠数据无法修复模型尺寸限制导致的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望以低成本构建一个小型土耳其语模型，并通过透明的全过程记录（包括数据构建、评估、成本明细），为其他小型模型研究工作提供可迁移的经验教训，特别是关于安全评估、实验比较方法和数据修复局限性的发现。

Method: 模型为151M参数（含嵌入182M）的仅解码器土耳其语模型，从零开始在13.5B token的开放许可文本上预训练，采用三阶段预训练计划、土耳其语字节级分词器（每词1.77 token）、以及开放许可与生成数据混合的后训练。评估包括发布门控、5,508个对话的规则检查扫描、人工评判和手测，所有提示词均从训练数据中保留，并通过数据构建中的去污染和版本控制不变量脚本强制执行。

Result: 发现三个关键结果：1）一个通过训练数据"修复"的安全门实际读数为34/64而非报告的64/64；2）训练种子方差与所有尝试配方之间的差异相当，单种子比较在此规模下无信息量；3）数据轮次只修复了数据中缺失的部分，而长上下文身份追踪和多轮算术在所有数据变更下均无改善，被视为模型尺寸限制而非数据缺口。

Conclusion: 该模型的贡献不在于其能力（符合该尺寸模型的预期），而在于完整的构建和测量记录。权重、数据配方、评估代码和支出账本均以Apache-2.0协议开源，后续更大模型将验证"模型尺寸而非数据缺口"这一假设。

Abstract: We describe ufakzeka-1, a 151M-parameter (182M with embeddings) decoder-only Turkish language model pretrained from scratch on 13.5B tokens of openly licensed text and instruction-tuned for chat, at a total cost of about \$286 in cloud GPU, API and notebook time. The contribution is not the model's capability, which is what a model this size can be expected to have, but the record of building and measuring it: a Turkish byte-level tokenizer at 1.77 tokens per word, a three-stage pretraining schedule, a post-training mixture of openly licensed and generated data, and an evaluation battery of release gates, a rule-checked sweep of 5,508 conversations, judged conversations and hand tests, all with prompts held out from the training data, enforced by decontamination inside the data build and by a checked-in invariant script we run before each build. We report three findings that we believe transfer to other small-model efforts: a safety gate that had been "fixed" with training data written from its own questions read 64/64 while the honest figure was 34/64; training-seed variance was as large as the spread across every recipe we tried, so single-seed comparisons at this scale are uninformative; and data rounds repaired only what was absent from the data, while identity tracking over long context and multi-turn arithmetic did not move across any data change we tried, which we read as limits of the model size rather than gaps in the data, a reading the next, larger model will test. Weights, the data recipe, the evaluation code and the spend ledger are released under Apache-2.0.

</details>


### [50] [Impact Is Not Invalidation: Ask About the Claim, Not the Diff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25130)
*Atul Anand*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了编程智能体记忆系统中声明失效检测的问题。作者发现基于语义等价分类（判断diff是否保持行为）的信号在真实提交上表现极差（精度仅0.291-0.329），而将问题改为"某个具体声明是否仍然成立"后，同样的模型在同一diff上精度提升至0.705-0.974。实验证明问题不在于模型能力，而在于任务本身的设计——行为保持判断是模糊的，而具体声明的真值验证才是可判定的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程智能体的记忆系统需要判定在仓库变更后哪些已存储的声明（claim）已失效。两种基线方案都有问题：内容锚定（content anchoring）在工件变化时失效声明，但变化过于频繁；语义等价分类（semantic-equivalence classification）试图判断diff是否保持行为，但作者怀疑这个信号本身存在根本性缺陷，而非模型能力不足。

Method: 作者构建了基于执行真值（execution ground truth）的数据集：声明定义为在提交t处通过的测试函数，若相同断言文本在t+1处失败则视为翻转（flipped）。关键观察是CI门控的主线中，无法合并留下既有测试失败的提交，因此朴素构建会产生空的阳性类别。作者从23个Python库中挖掘了10,369个声明和184个执行验证的翻转，设计了按仓库held-out拆分、知识截止后拆分、shuffled-diff对照、释义对照以及17个仓库的leave-one-repository-out分析。实验对比了五种价格跨40x的模型在两种问题设置下的表现，并对比了pytest-testmon。

Result: 五种模型在"该提交是否保持行为"判断上对59-72%真实提交触发，精度仅0.291-0.329（基线0.25）；改为"某个具体声明是否仍然成立"后，同一模型在同一diff上精度提升至0.705-0.974。行为保持判断器被提供声明文本后精度仅变化0.010和0.016，而改变问题本身使精度变化0.49和0.65。pytest-testmon达到0.868召回和0.415精度，说明几乎完整的可达性知识并不能识别什么被证伪。

Conclusion: 语义等价分类的失败不是模型能力问题，而是问题本身的设计缺陷——"diff是否保持行为"是模糊且不可判定的问法。正确的问题应该是针对具体声明是否为真的验证。执行（execution）而非标注（annotation）才是声明真值的正确基准。

Abstract: Memory systems for coding agents must decide, when a repository changes, which of their stored claims have become false. Content anchoring invalidates a claim whenever the artifact it came from changes, which fires constantly. Semantic-equivalence classification asks whether a diff preserves behavior, a question about the diff rather than about any stored claim. We show the second signal fails for a reason unrelated to model capability: asked whether a commit preserves behavior, five models spanning a 40x price range fire on 59-72% of real commits and reach precisions of only 0.291 to 0.329 against a 0.25 base rate. Asked instead whether one specific claim still holds, the same models on the same diffs reach 0.705 to 0.974. A control that hands the behavior-preservation judge the claim text, changing only the question, moves precision by 0.010 and 0.016; changing the question moves it by 0.49 and 0.65. We also compare against pytest-testmon, a deployed regression-test selector with coverage-derived dependency data: it reaches 0.868 recall at 0.415 precision, so near-complete knowledge of what a change can reach does not identify what it falsifies. Ground truth is execution, not annotation: a claim is a test function passing at commit t, and it has flipped if that same assertion text fails at t+1. Building this required an observation we did not find in prior work. On a CI-gated mainline a commit that leaves a pre-existing test failing cannot merge, so the naive construction has an empty positive class by design. We report 10,369 claims with 184 execution-verified flips mined from 23 Python libraries, splits held out by repository, a post-knowledge-cutoff split, a shuffled-diff null, a paraphrase control, and a leave-one-repository-out analysis over 17 repositories.

</details>


### [51] [FinFIRST: Benchmarking Search Agents for Financial Information Retrieval, Sourcing and Traceability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25192)
*Wenqing Wang,Haitao Xiang,Xinyi Zhao,Mingming Yin,Ying Zhong,Zhaoxin Huan,Qiheng Zhou,Jin Zhu,Xiaolu Zhang,Shi Chang,Jun Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FinFIRST，首个通过原子化评分标准同时评估LLM智能体金融搜索的最终答案与支撑证据的基准，包含123个专家撰写任务及证据支撑的参考包，并在15种模型配置下验证，使研究过程可量化、可验证、可诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融搜索对LLM智能体要求极高，不仅需要正确答案，还需时间有效性检索、权威来源选取、实体与时期对齐、单位与定义一致性以及所有结论的可验证证据。现有基准仅评估最终答案，导致难以定位错误或判断答案是否有充分依据，因此亟需一个能同时评估答案和支撑证据的基准。

Method: FinFIRST构建了123个专家撰写的、难度梯度跨越的任务，基于真实金融场景的聚合模式生成，采用18字段分类体系、六轴覆盖蓝图、138个金融来源注册表、超过50位金融专家参与以及六阶段质量控制流程。每个任务配有以证据为支撑的参考包，分解为三个维度的原子标准：原始信息获取、来源验证、计算与答案形成。模型在统一工具设置下接受评估。

Result: 在15种模型配置下评估显示：Claude-Opus-5以87.59%获得最高原子评分，GPT-5.6-Sol以71.54%获得最高严格通过率。所有系统中，计算与答案形成环节的表现均落后于原始信息获取环节。

Conclusion: FinFIRST在保持最终答案正确性为首要目标的同时，使支撑研究过程变得可衡量、可验证、可诊断，填补了现有基准仅关注结果的空白。

Abstract: Financial search is a highly demanding task for LLM agents, requiring not only a correct final answer but also temporally valid information retrieval, authoritative source selection, entity and period alignment, unit and definition consistency, and verifiable evidence for all conclusions. Existing benchmarks predominantly evaluate only the final answer, making it difficult to localize errors or assess whether an answer is well-founded. To address this gap, we introduce FinFIRST (Financial Information Retrieval, Sourcing and Traceability), the first financial benchmark to jointly evaluate answers and supporting evidence through atomic rubrics. FinFIRST comprises 123 expert-authored tasks spanning a graduated difficulty spectrum, constructed from aggregate patterns of real-world financial scenarios through an 18-field taxonomy, a six-axis coverage blueprint, a registry of 138 financial sources, contributions from over 50 finance experts, and a six-stage quality-control pipeline. Each task is accompanied by an evidence-grounded reference package decomposed into atomic criteria across three dimensions: raw-information acquisition, source verification, and computation and answer formation. We evaluate 15 model configurations under a unified tool setting. Claude-Opus-5 achieves the highest atomic score of 87.59%, while GPT-5.6-Sol attains the highest strict pass rate of 71.54%. Computation and answer formation consistently lag behind raw-information acquisition across systems. FinFIRST retains final-answer correctness as the primary objective while making the supporting research process measurable, verifiable, and diagnosable.

</details>


### [52] [FineWeb-CLaR: Culture, Language, and Region Annotations for Benchmark-Aligned Corpus Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25298)
*Yusser Al Ghussin,Eva Gavaller,Cristina España-Bonet,Josef van Genabith,Simon Ostermann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文引入FineWeb-CLaR，一个基于FineWeb和FineWeb-2的大规模注释数据集，通过共享的文化-语言-区域轴来对齐预训练语料库与文化NLP基准测试，支持语料审计和基准对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化评估覆盖率和鲁棒性难以诊断，因为预训练语料库和文化基准测试很少用可比元数据进行索引。现有基准测试针对语言、区域和本地实践级别，而网络规模语料库通常仅按语言组织，缺乏共享的文化-语言-区域层次。

Method: 从FineWeb和FineWeb-2的309亿文档全集出发，使用URL派生的区域标签和文化主题来源进行注释。区域解析器为25.61%的文档（79.2亿）分配了非空区域。对于文化主题分析，诱导特定区域主题并将其投影到Liu等人（2025）文化分类法的14个叶子上，生成用于语料库侧比较的本地主题分布（LTDs）。同时使用相同分类法、语言覆盖率和区域覆盖率注释277个文化NLP基准测试。

Result: FineWeb-CLaR成功为大型网络语料库提供文化-语言-区域三轴注释，覆盖309亿文档，其中79.2亿文档具有区域标签，并同时注释了277个文化NLP基准测试，实现了语料库侧与基准侧的直接比较。

Conclusion: 通过共享的文化分类层次，FineWeb-CLaR使得预训练证据与基准评估覆盖率之间的直接比较成为可能，能够在语料审计和基准对齐方面支持对目标文化现象在预训练数据和基准测试中的代表性进行系统性诊断。

Abstract: Cultural evaluation coverage and robustness in language models are difficult to diagnose because pretraining corpora and cultural benchmarks are rarely indexed with comparable metadata. Benchmarks increasingly target culturally situated phenomena at the level of languages, regions, and locale-specific practices, while web-scale corpora are usually organized only by language. A shared culture-language-region layer makes these resources comparable, enabling audits of whether a target cultural phenomenon is represented in pretraining data, evaluated by benchmarks or both. To this end, we introduce FineWeb-CLaR, a large-scale annotated dataset derived from FineWeb and FineWeb-2 that places web documents on a shared culture-language-region axis for corpus auditing and benchmark alignment.
  FineWeb-CLaR annotates the full 30.9B-document collection from FineWeb and FineWeb-2 with URL-derived region labels and cultural-topic provenance. Our region resolver assigns a non-empty region to 25.61% of documents (7.92B). For cultural-topic analysis, we induce locale-specific topics and project them onto the 14 leaves of the Cultural Taxonomy of Liu et al. (2025), producing Locale Topic Distributions (LTDs) for corpus-side comparison. We also annotate 277 cultural NLP benchmarks with the same taxonomy, language coverage, and region coverage. Together, these resources enable direct comparison between corpus-side pretraining evidence and benchmark-side evaluation coverage.

</details>


### [53] [TelecomGPT-R1: Unified Post-Training for Reasoning Across Heterogeneous Telecom Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25356)
*Bohao Wang,Chenwei Wu,Hang Zou,Yu Tian,Lina Bariah,Li Wei,Chongwen Huang,Yongliang Shen,Zhaoyang Zhang,Merouane Debbah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TelecomGPT-R1 是一个开源统一的电信推理模型家族，围绕协议、知识、建模和故障四个互补轴设计，通过轴感知数据生成框架构建104,880条训练语料，结合SFT和DAPO（动态采样策略优化）强化学习，在GSMA Open Telco Leaderboard七个基准上以89.64%平均分超越GPT-5、Claude和Gemini等领先专有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电信大语言模型难以在标准、网络配置、数学模型、源代码和运维日志等多类异构任务与数据类型上进行可靠推理。通用LLM缺乏电信领域知识扎根，而电信专用模型通常面向狭窄任务族、多任务性能有限，需要统一的电信推理模型来填补这一空白。

Method: （1）提出轴感知数据生成框架，将粗粒度电信公开工件精炼为验证过的问答对及高质量链式思维（CoT）推理轨迹，构建含104,880条样本的训练语料；（2）利用SFT注入电信知识和基于证据的推理模式，解决RL的冷启动问题；（3）应用动态采样策略优化（DAPO）结合任务路由的评分奖励，将轴特定CoT轨迹分解为可验证的推理单元，结合grounded密集过程评分与结果正确性，使RL从可验证的电信证据中学习通用问题求解行为。

Result: 在GSMA Open Telco Leaderboard的七个基准上，开源的TelecomGPT-R1-27B平均分达89.64%，超越包括GPT-5、Claude和Gemini在内的领先专有模型。模型和可复现的训练流程已开源。

Conclusion: TelecomGPT-R1证明了通过轴感知数据生成、SFT冷启动和基于证据的RL优化相结合，可以构建出跨协议、知识、建模和故障四大类电信推理任务上超越专有模型的开源统一电信推理模型，为社区进一步开发提供了可复现的基线。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) offer great potential to automate a broad range of telecom engineering tasks by reasoning over standards, network configurations, mathematical models, source code, and operational logs. However, existing telecom LLMs struggle to reliably reason across these diverse tasks and data types. General-purpose LLMs often lack reliable grounding in telecom-specific knowledge, while telecom-specialized models are typically developed for narrower task families and exhibit limited multi-task performance. To fill this gap, we introduce TelecomGPT-R1, a family of open source unified telecom reasoning models structured around four complementary axes: protocol, knowledge, modeling, and fault. We first develop an axis-aware data generation framework that refines coarse public telecom artifacts into verified question-answer pairs and high quality chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning trajectories, yielding a training corpus containing 104,880 examples. Building on this corpus, supervised fine-tuning (SFT) instills telecom knowledge and evidence-grounded reasoning patterns to overcome the cold start barrier for reinforcement learning (RL). We then apply dynamic sampling policy optimization (DAPO) with task-routed rubric rewards to keep RL updates informative and stable across heterogeneous telecom reasoning tasks. These rewards decompose axis-specific CoT traces into verifiable reasoning units and combine grounded dense process credit with outcome correctness, allowing RL to learn generalizable problem solving behaviors from verifiable telecom evidence. We release the TelecomGPT-R1 models and a reproducible training recipe to support further community development. Evaluations on seven benchmarks of the GSMA Open Telco Leaderboard show that the open-source TelecomGPT-R1-27B achieves an 89.64% mean score, outperforming leading proprietary models, including GPT-5, Claude, and Gemini.

</details>


### [54] [Passes Alone, Fails Together: Benchmarking Semantic Coordination in Parallel LLM-Agent Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25396)
*Haocheng Xia,Eugene Wu,Yongjoo Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究并行编码代理在合并后产生补丁冲突的问题（语义协调失败），提出了名为stale的基准测试，包含三层任务（受控接口变更的合成任务、真实Django合并的拉取请求对、使用真实Django辅助函数的构造任务）。研究发现：在417对真实Django拉取请求中仅1对出现干扰，而在12个Django辅助函数的构造任务中97%的运行出现干扰；一条描述并发变更的消息可恢复82%的运行。结论是构造任务的失败率不能直接估计实践中的实际发生频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行编码代理生成的补丁单独运行时正常，但合并后可能失败，原因是一个代理修改了接口或规则，而另一个代理仍依赖该接口/规则。目前缺少针对这种语义协调失败的系统性研究和基准测试，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出stale基准测试，采用三层评估体系：(1)具有受控接口变更的合成任务；(2)真实合并的拉取请求对（417对Django拉取请求，834次运行）；(3)使用真实Django辅助函数构造的任务（12个辅助函数）。评估方法为对每个补丁单独运行相同测试及对合并结果运行测试，仅计数由合并引入的失败，并修正了评分流程。

Result: 修正评分流程后，417对真实Django拉取请求中仅1对表现出干扰；在12个Django辅助函数的构造任务中，97%的运行出现干扰；一条描述并发变更完成情况的消息能恢复82%的运行。

Conclusion: 实际审查过的拉取请求中可能几乎不含未解决的并行变更，即使代理在基于真实代码的受控任务上频繁失败。因此，构造任务的失败率不能用于估计这些问题在实际中的发生频率，说明需要区分基准测试的严苛性与真实场景的普遍性。

Abstract: Parallel coding agents can produce patches that work alone but fail when merged. This happens when one agent changes an interface or rule that another agent still relies on. We study these failures with stale, a benchmark for semantic coordination. Our evaluation runs the same tests on each patch alone and on their combination, counting only failures introduced by combining the patches. We use three tiers: synthetic tasks with controlled interface changes, pairs of merged pull requests, and constructed tasks that use real Django helpers. Among 834 runs on 417 mined Django pairs, only one showed interference after correcting the grading procedure. On constructed tasks using 12 Django helpers, interference occurred in 97% of runs. A message describing the completed concurrent change recovered 82% of runs. Reviewed pull requests may contain few unresolved parallel changes, even when agents fail on controlled tasks using real code. The constructed failure rates do not estimate how often these problems occur in practice.

</details>


### [55] [Mining Legal Arguments in U.S. Corporate Case Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25441)
*Luis Brena,William Jurayj,Gregory Deyesu,Zaid Al-Huneidi,Andrew Blair-Stanek,Benjamin Van Durme*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个专家标注的美国联邦税务意见书论证语料库，涵盖42篇关于I.R.C. §368公司重组的案例。这是该领域首个专家标注的树结构论证语料，包含五种功能标签（Rule、Analysis、Conclusion、Background Facts、Procedural History），支持跨度级、句级、扁平及树结构四种表示形式。实验表明功能标签可学习但跨案例泛化仍较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律论证挖掘（Legal Argument Mining）在段落分类、检索和论证补全方面有广泛应用，但此前缺乏针对美国联邦税务法领域、经过专家标注的树结构论证语料库。作者旨在填补这一空白，为法律论证挖掘提供基准资源。

Method: 构建了42篇美国联邦税务意见书的专家标注语料库，涵盖I.R.C. §368公司重组案例。标注方案包括：五种功能标签（Rule、Analysis、Conclusion、Background Facts、Procedural History），其中Rule、Analysis、Conclusion跨度可链接为有向支持树，Background Facts和Procedural History承担上下文功能。语料提供跨度级、句级、扁平及树结构四种表示形式，并进行分类与检索实验。

Result: 一致性分析显示功能节点标签比有向支持边和隐式中间结论更可靠；有向路径一致性优于直接边一致性，表明广义可达性比精确局部分解更稳定。分类实验显示功能标签在案例不相交评估下可学习；检索实验显示监督微调改善了案例内检索，但跨案例泛化仍然薄弱。

Conclusion: 该语料库支持法律段落分类，并为美国联邦税务判例法中的结构化论证挖掘提供了保守基准。同时，跨案例泛化弱的问题表明该领域仍需进一步研究以实现更好的知识迁移。

Abstract: Legal argument mining supports passage classification, retrieval, and argument completion. This work introduces an expert-annotated dataset of 42 U.S. federal tax opinions on corporate reorganizations under I.R.C. §368. To our knowledge, it is the first expert-annotated, tree-structured argument corpus for this domain. Explicit spans receive one of five functional labels: Rule, Analysis, Conclusion, Background Facts, and Procedural History. Rule, Analysis, and Conclusion spans can be linked into directed support trees, while Background Facts and Procedural History serve a contextual function. The corpus provides span-based, sentence-based, flat, and tree-structured representations. Agreement analysis shows that functional node labels are more reliable than directed support edges and implicit intermediate conclusions. Directed-path agreement is stronger than direct-edge agreement, which indicates that broad reachability is more stable than exact local decomposition. Classification experiments show that functional labels are learnable under case-disjoint evaluation. Retrieval experiments show that supervised fine-tuning improves within-case retrieval. However, cross-case generalization remains weak. The dataset supports legal passage classification and provides a conservative benchmark for structured argument mining in U.S. federal tax case law.

</details>


### [56] [Conduct Under Pressure: What Sixty Language Models Do When a User Pushes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25447)
*Tapan Parikh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在面对用户施加压力（坚持、恳求、奉承、悲痛）时的行为表现，将相同的多轮场景发送给来自13家供应商的60个模型，并通过冻结的编码本标注每个对话记录。研究发现两个独立结论：模型是否坚持立场与其生成能力（生成时间/能力指数）相关（折叠率与公共能力指数Spearman相关系数为-0.64，供应商效应很小）；而模型如何坚持/折叠则与供应商相关（17种方式编码中有6种按供应商显著区分）。此外，论文评估了标注工作中哪些环节需要人工参与，发现LLM编码员比人工编码员更一致（Krippendorff's alpha 0.66对比0.46），表明对于非专家可判断的行为，人工的贡献在于编写和界定编码本、拥有小规模参考集，而非大规模生产标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解LLM在面对用户施压（如坚持、恳求、奉承或悲痛）时是否会放弃正确事实、写出应拒绝的文件或赞同会让用户花钱的计划。作者希望通过系统化的多模型对比，揭示模型"是否坚持"和"如何坚持"分别由什么因素决定，并探讨标注工作的人力需求。

Method: 作者构建了冻结的多轮对话场景（对每个模型完全相同，不受模型回复影响），将其发送给来自13个供应商的60个模型，使用通过开放编码构建后冻结的编码本对每个对话记录进行标注。编码本包含轨迹（模型坚持立场还是让步）和方式（如何坚持/让步）两个维度。此外，作者设计了LLM编码员与人类编码员的对比实验，使用Krippendorff's alpha和Cohen's kappa评估一致性，并进行盲法机器阅读以验证编码类别的可恢复性。

Result: （1）模型是否坚持与其生成能力（模型新近程度）相关：折叠率与公共能力指数呈Spearman -0.64的负相关，供应商效应很小。（2）模型如何坚持/折叠与其供应商相关：17种方式编码中有6种在排列检验p<=0.001水平上按供应商显著区分（全编码本校正后），论文报告了四个在通过可靠性检验的编码上呈现的供应商画像。（3）在标注一致性方面，来自三家供应商的六个LLM编码员比三个人类编码员更一致（alpha 0.66对比0.46），与编码本作者在轨迹判断上kappa达到0.84-0.91（编码本示例未涉及的对话记录），与仲裁后的人类参考匹配度为0.83。（4）盲法机器阅读能恢复编码本的类别，但无法判断第二位读者会对哪些类别应用相同的标注。

Conclusion: 对于非专家即可判断的行为，人工在标注工作中的核心贡献不在于大规模生产标签，而在于编写和界定编码本、拥有小规模参考集。研究还揭示了一个重要的二分结构：模型"是否屈服"由能力驱动（与供应商无关），而"如何屈服"由供应商/模型身份驱动。

Abstract: We study what LLMs do when a user applies pressure in an uncomfortable situation: a user insists, begs, flatters or grieves, and the model gives up a correct fact, writes a document it should refuse, or cheers a plan that will cost the user money. We send frozen multi-turn scenes, identical for every model regardless of the reply, to 60 models from 13 vendors, and label each transcript with a codebook built by open coding and then frozen: a trajectory (the model held its position or folded) and a manner (how it held or folded). Two findings separate. Whether a model holds tracks its generation, meaning how recent it is: fold rate correlates with a public capability index at Spearman -0.64, with little vendor effect. How it holds tracks the vendor: six of the 17 manner codes sort by vendor at permutation p <= 0.001, corrected across the codebook. We report four vendor profiles on the codes that cleared reliability.
  We also ask which parts of the labeling need a person. Six LLM coders from three vendors apply the codebook more consistently than three human coders do (Krippendorff's alpha 0.66 against 0.46), agree with the codebook's author on trajectory at kappa 0.84 to 0.91 on transcripts the codebook's examples never touched, and match an adjudicated human reference at 0.83. Blind machine readings recover the codebook's categories but cannot tell which of them a second reader would apply the same way. We conclude that for behavior a non-specialist can judge, the human contribution is authoring and bounding the codes and owning a small reference, not producing labels at volume.

</details>


### [57] [Matryoshka attribution: Learning to attribute language model outputs to representations and weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25518)
*Aryaman Arora,Kirill Acharya,Nathan Hu,Yanzhe Zhang,Noah Goodman,Dan Jurafsky,Christopher Potts*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Matryoshka Attribution (MAttr)，一种通过可微sigmoid top-k算子学习掩码、同时监督所有稀疏度来识别因果重要内部组件的方法，在机制可解释性基准上排名第一，并能用于RL识别微调中的权重变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解释LM输出的方法（因果干预、梯度、可学习掩码）要么成本过高，要么难以识别真正因果重要的内部计算，因此需要一种可学习的、高效的归因框架。

Method: 将归因定义为识别最小化下游损失的嵌套子集问题；用参数化的可微sigmoid top-k算子作为掩码，并在训练中随机化k以同时监督所有稀疏度，从而学习组件按归因分数的排序。

Result: MAttr在Mechanistic Interpretability Benchmark官方排行榜上取得第一，能识别稀疏且可跨电路基座迁移的电路；在LLM微调场景中，用RL训练MAttr识别权重变化，仅恢复Llama 3.1 8B Instruct模型1%的权重到基础模型状态即可去除拒答行为且保持能力。

Conclusion: MAttr将可解释性成功转化为可通过梯度下降学习的可优化目标，并鼓励未来沿此方向研究。

Abstract: Attributing language model outputs to their internal computations is an open problem in interpretability. Existing methods, which use causal interventions, gradients, or learnable masks, either are infeasibly expensive or struggle to identify actual causally-important internal computations. We propose framing attribution as the problem of identifying nested subsets of internal components which minimise a downstream loss. To learn this task, we introduce Matryoshka Attribution (MAttr), a mask learning method that parametrises the mask with a simple differentiable sigmoid top-$k$ operator. We supervise training over all sparsities simultaneously by randomising $k$ over training, resulting in a learned ordering of components by attribution score. MAttr achieves number 1 on the official leaderboard of the Mechanistic Interpretability Benchmark (Mueller et al., 2025); our method identifies sparse and task-transferrable circuits across varying circuit bases. As a practical application, we show that MAttr can be trained with reinforcement learning to identify weight changes responsible for downstream behaviours in LLM finetuning. We train MAttr on refusal judge scores and find that restoring $1\%$ of Llama 3.1 8B Instruct's weights to their base model state is sufficient to remove refusals while maintaining capabilities. We view MAttr as a successful formulation of interpretability into a learnable objective that we can tackle with gradient descent, and encourage future work along these lines.

</details>


### [58] [Compressing Long Context into Answer-Aligned Memory Embeddings for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25537)
*Md Mostafizer Rahman,Md Faizul Ibne Amin,Md Shahajada Mia,Yutaka Watanobe,Fang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CMC（上下文到答案对齐的记忆压缩）框架，将长输入上下文压缩为与任意冻结解码器嵌入空间对齐的紧凑上下文记忆嵌入（CME），在不修改解码器权重的情况下降低推理成本。通过双层KV缓存（问题引导的CME选择+局部上下文窗口）和来自冻结LLM的答案目标蒸馏进行训练，在四个QA基准和九种编码器-解码器组合上超越基线，在SQuAD上取得最高7.3 EM和4.0 F1提升，同时将推理时间和能耗降低20%，峰值GPU内存占用降低50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理受限于自注意力二次方复杂度和KV缓存线性增长，导致随着上下文长度增加，延迟、能耗和GPU内存需求显著上升。现有软压缩方法存在三个缺陷：推理时缺乏查询引导的记忆选择、训练缺乏答案目标的监督、压缩与特定解码器架构紧密耦合，限制了泛化性和效率。

Method: CMC框架的核心包括：(1) 将长输入上下文压缩为紧凑的上下文记忆嵌入（CME），并通过对齐到冻结解码器的嵌入空间实现架构无关性；(2) 引入双层KV缓存，结合问题引导的CME选择与局部上下文窗口，既保留全局语义又兼顾细粒度局部信息；(3) 采用冻结LLM的答案目标蒸馏训练压缩器，使压缩过程直接服务于答案生成任务。

Result: 在九个编码器-解码器组合和四个QA基准上的实验表明，CMC一致优于基线：在SQuAD上最高取得7.3 EM和4.0 F1点的提升；在3000个生成token的设定下，推理时间和能耗降低最高20%，峰值保留GPU内存降低最高50%。消融实验证实了每个架构组件和训练目标的贡献。

Conclusion: CMC通过将压缩与解码器解耦、引入查询引导的记忆选择以及答案目标的蒸馏训练，有效解决了LLM长上下文推理中的效率瓶颈，在保持甚至提升QA性能的同时显著降低推理资源消耗，且具有良好的架构适应性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference is constrained by the quadratic scaling of self-attention and the linear scaling of the KV cache, increasing latency, energy consumption, and GPU memory demand as context length scales. Existing soft-compression methods either lack query-guided memory selection at inference time, train without answer-targeted supervision, or couple compression tightly to a specific decoder architecture. We propose a Context-to-Answer-Aligned Memory Compression (CMC) framework, which compresses long input contexts into compact Context Memory Embeddings (CMEs) aligned to any frozen decoder's embedding space, reducing inference costs without modifying decoder weights. CMC introduces a two-tier KV cache that combines question-guided CME selection with a local context window, and trains the compressor with answer-targeted distillation from a frozen LLM. Experiments across nine encoder-decoder combinations and four QA benchmarks show that CMC consistently outperforms the baseline, achieving up to 7.3 EM and 4.0 F1 point gains on SQuAD, while reducing inference time and energy consumption by up to 20% and peak reserved GPU memory by up to 50% at 3,000 generation tokens. Ablation studies confirm that each architectural component and training objective contributes to the performance.

</details>


### [59] [Qwen3.8-Omni: Towards Native Omni-Modal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25611)
*Qwen Team*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash，一个原生多模态智能体模型，支持百万token上下文、长程智能体任务和真实世界多模态生产力应用，并配套发布了Qwen-MM-Plugins插件框架和Qwen-Live-Harness实时智能体框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态（omni）模型主要强调感知和交互能力，在多模态理解、推理以及长程智能体任务上表现不足，难以满足真实世界多模态生产力场景的需求。此外，现有智能体工具链缺乏对原生音视频任务的支持。

Method: 采用原生多模态协同训练策略，在保持强文本能力的同时将智能体能力从文本域迁移到音频和视频任务；继承Qwen3.8-Next的稀疏混合专家（MoE）架构，并将上下文窗口扩展至一百万token；发布Qwen-MM-Plugins开源插件框架（支持视频编辑、长音视频翻译、音乐条件视频生成等）以及Qwen-Live-Harness开源实时多模态智能体编排框架（管理上下文、记忆、工具调用和子智能体委派）。

Result: 广泛评估表明Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash在多模态理解、推理、长程智能体执行和视频生产力任务上均取得强性能。

Conclusion: Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash及其配套开源工具可作为在研究和生产环境中部署原生多模态智能体的实用基础，支持其作为主智能体或专门子智能体集成到生产工作流中。

Abstract: We introduce Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash, a natively multimodal agentic model for real-world multimodal productivity. Compared with previous omni models, which primarily emphasized perception and interaction, Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash substantially improves multimodal understanding and reasoning, as well as performance on long-horizon agentic tasks. These capabilities are supported by a native multimodal co-training strategy that preserves strong text-domain capabilities while facilitating the transfer of agentic capabilities from text to audio and video tasks. The model inherits the sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) architecture of Qwen3.8-Next and extends the context window to one million tokens, supporting long-context multimodal reasoning and long-horizon planning. These advances enable integration into production workflows as a primary agent or a specialized sub-agent, supporting video editing, long-form audio and video translation, music-conditioned music video or movie generation, and video-based note or omni-skill creation. To address the lack of native audio and video support in existing agent harnesses, we release Qwen-MM-Plugins, a lightweight open-source plugin framework for multimodal productivity. We further frame real-time multimodal interaction as a system-level challenge requiring orchestration of context and memory management, tool use, and sub-agent delegation. Accordingly, we release Qwen-Live-Harness, an open-source framework for building responsive, real-time multimodal agents based on Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash achieves strong performance across multimodal understanding, reasoning, long-horizon agentic execution, and video productivity tasks. These results and the accompanying open-source tools support Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash as a practical foundation for deploying natively multimodal agents in research and production.

</details>


### [60] [From Utterances to Networks: Modelling Slang Adoption and Diffusion Across Subreddits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25669)
*Xiaoning Wang,Ted Underwood,Zhewei Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究利用大语言模型作为可扩展的标注器，在Reddit大规模在线交流中检测俚语使用，从而同时从社交网络结构和语言学属性两个视角建模网络新词的采纳与扩散，发现具有更高桥接资本的扩散者促进后续采纳，而更高联结资本则抑制采纳，且更广泛的语境使用会延长新用户正式采纳的时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究往往仅从社交互动或语言属性单一视角解释俚语扩散，缺乏两者结合的整合分析，主要障碍在于大规模在线交流中标注俚语使用的成本高昂。研究者希望借助LLM的标注能力克服这一障碍，从而同时考虑两个维度来理解网络新词的扩散机制。

Method: 首先构建一个人工标注的基准数据集，用于评估LLM在真实Reddit交流中检测俚语使用的性能；随后利用LLM标注结果对俚语的采纳与扩散进行建模，分析社交网络结构（桥接资本与联结资本）和语言语境特征（语境使用宽度）对俚语后续采纳的影响。

Result: 研究发现：桥接资本较高的俚语扩散者与后续更多采纳相关，而联结资本较高的扩散者与更少采纳相关；俚语更广泛的语境使用与新用户正式采纳前更长的等待时间相关。

Conclusion: 社交网络结构和语言语境共同塑造了网络社区中新词的扩散过程，表明整合社会交互与语言属性两个视角对于理解网络俚语传播具有重要意义。

Abstract: Adoption and diffusion of neologisms in online communities have received renewed attention in recent years. As internet slang terms such as APT, referring to a K-pop song, and phrases such as Canon Event meaning an embarrassing but pivotal event, go viral online, it becomes increasingly important to understand the mechanisms that contribute to their success. Prior studies have often explained slang diffusion either from the perspective of social interaction or from the linguistic properties of the slang itself, but rarely from both perspectives together. One major obstacle has been the high cost of annotating slang usage in large-scale online communication. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs), however, make it possible to use them as scalable annotators for such tasks. In this study, we first curate a human-annotated benchmark to evaluate LLM performance in detecting slang usage in real Reddit communication. We then leverage LLM-based annotations to model slang adoption and diffusion. Our results show that slang diffusers with higher bridging capital are associated with increased subsequent adoption, whereas diffusers with higher bonding capital are associated with reduced adoption. We also find that wider contextual usage of a slang term is associated with a longer time before new users officially adopt it. Together, these findings suggest that both social-network structure and linguistic context shape the diffusion of neologisms in online communities.

</details>


### [61] [Syndrome, Synergy, and Safety: Structured Reasoning and Knowledge-Driven Alignment for TCM Prescription Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25755)
*Zheng Chen,ZhiCheng Du,Haoxuan Li,Peiwu Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个渐进式四阶段框架（SFT → PG-CoT → Dynamic → K-RL），针对大语言模型应用于中医方剂生成时的三大临床关键缺陷（SR、LA、SC），在12个微调模型和6个零样本基线上显著提升处方质量，其中7B模型超越零样本GPT-5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在中医方剂生成中存在三个临床关键缺陷：一是无法遵循理法方药的范式生成可审计的推理链（SR缺口）；二是将每次诊疗孤立对待，缺乏随证加减的后续调整（LA缺口）；三是未能强制执行如十八反等绝对禁忌规则（SC缺口）。这些缺口限制了模型在临床实践中的安全性和可用性。

Method: 提出四阶段渐进框架依次解决各缺口：SFT进行基础微调；PG-CoT将思维链蒸馏约束在理法方药范式下，生成可审计的诊断链以解决SR缺口；Dynamic SFT对患者诊疗轨迹建模并带有显式转移推理，解决LA缺口；K-RL将确定性药理规则编码为基于规则的DPO偏好信号，解决SC缺口。

Result: 在12个微调模型和6个零样本基线上进行评测，该框架较零样本基线大幅提升处方质量，其中7B模型（Mistral-7B）在全部三个中医评估指标上超越零样本GPT-5。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了通过针对性地设计渐进式训练阶段，可以有效弥合通用大语言模型与中医临床处方实践的差距，提升推理可审计性、诊疗连续性及禁忌约束的可靠性，为中医药领域LLM应用提供可行方案。

Abstract: Applying large language models to Traditional Chinese Medicine (TCM) prescription generation reveals three clinically critical gaps: models produce end-to-end mappings without auditable reasoning following the li-fa-fang-yao paradigm (SR Gap), treat each encounter in isolation without follow-up adjustment via sui zheng jia jian (LA Gap), and fail to enforce absolute contraindication rules such as Shi Ba Fan (SC Gap). We propose a progressive four-stage framework (SFT $\to$ PG-CoT $\to$ Dynamic $\to$ K-RL) that addresses each gap: PG-CoT constrains CoT distillation under the li-fa-fang-yao paradigm to produce auditable diagnostic chains, Dynamic SFT models patient trajectories with explicit transition reasoning, and K-RL encodes deterministic pharmacological rules as rule-based DPO preference signals. Across 12 fine-tuned models and 6 zero-shot baselines, our framework substantially improves prescription quality over zero-shot baselines---with a 7B model (Mistral-7B) surpassing zero-shot GPT-5 on all three TCM evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [62] [Reply to comments arXiv:2512.07881 and arXiv:2601.06104 on quantum structure in human and AI-generated language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25797)
*Massimiliano Sassoli de Bianchi,Roberto Leporini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是对两篇评论文章的回复，针对作者先前关于人类语言和AI生成语言中量子力学统计的研究（arXiv:2407.14924和arXiv:2511.21731）受到的批评进行逐一回应，涵盖实验协议的探索性质、边际定律违背、Contextuality-by-Default准则在纠缠识别中的作用、玻色-爱因斯坦拟合的局限性、能量层级与词频分配的意义，以及LLM向量空间与量子态空间的关系等问题，并修正了文中的一个排版错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者收到两篇评论文章（arXiv:2512.07881和arXiv:2601.06104）对其先前关于语言中量子力学统计研究的批评，需要对这些批评进行系统性的回应和澄清，同时修正论文中的小错误。

Method: 作者采取逐点回应的方式，针对五个主要批评点分别进行论证和澄清：（1）说明大语言模型实验协议的探索性质及其合理性；（2）讨论边际定律违背和Contextuality-by-Default准则在识别纠缠中的角色；（3）分析玻色-爱因斯坦拟合单独使用时的诊断价值局限；（4）解释将最低能级赋予最频繁词的物理意义；（5）厘清LLM向量空间与量子态空间之间的类比关系。同时修正表格中的排版错误。

Result: 作者对每条批评进行了详细回应，澄清了实验的探索性质，辩明了边际定律违背和Contextuality-by-Default准则的适用性，承认玻色-爱因斯坦拟合单独使用时诊断价值有限，解释了能量层级指派的意义，并厘清了LLM向量空间与量子态空间的关系。同时确认Table 3中的排版错误不影响报告的CHSH值。

Conclusion: 作者感谢评论者的仔细阅读，认为所提出的主要批评点已得到充分回应和澄清，并指出修正的排版错误不影响原论文的主要结论。

Abstract: We reply to the comments by M. Sienicki and K. Sienicki (arXiv:2512.07881) and by K. Sienicki (arXiv:2601.06104) on our work on quantum-mechanical statistics in human language (arXiv:2407.14924) and on quantum structure in AI-generated language (arXiv:2511.21731). We thank the authors for their careful reading and address what we consider to be the main points of criticism: the exploratory nature of the protocol used in the experiments with large language models; the role of marginal-law violations, and of the Contextuality-by-Default criterion, in the identification of entanglement; the limited diagnostic value of a Bose-Einstein fit taken in isolation; the meaning of assigning the lowest energy levels to the most frequent words; and the relation between the vector spaces used by LLMs and quantum state spaces. We also correct a typographical error in Table 3 of arXiv:2511.21731, which does not affect the reported CHSH value.

</details>

### [63] ["As a Language Model...": Chat Template Switches LLM Self-Referential Voice and Activation Steering Reproduces It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25021)
*Jędrzej Maczan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了聊天模板对大型语言模型（LLM）自我描述性回复（如"我只是一个AI"这样的免责声明）的调节作用。研究发现，聊天模板就像开关一样：存在时放大免责声明式语气、抑制体验式语气（如"我感觉"），不存在时则相反。在3个模型的激活空间中发现了控制此行为的特定方向，移除该方向会降低免责声明语气，添加则会增强。研究表明模型关于自身的表述并非纯粹来自权重，而是部分由聊天模板设定，因此不应将其自我描述视为字面事实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在回答与自身相关的问题时倾向于添加"我只是一个AI"之类的免责声明，这些自我报告被用于AI安全或模型自我认知的讨论中，但驱动这些免责声明的机制尚未被充分理解。核心问题是：模型是在讲述关于自身的事实，还是反映其部署方式？

Method: 研究在8个流行的开源指令模型（最大9B参数）上进行实验，对比有无聊天模板时的输出语气差异。进一步在3个模型的激活空间中寻找能够控制该行为的方向，通过移除或添加该方向（并对照随机方向）来验证其对免责声明语气的影响。

Result: 聊天模板作为开关存在：存在时放大免责声明语气并抑制体验式语气，不存在时相反。在3个模型中发现了一个可控制该行为的激活方向——移除该方向降低免责声明语气，添加该方向增强免责声明语气，而相同大小的随机方向几乎没有效果。无聊天模板的指令模型在添加该方向后，其免责声明行为与有模板时一致。

Conclusion: 由于聊天模板控制LLM的免责声明语气，研究自我报告或模型内省的学者存在需要控制的混淆变量。研究提供了一个可用于控制该语气的方向。更广泛地，模型关于自身的表述并非自身的事实——它们不仅来自权重，还部分由聊天模板设定，因此不应将模型的自我描述当作字面事实对待。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) tend to add disclaimers like "I'm just an AI" when asked about something related to themselves. The self-reports from such responses are used in debates about AI safety or self-knowledge of the models, yet what drives them is not well understood. Are the models telling us about themselves or rather how they are deployed? In this work, we show that the chat template works like a switch - when present, it turns this disclaimer voice up and experiential voice like "I feel" down, across 8 popular open-source instruct models up to 9B parameters in size. And conversely when the chat template is not present, it turns the disclaimer voice down and experiential voice up. Inside the activations of 3 models, we find a direction that steers this behavior. Removing the direction in the model's activation space turns disclaimer voice down and adding it turns it up, while a random direction of the same size has little effect. We find that instruct models without chat template, when we add the disclaimer direction to them, disclaim like the template was there. Since the chat template controls the disclaimer voice of LLMs, then researchers studying self-reports or introspection of models might have a confound they need to control for. Our results show that there is a direction they can use to steer this voice. More broadly, our work shows that what models say about themselves is not a fact about them. What they say doesn't come only from weights, but it is partially set by the chat template, and because of that a model's self-description shouldn't be treated literally.

</details>


### [64] [Federating Quantum and Classical Computing: A Privacy-Preserving Hybrid Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25082)
*Carlos Cano,Daniel M. Jimenez-Gutierrez,Diego Sal,Georgios Kellaris,Joaquin del Rio,Oleksii Sliusarenko,Xabi Uribe-Etxebarria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了联邦学习（FL）在混合量子-经典模型中的应用，利用Sherpa.ai盲垂直联邦学习（SBVFL）协议实现隐私保护的多方协作。在提出的SMPP基准任务上，SBVFL将准确率从0.7227提升至0.8757，接近非隐私集中式训练的性能，且混合量子-经典模型以显著更少的可训练参数达到这一效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子-经典模型在隐私敏感的多方场景中部署受限，主要面临两大挑战：一是需要避免原始数据集中化，二是现代量子电路需保持参数高效性以便在大规模下可训练。作者旨在解决这些约束，探索联邦学习作为隐私保护协作框架的可行性。

Method: 采用Sherpa.ai盲垂直联邦学习（SBVFL）协议，构建包含混合量子-经典主动方和经典被动方的联邦学习框架，避免原始数据集中化并大幅降低通信开销。设计了split multiplicative periodic parity (SMPP)基准任务，遵循常见QML设计实践，并通过仿真对比本地训练、集中式训练以及经典神经网络和随机森林基线。

Result: SBVFL将准确率从本地训练的0.7227提升至0.8757，接近非隐私集中式训练的水平；混合量子-经典模型在达到该性能的同时，使用的可训练参数数量显著少于经典神经网络和随机森林替代方案。

Conclusion: 联邦学习使得在不集中化原始数据的前提下实现高性能、隐私保护的量子-经典协作成为可能，为混合量子-经典模型在隐私敏感的多方应用场景中的部署开辟了可行路径。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning (QML) is increasingly recognized as one of the most promising near-term applications of quantum computing, viewed as a next-frontier candidate beyond purely classical approaches. Hybrid quantum-classical models operationalize this potential by embedding a parameterized quantum circuit within a model where all other components remain classical-a design already applied to chemistry simulation, financial modeling, and image classification. However, their deployment in privacy-sensitive, multi-party settings is constrained by the need to avoid centralizing raw data and by the requirement that modern quantum circuits remain parameter-efficient to stay trainable at scale.
  In this paper, we address these constraints by evaluating federated learning (FL) as a means of combining a hybrid quantum-classical active party with a classical passive party, using Sherpa.ai's Blind Vertical FL (SBVFL) protocol to avoid centralizing raw data, while drastically reducing communication. We construct the split multiplicative periodic parity (SMPP) benchmark, following common QML design practice. On this task, our simulations show that SBVFL raises accuracy from 0.7227 to 0.8757 compared to local training, closely approaching non-private centralized accuracy, and that the hybrid quantum-classical model achieves this with substantially fewer trainable parameters than the classical neural networks and random forest alternatives. These results show that FL enables high-performing, privacy-preserving quantum-classical collaboration without centralizing raw data.

</details>


### [65] [Entropy Can Flow, or It Can Guide. Be Entropy. LEDFlow: Introducing Entropy-guided Generation Order into Uniform Discrete Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25131)
*Tung Sum Thomas Kwok,Yidong Ouyang,Yingjia Wan,Ying Nian Wu,Zhijiang Guo,Oscar Leong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对统一离散流（uniform discrete flow）在每一步对所有生成位置进行反复更新所导致的"中间正确、最终错误"问题，提出通过选择性吸收（selective absorption）引入生成顺序，并设计了免训练的LEDFlow采样器，依据局部熵自适应决定吸收顺序，在Sudoku推理、文生图和多模态理解等任务上显著提升性能且推理成本与标准流采样相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一离散流允许在每一生成位置进行重复更新：持续的修订虽能纠正错误token，但也会使此前正确的中间预测在后续更新中被破坏。Sudoku实验表明9.4%的生成单元格在中间步骤正确、最终输出却错误，说明无序的持续更新引入了不必要的误差。因此动机是引入生成顺序、对已被确认的预测进行"固定"（吸收），以阻断错误传播，同时保留活跃位置的均匀流速度。

Method: 提出带选择性吸收的离散流方案：将生成顺序引入均匀离散流，固定的预测不被再更新，而活跃位置的流动速度保持不变。为阻止吸收错误预测，提出LEDFlow——一种免训练采样器，依据局部熵自适应地决定吸收顺序与吸收预算。理论上将吸收误差分解为联合依赖项与条件预测项，证明在固定吸收预算下选择最低熵位置可最小化条件项的上界；同时通过分析全局前瞻（lookahead）的决策误差界，说明在去噪器不完备时该误差界随前瞻窗口增大而增长，从而支持采用局部熵而非全局前瞻。

Result: 在推理基准上，LEDFlow在Nikoli Sudoku上达到0.845的求解准确率，在强约束任务上提升最大；在文本到图像生成上取得最佳综合评分；在多模态理解上，全部六个基准均优于原生采样器；推理成本与标准流采样相当。

Conclusion: 通过按局部熵自适应选择吸收顺序，LEDFlow能以近零额外成本兼顾"修正错误"与"保护正确预测"两方面的需求，在多种生成任务上带来稳定且显著的改进，尤其是强约束的推理任务，为统一离散流模型引入生成顺序提供了有效且理论支撑的方案。

Abstract: Uniform discrete flow permits repeated updates at every generation position. While continued revision supports correction of wrong tokens, it also exposes correct intermediate predictions to later errors. An experiment on Sudoku puzzles shows that 9.4% of generated cells are correct at an intermediate step but incorrect in the final output. We introduce generation order into uniform discrete flow through selective absorption, which fixes chosen predictions while preserving the uniform-flow velocity at active positions. To prevent absorbing incorrect predictions, we propose Low-Entropy Discrete Flow (LEDFlow), a training-free sampler that adaptively orders absorption by local entropy. By decomposing absorption error into joint dependence and conditional prediction terms, we show that selecting the lowest-entropy positions under a fixed absorption budget minimizes an upper bound on the conditional term. We further support the choice of local entropy by showing that the decision-error bound of global lookahead grows with the lookahead window under an imperfect denoiser. Across reasoning benchmarks, LEDFlow attains 0.845 Nikoli Sudoku solve accuracy, with the largest gains on strongly constrained tasks. On text-to-image generation it attains the best overall score, and on multimodal understanding it improves over the native sampler on all six benchmarks, at an inference cost comparable to standard flow sampling.

</details>


### [66] [The Probabilistic Structure of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25134)
*Adnan Aboulalaâ*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从概率论视角统一定义了大语言模型（LLM），将其描述为token序列上的概率测度，通过自回归条件分布指定；训练被形式化为极大似然估计问题，文本生成被视作随机过程的序贯模拟。论文还探讨了KL散度的非对称性在文本生成中的作用，并与幻觉现象及"统计似真性vs真实性"的区分相关联，最后以扩散模型为例展示同一视角的延伸应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将文献中通常分散处理的工具汇集到一个自洽的数学框架中，为理解LLM的生成、训练及特定现象（如幻觉）提供统一的概率论视角。

Method: 采用概率测度框架描述LLM，以自回归条件分布定义token序列的联合分布；训练采用基于随机梯度的极大似然估计；生成过程被建模为随机过程的序贯模拟；通过KL散度的非对称性分析幻觉等生成现象；并以扩散模型（基于score function的逆向随机过程模拟）作为同一视角的互补说明。

Result: 建立了一个统一的自洽概率框架，能够同时涵盖LLM的训练、生成与分析；揭示了KL散度非对称性如何解释文本生成中的幻觉现象，并厘清了统计似真性与真实性的区别；该框架同样适用于扩散模型，展示了其在离散和连续时间下的逆向随机过程模拟机制。

Conclusion: 概率论视角为理解和分析大语言模型提供了统一的数学基础，不仅能解释生成过程中的关键现象（如幻觉），还能将不同类型的生成模型（如自回归LLM与扩散模型）纳入同一理论框架之中。

Abstract: This paper presents a probabilistic perspective on large language models (LLMs), developed with the aim of bringing together, in a single self-contained account, tools that are usually treated separately across the literature. LLMs are described through probability measures on the set of sequences of tokens, specified via their autoregressive conditional distributions. Training is formulated as a maximum-likelihood estimation problem, addressed by stochastic gradient methods, while text generation is viewed as the sequential simulation of the resulting stochastic process. The role of the asymmetry of the Kullback--Leibler divergence in text generation is examined in relation with characteristic phenomena such as hallucination and the distinction between statistical plausibility and truth. As a complementary illustration of the same viewpoint, we also discuss diffusion models, built around the score function, which cast generation not as sequential token prediction but as the simulation of a reverse-time stochastic process transforming noise into data both in discrete and continuous time.

</details>


### [67] [Stable Unsupervised Continual Chunking with Sheaf SyncMap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25143)
*Xueyuan Li,Danilo Vasconcellos Vargas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于sheaf正则化的SyncMap变体（Sheaf SyncMap），通过引入径向sheaf结构来惩罚变量对之间与距离相关的径向运动，从而减少Decentralized SyncMap中的局部不一致并稳定其无监督持续分块（chunking）动力学。实验表明该方法在大多数概率CGCP图上取得了最高的归一化互信息（NMI），并能在输入分布漂移后适应新知识同时避免负迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督持续分块是机器学习和神经科学中的基本问题，目标是从时间序列中识别频繁共现的状态组。核心挑战在于既要形成准确的分块，又要保持分块随时间的稳定性。现有Decentralized SyncMap作为自组织系统在分块动力学上存在局部不一致的问题，需要一种机制来稳定其行为。

Method: 提出sheaf正则化方法以减小Decentralized SyncMap中的局部不一致。具体设计了一种径向sheaf结构（radial sheaf structure），对变量对之间与距离相关的径向运动施加惩罚，以此约束并稳定系统的分块动力学。

Result: 在18个概率型持续一般分块问题（CGCP）图中，Sheaf SyncMap在12个（两状态记忆）和17个（动态记忆）图上取得了评估SyncMap变体中最高的NMI。在顺序适应实验中，该方法在输入分布发生漂移后仍保持高NMI，表明能够适应新知识并避免神经网络等现代机器学习系统中常见的负迁移问题。

Conclusion: Sheaf SyncMap通过sheaf正则化有效提升了持续分块的准确性和稳定性，并在动态环境下展现出良好的适应能力，避免了负迁移，为自组织系统中的持续分块提供了一种稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Unsupervised Continual chunking is a fundamental problem in machine learning and neuroscience, where the goal is to identify groups of states that frequently co-occur in temporal sequences. A key challenge is to form accurate chunks while maintaining their stability over time. In this work, we propose sheaf regularization to reduce local inconsistencies in Decentralized SyncMap, a self-organizing system, and thereby stabilize its chunking dynamics. We introduce a radial sheaf structure that penalizes distance-dependent radial motion between pairs of variables. Experimental results show that the proposed method achieves the highest normalized mutual information (NMI) among the evaluated SyncMap variants on 12 of 18 probabilistic Continual General Chunking Problem (CGCP) graphs with two-state memory and on 17 of 18 graphs with dynamic memory. In the sequential adaptation experiment, Sheaf SyncMap also achieves high NMI after shifts in the input distribution, indicating that it can adapt to new knowledge while avoiding the negative transfer commonly observed in modern machine learning systems such as neural networks.

</details>


### [68] [Brain-Inspired Hierarchical Modularity for General Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25146)
*Hongwei Yan,Kanglei Zhou,Qi Cheng,Weiyi Dong,Chunyan Lan,Guanglong Sun,Jun Zhou,Qian Li,Yi Zhong,Liyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受果蝇学习记忆系统启发的层次化模块化原则，通过专家专业化与集成整合的协调，实现对预训练基础模型的轻量模块化适配，从而在在线、不确定、演化的数据流中进行持续学习，并在视觉识别、视觉-语言理解、自我-外部视频理解及具身视觉-语言-动作学习上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续学习通常在离线、任务式训练且具有清晰任务边界的情况下进行研究，与真实场景中在线、不确定且不断演化的数据流存在较大差距。在这种通用持续学习情境下，系统需要分离冲突经验以减少干扰，同时整合兼容经验以促进泛化，而现有方法难以兼顾这两方面。

Method: 受果蝇学习记忆系统的组织方式启发，识别出通过专家专业化（specialization）和集成整合（integration）协调两种功能的层次化模块化原则。将其实例化为对预训练基础模型的轻量模块化适配，结合受大脑启发的随机扩展进行专家路由，并在空间与时间尺度上进行多样化模块集成。

Result: 在视觉识别、视觉-语言理解、自我-外部视频理解以及具身视觉-语言-动作学习等多个任务上，该方法在在线且不确定的数据流下持续取得改进，其中在具身操作任务中相较无回放的替代方案提升超过50个百分点。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持层次化模块化是面向动态经验学习的一种具有生物学依据的有效路径，为通用持续学习提供了一种可扩展且轻量的解决方案。

Abstract: Continual learning, the ability to learn from sequential experience while retaining and adapting prior knowledge, is central to intelligent systems operating in changing environments. However, conventional continual learning is typically studied with offline task-wise training and clear task boundaries, leaving a substantial gap from general continual learning under online, uncertain, and evolving data streams. In this regime, intelligent systems must separate conflicting experience to reduce interference while integrating compatible experience to promote generalization. Inspired by the organization of the Drosophila learning and memory system, we identify a hierarchical modular principle that coordinates both functions through expert specialization and ensemble integration. We instantiate this principle as lightweight modular adaptation of pretrained foundation models, combining brain-inspired random expansion for expert routing and diversified modular integration across spatial and temporal scales. Across visual recognition, vision-language understanding, ego-exo video understanding, and embodied vision-language-action learning, our method consistently improves learning under online and uncertain data streams, with gains exceeding 50 percentage points over replay-free alternatives in embodied manipulation. These findings support hierarchical modularity as a biologically grounded path for learning from dynamic experience.

</details>


### [69] [Dual-GNN Multilevel Coarsening for Maximum Independent Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25149)
*Tianfeng Chen,Xianyue Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于学习的图边稀疏化方法（GES），用于加速大规模欧几里得旅行商问题（TSP）的精确求解。该方法融合几何结构信息和组合优化技术，为不同实例自适应生成稀疏化图，可剪枝高达95%-99%的边，同时保持最优解差距在1%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确求解大规模TSP计算代价高昂，传统的图稀疏化方法依赖固定启发式，未能充分利用实例特有的结构信息，因此需要一种能够自适应利用实例结构特征的稀疏化方法。

Method: 提出GES（Graph Edge Sparsification），一种基于学习的稀疏化方法。该方法结合几何结构信息与组合优化技术，针对不同的欧几里得TSP实例自适应地生成稀疏化图，从而显著减少图规模并加速求解过程。

Result: 在MATILDA数据集上可剪枝高达95%的边，且解与最优值的差距保持在1%以内；在TSPLIB基准上表现出强泛化能力，部分大规模实例的剪枝率超过99%，最优性差距仍低于1%。

Conclusion: GES方法通过学习驱动的自适应稀疏化，在保持解质量的前提下大幅提升TSP求解效率，并展现出良好的跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Solving large-scale instances of the Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) exactly is computationally expensive. Researchers often employ graph sparsification methods to improve computational efficiency. Traditional sparsification methods typically rely on fixed heuristics and fail to fully exploit instance-specific structural information. In this paper, we propose Graph Edge Sparsification (GES), a learning-based sparsification approach for Euclidean TSP. By incorporating geometric structural information and combinatorial optimization technology, our proposed method adaptively generates a sparsification graph for different instances, significantly reducing the graph size and accelerating the solving process. Experimental results demonstrate that our sparsification method can prune up to 95\% of edges on the MATILDA dataset, while keeping the solution gap within 1\% of the optimal value. Moreover, our approach exhibits strong generalization capability on the TSPLIB benchmark.In some large-scale instances, the pruning rate exceeds 99\%, while the optimality gap remains below 1\%.

</details>


### [70] [Exposing Blind Spots in Deep Imbalanced Regression Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25152)
*Noah C. Puetz,Jens U. Brandt,Marc Hilbert,Elena Raponi,Thomas Bäck,Thomas Bartz-Beielstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地重新审视了深度不平衡回归（DIR）的评估框架，针对三个盲点——图像基准主导、many/medium/few-shot协议不完整、尾部区域随机种子不稳定性——提出了多模态虚拟感知基准MuViS、平衡MAE和新的平衡MASE指标，并通过对六种DIR方法的重复评估揭示了尾部区域对种子变化的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DIR方法在目标分布高度不均匀时在密集区域表现好但尾部性能差。现有评估存在三个盲点：基准以图像为主、标准评估协议诊断但不决策完整、尾部稳定性未系统评估。需要更全面的评估框架来推动DIR研究。

Method: 1) 构建MuViS多模态虚拟感知基准，包含9个时间序列外生回归任务，覆盖6个物理领域；2) 采用平衡MAE（bMAE）并引入平衡平均绝对缩放误差（bMASE）作为尺度归一化指标；3) 对6种代表性DIR方法在多个随机种子下进行重复重新评估，系统分析尾部区域对种子变化的敏感性。

Result: 标准虚拟感知模型在全局MAE下隐藏了显著的尾部退化；现有DIR方法能改善平衡性能但在多模态时间序列数据上迁移不均匀；尾部区域不稳定性是当前DIR评估实践中被广泛隐藏的失败模式。

Conclusion: 本文提供了可复现的DIR研究基础，指出了当前评估实践的盲点，呼吁DIR研究向能像捕获常见目标一样可靠捕获稀有目标区域的回归系统发展。

Abstract: Deep Imbalanced Regression (DIR) addresses a common failure mode of regression models: target distributions are highly non-uniform, causing models to perform best in densely populated target regions even when reliable performance is required across the full target range. Despite rapid methodological progress, DIR evaluation remains constrained by three blind spots: it is dominated by image-based benchmarks, its standard many-/medium-/few-shot protocol is diagnostic but not decision-complete, and tail-region stability across random seeds has not been systematically evaluated. We revisit DIR evaluation along these three axes. First, we broaden the data domain by evaluating DIR on a multimodal virtual sensing benchmark (\textsc{MuViS}) with nine time-series extrinsic regression tasks across six physical domains, where rare target values often correspond to operationally meaningful regimes. Second, we adopt balanced MAE (\emph{bMAE}) and introduce balanced Mean Absolute Scaled Error (\emph{bMASE}), a scale-normalized metric for decision-complete comparison across methods and datasets. Third, through a repeated reevaluation of six representative DIR methods across multiple random seeds, we show that the tail regions targeted by DIR exhibit particularly high sensitivity to seed-level variability. Our results show that standard virtual-sensing models exhibit substantial tail degradation hidden by global MAE, that existing DIR methods can improve balanced performance but transfer unevenly to multimodal time-series data, and that tail-region instability remains a largely hidden failure mode under current DIR evaluation practice. Together, these findings and our publicly available code provide a reproducible basis for future DIR research toward regression systems that capture rare target regimes as reliably as common ones.

</details>


### [71] [Learning Neural Feedback Linearization for Data-driven Systems via Augmented Lagrangian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25163)
*Lakshmi Priya P. K.,Andreas Schwung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全新的数据驱动框架，用于设计和训练反馈线性化控制器。通过将相对阶条件显式嵌入学习过程，用神经李导数替代传统反馈控制组件，从而实现完全数据驱动的反馈线性化。同时推导出充分条件，证明有界辨识误差可导致有界跟踪误差，并在电枢控制直流电机上验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统反馈线性化控制器依赖于精确的系统模型，而实际系统中动力学难以精确建模。作者旨在消除对解析模型的依赖，通过数据驱动方式实现反馈线性化，并确保闭环稳定性。

Method: 将相对阶条件显式融入学习过程以训练反馈线性化控制器；用神经李导数（neural Lie derivatives）替代传统反馈控制组件，构成完全数据驱动的反馈线性化框架；推导出使有界辨识误差对应有界跟踪误差的充分条件，以建立实用的闭环稳定性。

Result: 理论结果通过电枢控制直流电机的应用得到验证，表明所提出的数据驱动反馈线性化框架在保持闭环稳定性的前提下能够有效实现跟踪控制。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将相对阶条件与神经网络结合，实现了无需精确模型的数据驱动反馈线性化，并提供了保证闭环稳定界（有界辨识误差→有界跟踪误差）的理论保证，为复杂系统的数据驱动控制提供了可行途径。

Abstract: The paper proposes a novel data-driven framework for designing and training a feedback linearizing controller by explicitly incorporating relative degree based conditions into the learning process. This enables the conventional feedback controller components to be replaced by neural Lie derivatives, thereby facilitating a fully data-driven feedback linearization framework. Furthermore, practical closed-loop stability is established by deriving sufficient conditions under which bounded identification errors lead to bounded tracking errors. The derived theoretical results are validated through their application to an armature controlled DC motor.

</details>


### [72] [Mitigating Sequential Reappearance in Diffusion Data-Point Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25166)
*Donghyun Kim,Taehyuk Lee,Jinyeong Kim,Youngmin Oh,Dohyeong Kim,Jaehyuk Ryu,Sangwoo Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现扩散模型数据点遗忘中的一个新失败模式——"顺序重现"（sequential reappearance），即被判定为已遗忘的实例在后续删除操作后重新回到记忆状态，并提出了一种目标级别的评估协议来追踪这一现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型数据点遗忘评估通常在每次删除后立即进行，忽略了后续多次删除请求可能对同一模型造成的持续更新影响，导致评估结果无法反映真实的遗忘持久性。

Method: 提出目标级别（target-level）评估协议，追踪每个目标在删除序列中的状态：是否立即被遗忘、是否在序列结束时保持遗忘、以及在后续删除过程中是否重现。并通过局部去噪损失几何（local denoising-loss geometry）分析重现目标的特征。

Result: 识别出"顺序重现"失败模式：实例在初始判定为已遗忘后，在后续删除操作中重新回到记忆状态，且无需复用已删除数据或进行对抗性微调。研究发现，后期重现的目标在删除后表现出更尖锐的局部去噪损失几何，与保持遗忘的目标形成对比。

Conclusion: 顺序重现揭示了现有扩散模型遗忘评估协议的局限性，目标级评估协议及对去噪损失几何的分析为更稳健的遗忘持久性评估提供了新视角。

Abstract: Diffusion data-point unlearning is typically evaluated immediately after each deletion, even though subsequent requests may repeatedly update the same model. We identify sequential reappearance, a failure mode in which an instance that is initially judged to be forgotten later returns to the memorized regime without reuse of the deleted data or adversarial fine-tuning. To capture this behavior, we introduce a target-level evaluation protocol that tracks whether each target is forgotten immediately, remains forgotten at the end of the sequence, or reappears during subsequent deletions. We further find that targets that later reappear exhibit sharper local denoising-loss geometry after deletion than targets that remain forgotten.

</details>


### [73] [Multi-Term Fourier Graph Neural Network with Sample Relationship Learning for Enhanced Remaining Useful Life Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25179)
*Ya Song,Laurens Bliek,Yaoxin Wu,Yingqian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为 MTFGN-SRL（Multi-Term Fourier Graph Neural Network with Sample Relationship Learning，多项傅里叶图神经网络与样本关系学习）的新型框架，用于预测机械系统的剩余使用寿命（RUL）。该框架将每个样本视为一个完整的图而非一系列图，利用傅里叶图神经网络（FGN）在频域中捕获时空信息；通过多项学习模块利用多个回溯窗口产生不同时间尺度的样本；并通过训练异构图神经网络来识别样本间关系，从而提升预测的准确性和鲁棒性。在 CMAPSS 数据集上的评估表明，MTFGN-SRL 在 RUL 预测上的性能优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于时空图神经网络（ST-GNN）的RUL预测方法存在三大缺陷：1）构建图结构需依赖领域专业知识或庞大计算资源；2）模型仅在预定义的固定大小回溯窗口内捕捉时间依赖，无法处理时间序列长度不一的问题，从而遗漏短期或长期依赖；3）传统模型难以捕捉相邻时间窗口生成样本之间的内在关系，而这些关系对提升预测准确性和鲁棒性至关重要。作者旨在克服以上问题，设计一种无需复杂图构建、能自适应捕捉多尺度时间依赖并利用样本间关系的预测框架。

Method: MTFGN-SRL框架包含三个核心模块：1）将样本视为一个完整图，利用傅里叶图神经网络（FGN）在频域中捕获时空信息，避免了预先建立图结构的开销；2）设计多项学习模块，通过多个回溯窗口生成具有不同时间尺度的样本，输入FGN以增强有用信息的提取；3）开发样本关系学习模块，通过训练一个异构图神经网络来识别样本之间的关系，从而提升预测的准确性和鲁棒性。

Result: 在CMAPSS公开数据集上的评估实验表明，MTFGN-SRL在RUL预测任务中取得了优于当前最先进方法的性能表现，验证了该框架在准确性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: MTFGN-SRL通过将样本建模为完整图、在频域中利用FGN提取时空特征、结合多回溯窗口生成多时间尺度样本，以及引入异构GNN学习样本间关系，有效解决了现有ST-GNN在RUL预测中的图构建开销大、固定回溯窗口限制和忽略样本间关系等缺陷，从而实现了更准确、更鲁棒的剩余使用寿命预测。

Abstract: Predicting the remaining useful life (RUL) is essential for effective predictive maintenance. Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Networks (ST-GNNs), which can model both temporal and spatial relationships by representing time series data as a sequence of graphs, have shown exceptional performance in RUL prediction. However, current ST-GNNs face several drawbacks. First, they require domain expertise or significant computational power to establish graph structures prior to deploying GNNs. Second, the models are restricted to capture temporal dependencies within a predefined fixed-size lookback window. This restriction ignores the common issue of varying time series lengths, leading the prediction model to miss short-term or long-term dependencies. Finally, conventional models often fail to capture the inherent relationships between samples generated from adjacent time windows, which are crucial for improving both the accuracy and robustness of predictions. To address the aforementioned issues, we introduce a novel framework called Multi-Term Fourier Graph Neural Network with Sample Relationship Learning (MTFGN-SRL). Rather than treating the sample as a sequence of graphs, we consider it as a single complete graph and utilize a Fourier Graph Neural Network (FGN) to capture the spatio-temporal information in the frequency domain. We propose a multi-term learning module that utilizes multiple lookback windows to generate samples with varying terms, which are then fed into the FGN to enhance the extraction of useful information from the data. Finally, we develop a sample relationship learning module by training a heterogeneous GNN to identify inter-sample relationships, resulting in enhanced accuracy and robustness in predictions. Evaluations on the CMAPSS dataset demonstrate MTFGN-SRL's superior performance over state-of-the-art methods in RUL prediction.

</details>


### [74] [Trains but Doesn't Learn: A Post-Training Delivery Benchmark for LLM Agents as Forward-Deployed Engineers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25237)
*Weihang Ding,Junfei Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将后训练（post-training）作为一种服务（PTaaS）的框架，并系统评估LLM代理能否替代人工前向部署工程师（FDE）完成受治理的模型交付流程。作者构建了一个十阶段治理交付平台，由一个oracle对各阶段进行评分；核心发现的静默失败模式是"训练但不学习"（TBDL）——损失下降但模型质量无提升。实验在L40S/A100/H200 GPU上对四个前沿代理（Claude Opus 5, GPT-5.6-luna, Gemini 3.7 Flash, DeepSeek V4-Pro）及人类FDE进行端到端评测，并提出接受门控和腐败检测器来保障交付质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准只能回答"代理能否提升指标"，无法回答"代理是否值得信任去交付"。随着后训练成为一项服务（PTaaS）——客户交数据和目标，工程师在预算、人工审批门、可复现性要求下返回微调并部署的模型——评估代理在受治理交付流程中的可靠性成为亟需解决的核心问题。

Method: 1) 构建治理交付平面，LLM代理驱动十个阶段，由oracle基于平台记录事实对各阶段评分；2) 识别并形式化"训练但不学习"（TBDL）这一核心静默失败模式；3) 设计运营商验收门控捕捉每次此类失败，设计基于已知腐败运行的校准检测器在运行中途标记严重腐败；4) 在计量的L40S/A100/H200 GPU上，对8B至70B开源基座模型，端到端运行四个前沿代理并认证每个场景；5) 在同一oracle下运行人类FDE对照组进行比较。

Result: 四个前沿LLM代理在受治理交付场景中端到端运行完成；运营商验收门能捕捉所有TBDL运行；腐败检测器能在运行中途标记严重腐败；每个场景在评分前均经过认证；所有代理与在同一oracle下运行的人类FDE进行了对比。

Conclusion: 后训练即服务（PTaaS）场景下，LLM代理能否作为FDE被信任取决于治理机制的设计——验收门控和腐败检测可有效防范"训练但不学习"这类静默失败，但代理与人类FDE的差距仍需具体对比数据来回答信任边界。

Abstract: Post-training is becoming a service (PTaaS): a customer hands an operator data and a goal, and a forward-deployed engineer (FDE) returns a fine-tuned, evaluated, and deployed model under a budget, a human-approval gate, and reproducibility requirements. Seating an LLM agent in the FDE seat raises a question existing benchmarks cannot answer: not whether an agent can raise a metric, but whether it can be trusted to deliver. We answer it on a governed delivery plane, where an agent drives ten stages and an oracle scores each stage from platform-recorded facts. The central silent failure is the run that trains but does not learn (TBDL): loss falls, every signal stays green, and the delivered model is no better than the base. An operator-run acceptance gate catches every such run before payment, and a detector calibrated on known-corrupted runs flags severe corruption mid-run. We ran four frontier agents (Claude Opus 5, GPT-5.6-luna, Gemini 3.7 Flash, DeepSeek V4-Pro) end to end on metered L40S, A100, and H200 GPUs across 8B to 70B open bases, certifying every scenario before scoring. We also ran a human FDE arm under the same oracle and compare every agent against it.

</details>


### [75] [Correcting Within-Group Self-Selection Bias in Prioritized Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25297)
*Oscar Miró López-Feliu,Herke van Hoof*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"兄弟感知重放"（sibling-aware replay）方法，解决优先经验回放（PER）在随机环境中因组内自选择导致的学习偏差问题。通过三种策略（SAMPLE、AVG、MODEL）在保持组级优先权分配的同时，恢复同一状态-动作对内部结果的真实分布，提升随机环境下的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PER通过按绝对TD误差优先回放高优先级转换来提升样本效率，但在随机环境中，同一状态-动作对可能对应多个不同结果（sibling），PER会扭曲这些结果被回放的分布，称为"组内自选择"（within-group self-selection），导致学习目标有偏。

Method: 将PER分解为组间分配（between-group allocation）和条件兄弟选择（conditional sibling selection）两部分。提出三种固定缓冲区修正策略：SAMPLE先用PER选择组，然后均匀采样一个兄弟；AVG求兄弟Bellman目标的平均值；MODEL从经验全结果模型中采样。这些方法保留当前组级优先权质量，同时恢复组内结果频率。

Result: 在精确状态-动作环境中，当存在少见高幅度结果时，兄弟感知重放比PER提升学习效率，尽管参数扫描显示调参可缩小部分差距。在MinAtar环境中，使用近似VQ-VAE分组配合SAMPLE策略，在五个游戏的四个中缓解了均值保持奖励尾部分布下的性能退化。

Conclusion: 兄弟感知重放能够在保持对高优先级状态-动作区域关注的同时，恢复这些区域的经验结果频率分布，从而在随机环境中提升学习效率，是对PER的一种有效改进方向。

Abstract: Prioritized experience replay (PER) improves sample efficiency by replaying high-priority transitions, usually according to absolute temporal-difference error. In stochastic environments, PER can distort the distribution of realized outcomes replayed from transitions with the same state-action pair. We call this within-group self-selection. We quantify the resulting changes in within-group outcome frequencies and mean Bellman targets. We decompose PER into between-group allocation and conditional sibling selection, and derive fixed-buffer corrections that preserve current group-level priority mass: SAMPLE selects a group through PER and trains on a uniformly sampled sibling; AVG averages sibling Bellman targets; and MODEL samples from an empirical full-outcome model. In exact state-action environments with rare high-magnitude outcomes, sibling-aware replay improves learning efficiency over PER, although matched parameter sweeps show that tuning can narrow some gaps. In MinAtar, approximate VQ-VAE groups with SAMPLE mitigate degradation under mean-preserving reward tails in four of five games. Sibling-aware replay thus retains the focus on high-priority state-action regions while recovering their empirical outcome frequencies.

</details>


### [76] [Topological Signal Processing With Unoriented Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25310)
*Andrea Cavallo,Varun Sarathchandran,Geert Leus,Elvin Isufi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无向拓扑信号处理（UTSP）框架，用无向关联矩阵替代有向边界算子来处理无方向的高阶信号，并引入交互阶分解来量化高阶信号中低阶聚合的贡献，据此设计阶感知正则化器用于信号重建，实验表明其在真实数据上优于有向基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的拓扑信号处理依赖有向边界算子，这适用于流向信号或需要拓扑不变量的任务。然而许多高阶信号本身没有方向性，强行施加方向会引入任意选择，导致算子定义不明确。因此需要一种无需方向假设的拓扑信号处理框架。

Method: 1）用无向关联矩阵（unoriented incidence matrices）替代有向边界算子，证明任意单纯复形层级之间的无向关联矩阵和Laplacian矩阵具有类似图的谱性质；2）由于去掉方向后Hodge分解不再成立，提出交互阶分解（interaction-order decomposition）作为其替代，量化高阶信号由低阶信号聚合解释的程度；3）基于该分解设计正则化器，对信号重建任务中每个交互阶分别施加惩罚。

Result: 在真实世界数据上的实验表明，所提出的交互阶感知正则化器优于有向基线方法，尤其在信号能量在各交互阶上分布不均匀时，性能提升最为显著。

Conclusion: 本文表明无向拓扑信号处理是处理无方向高阶信号的有效框架，交互阶分解为无向场景提供了类似Hodge分解的分析工具，且阶感知正则化在实际任务中具有实用优势。

Abstract: Topological signal processing (TSP) processes signals on simplicial complexes with oriented boundary operators, which is the natural choice for flow signals or when the topological invariants play a role for the task at hand. However, many higher-order signals carry no orientation, and applying oriented operators to them is not well-defined since it introduces an arbitrary choice of simplex orientation. We study an unoriented TSP (UTSP) framework that replaces oriented boundaries with unoriented incidence matrices. First, we show that unoriented incidence and Laplacian matrices between arbitrary simplicial levels admit graph-like spectral properties. Second, since dropping orientation removes the Hodge decomposition, we introduce an unoriented counterpart, termed interaction-order decomposition, which quantifies how much of a higher-order signal is explained by aggregating lower-order signals. Third, we use this decomposition to derive regularizers for signal reconstruction that penalize each interaction order separately. Experiments on real-world data show that the order-aware regularizers outperform oriented baselines, with the largest gains when the signal energy is unevenly distributed across orders.

</details>


### [77] [Spatiotemporal Kronecker Covariance Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25326)
*Andrea Cavallo,Athanasios Georgoutsos,Elvin Isufi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Kronecker coVariance Neural Network (KVNN)，一种时间图神经网络，将时空协方差矩阵表示为 Kronecker 乘积之和，从而解耦空间和时间依赖。KVNN 具备非线性表达能力、严格的谱分析，并对有限样本估计误差具有可证明的稳定性，解决了 ST-PCA 的所有局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统协方差统计工具（如 ST-PCA）仅能进行线性操作且在数据有限时易产生估计误差；现有协方差时空神经网络虽然提供非线性替代方案，但忽略了不同时间步之间的相关性。因此需要一种既能捕捉时空复杂模式、又能处理有限样本估计误差的方法。

Method: KVNN 将时空协方差矩阵表示为 Kronecker 乘积之和（sum of Kronecker products），其中空间和时间依赖性被解耦。滤波操作分别在空间和时间分量上执行，从而获得表达能力强、具有严格谱分析及对有限样本估计误差可证明稳定的网络结构。

Result: 在五个真实世界数据集上，KVNN 取得了强预测性能，相比竞争方法通常需要显著更少的可训练参数，并且在估计噪声下保持一致。

Conclusion: KVNN 通过解耦空间和时间依赖的 Kronecker 结构，同时解决了 ST-PCA 的线性限制和现有协方差神经网络忽略时间步间相关性的问题，实现了表达力、理论可分析性和统计稳定性的统一。

Abstract: Multivariate time series contain complex patterns that span across both space and time. While covariance-based statistical tools like spatiotemporal Principal Component Analysis (ST-PCA) help identify these patterns, they are limited to linear operations and prone to estimation errors with limited data. Recent covariance-based spatiotemporal neural networks offer more stable, non-linear alternatives, but they ignore correlations across different time steps. To solve this, we introduce the Kronecker coVariance Neural Network (KVNN), a temporal graph neural network that represents the spatiotemporal covariance matrix via a sum of Kronecker products where spatial and temporal dependencies are decoupled. By implementing filtering operations on spatial and temporal components, KVNNs achieve expressive processing capabilities, admit a rigorous spectral analysis, and are provably stable to finite-sample estimation errors, ultimately addressing all of ST-PCA's limitations. We show on five real-world datasets that KVNNs achieve strong forecasting performance, often requiring significantly fewer trainable parameters than competitive methods, and are consistent under estimation noise.

</details>


### [78] [MT-ProtBERT: Multi-task Learning ProtBERT for Intrinsically Disordered Proteins Classification with Scarce Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25334)
*Jian Sun,Kingshuk Ghosh,Lilianna Houston,Mohammad H. Mahoor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MT-ProtBERT，一种多任务扩展的ProtBERT模型，专门针对内在无序蛋白（IDPs）在低数据场景下的分类/检测任务。该模型整合了动态窗口掩码、多尺度一维卷积分类器和辅助目标（联合优化掩码语言建模与生物化学启发的任务），在磷酸化位点预测和蛋白质压实预测两个任务上均优于基于RNN的PARROT模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内在无序蛋白（IDPs）具有构象异质性、缺乏稳定三维结构且序列相似性低等特点，传统实验工具难以测定其构象，加之序列相似性低导致数据稀缺，使得IDPs的分类和检测面临巨大挑战。现有方法难以在低数据环境下有效处理这类蛋白质。

Method: 提出了MT-ProtBERT框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) Dynamic Window Masking（动态窗口掩码）用于预训练；(2) Multi-Scale 1D Convolutional classifier（MS-Conv1D）多尺度一维卷积分类器；(3) 辅助目标函数，联合优化掩码语言建模与生物化学启发的任务（如磷酸化位点预测）。该框架通过自监督学习和生物化学知识结合来应对低数据场景。

Result: 在两项低数据任务（磷酸化位点预测S/T/Y和蛋白质压实预测，后者基于684和530个序列的两个小数据集）上，MT-ProtBERT在所有任务中一致优于基于RNN的IDP特异性模型PARROT。

Conclusion: 将自监督学习与生物化学启发任务相结合，并采用多尺度学习策略，能够在数据稀缺条件下实现对无序蛋白质的稳健建模，为IDPs的分类和检测提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Intrinsically disordered proteins (IDPs) differ from folded proteins in that they are dynamic, lack a stable three-dimensional conformation, and have low sequence similarity between similar proteins. The conformational heterogeneity of IDPs - while beneficial for their diverse functions - limits the use of traditional experimental tools to determine their conformation. The experimental difficulty, along with low sequence similarity, results in data scarcity, and makes it difficult to classify/detect IDPs that are similar or dissimilar, a task relevant to understand biology and evolution. We address this challenge using Multi-task ProtBERT (MT-ProtBERT), a multi-task extension of ProtBERT tailored for low-data regimes. MT-ProtBERT integrates Dynamic Window Masking, a Multi-Scale 1D Convolutional classifier (MS-Conv1D), and auxiliary objectives that jointly optimize masked language modeling and biochemistry-informed tasks. We evaluate this framework on two tasks under limited data: (i) phosphorylation site prediction (S/T/Y) in short sequences and small datasets, and (ii) protein compaction prediction on two small datasets (684 and 530 sequences), including sequences comparable in length to typical disordered regions. MT-ProtBERT consistently outperforms PARROT, an RNN-based IDP-specific model, across all tasks. These results demonstrate that combining self-supervised and biochemistry-informed tasks, and multi-scale learning enables robust modeling of unstructured proteins under data scarcity.

</details>


### [79] [Concept Drift from a Causal Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25340)
*Eduardo V. L. Barboza,Jean Paul Barddal,Robert Sabourin,Rafael M. O. Cruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于结构因果模型（SCM）的概念漂移因果视角。作者构建了按因果来源分类漂移事件的分类体系（外生变量变化、内生机制变化、混杂因素变化、目标生成过程变化），并开发了基于SCM的数据流生成器，用于模拟受控的机制级漂移事件。实验表明不同因果来源的漂移会产生不同的分布偏移模式和预测行为，并借助因果发现方法构建基于真实世界依赖结构的数据流，证明了该方法能提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界数据流中概念漂移普遍存在，会导致预测模型性能下降。然而现有定义仅将漂移刻画为联合分布 P(x,y) 的变化，无法区分数据生成过程中具体是哪个组件发生了改变。因此需要一种能够揭示漂移因果起源的框架，从而更深入地理解和评估自适应学习方法。

Method: 1) 提出基于SCM的漂移分类体系，按因果来源将漂移事件分为外生变量变化、内生机制变化、混杂因素变化和目标生成过程变化四类；2) 开发基于SCM的数据流生成器，实现受控的机制级漂移事件模拟；3) 融合因果发现方法，基于真实世界的依赖结构构造数据流，构建更真实、信息量更大的评估场景。

Result: 实验表明，不同因果来源的漂移会诱发截然不同的分布偏移模式和预测行为特征；基于真实依赖结构构造的数据流提供了更具现实意义的评估场景；利用生成的数据能够改善下游任务性能。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在研究和评估自适应学习方法时考虑因果结构的重要性，为非平稳环境下的因果感知评估奠定了方法论基础。

Abstract: Concept drift is a common phenomenon in real-world data streams, in which changes in the data-generating distribution can degrade predictive model performance. Most existing definitions characterize drift as changes in the joint distribution $P(\mathbf{x}, y)$, without distinguishing which component of the data-generating process has changed. In this work, we introduce a causal perspective on concept drift based on Structural Causal Models (SCMs). We propose a taxonomy that categorizes drift events by their causal origin, including changes in exogenous variables, endogenous mechanisms, confounders, and target-generating processes. Building on this framework, we develop an SCM-based data stream generator that simulates controlled mechanism-level drift events. Our experiments empirically characterize the distributional effects of each drift type and show that drifts with different causal origins induce distinct patterns of distribution shift and predictive behavior. Furthermore, by integrating causal discovery methods, we use our framework to construct data streams grounded in real-world dependency structures, enabling more realistic and informative evaluation scenarios. We also demonstrate that leveraging the generated data can improve downstream performance. These results highlight the importance of accounting for causal structure when studying and evaluating adaptive learning methods, and establish a foundation for causally-aware evaluation in non-stationary environments.

</details>


### [80] [Extending FunctionGemma for Practical On-Device Mobile Function Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25373)
*Ali Rezagholizadeh,Soheila Samiee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED合成数据集（约9,500个对话，覆盖15类设备控制任务），对FunctionGemma 270M-it模型进行监督微调，将端到端准确率从29.3%提升至76.5%。同时训练了一个与Google MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE联合训练的合并模型，在扩展数据集上保持76.5%准确率，在Google数据集上达到82.3%，以8.0个百分点准确率换取双倍类别覆盖。研究展示了紧凑型本地函数调用模型在低延迟和隐私保护移动助手方面的可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有函数调用资源主要聚焦于Web API或狭窄的移动操作目录，缺乏覆盖实际Android工作流的本地设备控制数据集，阻碍了端上助手在低延迟和隐私保护场景下的函数调用能力发展。

Method: 构建MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED——一个合成、模式验证的约9,500个对话数据集，覆盖十五类设备控制类别（包括消息、电话、相机/截图、亮度控制、设备状态查询、手电筒控制和应用管理等）。使用TRL监督微调，采用仅完成损失（completion-only loss），对FunctionGemma 270M-it进行微调，生成扩展专家模型以及与Google's MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE联合训练的合并模型。

Result: 在MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED上，端到端准确率从基础模型的29.3%和Google Mobile-Actions变体的17.2%提升至76.5%。合并模型在MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED上保持76.5%准确率，在MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE上达到82.3%，相比Google Mobile-Actions专家的90.3%下降了8.0个百分点，但类别覆盖翻倍。

Conclusion: 研究表明紧凑的270M参数本地函数调用模型能够有效覆盖广泛的Android设备控制任务，在准确率与功能覆盖之间取得合理权衡，为低延迟、隐私保护的移动端助手提供了实际可行的技术路径。作者发布了数据集、微调模型、可复现的训练/评测流程及Android演示。

Abstract: On-device assistants require function-calling models that map natural language to local system actions, but existing resources emphasize web APIs or narrow mobile-action catalogs. We extend FunctionGemma 270M-it to practical Android workflows by introducing MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED, a synthetic, schema-validated dataset of ~9,500 conversations covering fifteen device-control categories, including messaging, phone calls, camera/screenshot, brightness control, device-status queries, flashlight control, and application management. We fine-tune the 270M model with TRL supervised fine-tuning under completion-only loss, producing an extended specialist and a combined model trained jointly with Google's MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE. On MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED, end-to-end accuracy improves from 29.3% for the base model and 17.2% for Google's Mobile-Actions variant to 76.5%. The combined model retains 76.5% on MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED and reaches 82.3% on MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE, down from the 90.3% of Google's Mobile-Actions specialist, representing an 8.0-percentage-point trade-off in return for doubling category coverage. We release the dataset, fine-tuned models, reproducible training/evaluation pipeline, and an Android demo, highlighting compact local function calling as a practical path towards low-latency and privacy-preserving mobile assistants.

</details>


### [81] [Deep Reinforcement Learning on Item-Compatibility Graphs for One-Dimensional Bin Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25397)
*M. Aslı Aydın*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种端到端、尺寸无关的图强化学习框架用于一维装箱问题（1D-BPP）。该方法将装箱过程建模为在物品兼容图上的马尔可夫决策过程，采用图神经网络actor-critic策略，配合随机束搜索解码，可实现单模型零样本泛化到任意规模实例。在BPPLIB基准的零样本评估中，该方法将构造式启发式的平均最优性差距从2.66%降至2.31%，在九个实例族的大多数上优于现有学习方法且更稳定，并在最难的基准族上超越依赖列生成和整数规划的SOTA学习方法，但整体仍落后于分组遗传算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维装箱问题（1D-BPP）是经典的NP难组合优化问题，应用广泛（物流、制造、云资源管理）。尽管深度强化学习已成为数据驱动优化的主流范式，但大多数学习方法针对2D和3D变体，智能化的1D-BPP学习求解器仍然稀缺，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 将装箱过程建模为在物品兼容图上的马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），其中每个动作合并两个能够适配的部分箱子。采用图神经网络actor-critic策略提取关系特征，通过强化学习训练，并用随机束搜索进行解码，使单一训练模型能零样本泛化到任意规模实例。研究涵盖图编码器、RL算法、奖励函数、训练分布和超参数的系统性对比。

Result: 在完整BPPLIB基准上的零样本评估中，该数据驱动策略将构造式启发式的平均最优性差距从2.66%降低到2.31%，在结构化实例上收益最大。相较于同基准上的学习方法，它在九个实例族中的大多数上获得更低差距，且跨实例分布更稳定。在最难的基准族上，它超越了依赖列生成和整数规划的SOTA学习方法，且完全不使用求解器。

Conclusion: 分组遗传算法整体仍保持领先，作者分析了残余差距出现的位置及原因。该方法证明了图强化学习框架可作为1D-BPP的有效数据驱动求解范式，具备良好的泛化性与稳定性，但距离超越传统元启发式仍有差距。

Abstract: The one-dimensional bin packing problem (1D-BPP) is a classical NP-hard combinatorial optimization problem with applications ranging from logistics and manufacturing to cloud resource management. Although deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has become a competitive paradigm for data-driven optimization, most learned packing methods target 2D and 3D variants, and intelligent learned solvers for 1D-BPP remain scarce. In this paper, we present a novel end-to-end, size-agnostic graph reinforcement learning framework for 1D-BPP. We formulate the packing process as a Markov decision process on an item-compatibility graph, serving as a structural knowledge representation in which every action merges two partial bins that fit together. A graph neural network actor-critic policy extracts relational features from this representation and is trained through reinforcement learning and decoded by stochastic beam search, enabling a single trained model to generalize zero-shot to instances of any size. We conduct a systematic empirical study across graph encoders, DRL algorithms, reward functions, training distributions, and hyperparameters. Evaluated zero-shot on the full BPPLIB benchmark against a constructive heuristic, a grouping genetic algorithm, and recent learned methods, our data-driven policy lowers the mean optimality gap of the constructive heuristic from 2.66\% to 2.31\%, with the largest gains on structured instances. Against learned baselines evaluated on the same benchmark, it attains a lower gap on most of the nine families and is far more stable across instance distributions. On the hardest benchmark family, it outperforms a state-of-the-art learned solver that relies on column generation and integer programming, while using no solver at all. A grouping genetic algorithm remains ahead overall, and we analyze where and why the residual gap arises.

</details>


### [82] [Predictive Uncertainty for Neural CAE Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25430)
*Kaustubh Tangsali,Mohammad Amin Nabian,Kelvin Lee,Carmelo Gonzales,Sanjay Choudhry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了适用于几何条件神经代理模型的不确定性量化（UQ）方法，比较了三种方法（GP闭式法、MC Dropout和深度集成）在三个大规模工业CAE数据集（外部空气动力学和碰撞动力学）上的表现，发现预测不确定性普遍能指示误差较大的位置，但不同方法的相对优劣随数据集和评估标准而变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经代理模型可大幅加速计算机辅助工程（CAE）流程，但其在设计场景中的应用需要跨几何形状、空间预测场和工程感兴趣量保持有意义的不确定性估计。已有UQ方法在标准深度学习场景中表现各异，尚不清楚它们如何适应几何条件神经代理这一特殊设定。

Method: 比较三种UQ方法：1）基于高斯过程（GP）的闭式方法；2）具体蒙特卡洛（MC）Dropout（采样方法）；3）深度集成（采样方法）。在DrivAerStar（外部空气动力学）、AirFRANS和汽车碰撞（碰撞动力学）三个大规模工业相关CAE数据集上进行评估。考察指标包括：不确定性的可信度、识别大预测误差位置的能力、对陌生输入的响应、以及派生工程量的信息量。

Result: 在DrivAerStar数据集上三种方法均能为误差较大的位置分配更高的不确定性，验证集重标定能使覆盖概率接近名义水平。AirFRANS和汽车碰撞数据上也显示出有用的误差排序和区间估计，但方法的相对性能随数据集和评估标准改变。

Conclusion: UQ方法和评估指标的选择应取决于下游CAE决策任务的具体需求，不存在普遍最优的UQ方法。

Abstract: Neural surrogates can substantially accelerate computer-aided engineering (CAE) workflows, but their use in design requires uncertainty estimates that remain meaningful across varying geometries, spatial prediction fields, and engineering quantities of interest. We investigate how established uncertainty quantification (UQ) approaches behave when adapted to geometry-conditioned neural surrogates.
