We compare one closed-form and two sampling-based approaches-a Gaussian process (GP)-based method, concrete Monte Carlo (MC) dropout, and deep ensembles-and evaluate them on three large, industry-relevant CAE datasets for external aerodynamics and crash dynamics.
  We examine whether predicted uncertainties have credible magnitudes, identify locations with larger prediction errors, respond to unfamiliar inputs, and remain informative for derived engineering quantities. On the DrivAerStar dataset, where all three methods are compared, each generally assigns higher uncertainty to locations with larger prediction errors, and validation-based rescaling brings interval coverage close to nominal on a disjoint in-distribution test set. Results on AirFRANS and automotive crash also show useful error ranking and interval estimates, but the relative performance of the methods changes with the dataset and evaluation criterion. UQ methods and evaluation metrics should therefore be selected based on the intended downstream CAE decision.

</details>


### [83] [Lightweight Ranking Heads: Accelerating Multi-Task Experimentation in Production Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25433)
*Sanjay Surendranath Girija,Aniruddh Nath,Li Wei,Yanhao Jiang,Shawn Andrews,Lukasz Heldt,Yi Wu,Aditya Mahajan,Mohit Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了轻量级排序头（Light Heads）框架，用于在持续在线学习环境中向现有大规模多任务排序模型动态注入新任务，无需重新训练骨干模型，从而将多任务实验迭代周期从数周缩短至数天，已在YouTube规模成功部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级推荐系统依赖复杂多任务排序模型，引入新预测任务会造成瓶颈：新任务可能与现有任务产生负面冲突，且因骨干模型与下游模型的昂贵重训练或奖励组合公式调优，导致开发和实验周期漫长。核心挑战是实验迭代速度过慢。

Method: Light Heads框架专为持续在线学习环境设计，通过停止梯度（stop-gradients）与无状态日训练（stateless daily training）实现新任务的严格隔离，规避任务冲突与模型冷启动和骨干重训练；采用集中化配置，支持将Light Heads同时加入多个模型，加速训练数据生成与下游模型协同训练。

Result: 该框架已在YouTube规模成功部署，将多任务实验的迭代周期从数周缩短至数天，并实现了新的排序任务的快速A/B实验与部署，产生了可测量的生产价值。

Conclusion: 论文详述系统架构、分析无状态冷启动头的训练动态、将其与完整排序头比较，并展示Light Heads如何促成快速实验与部署。其核心价值在于通过任务隔离架构提高多任务推荐的实验迭代速度。

Abstract: Modern production-scale recommender systems rely on complex, multi-task ranking models. Introducing new prediction tasks into these massive systems often causes bottlenecks - it risks negative task conflicts with existing tasks, and can lead to long development and experimentation cycles due to the expensive retraining of backbone models and downstream models or tuning of reward combination formulas. To address the critical challenge of slow experimentation velocity, we introduce the Lightweight Ranking Heads (Light Heads) framework. Designed for continuous online learning environments, Light Heads enable the dynamic injection of new tasks into existing multi-task ranking models, effectively obviating the need for model cold-starting and retraining of backbone models. By utilizing stop-gradients and stateless daily training, this design strictly isolates new tasks, mitigating the risk of adverse task conflicts. Crucially, this framework uses a centralized configuration that allows Light Heads to be added to multiple models simultaneously, unblocking faster training data generation and co-training of downstream models. Successfully deployed at YouTube scale, this approach reduces the iteration cycle for multi-task experimentation from several weeks to days. In this paper, we detail the system architecture, analyze the training dynamics of stateless cold-started heads, compare their performance to full heads, and demonstrate how Light Heads have enabled the rapid A/B experimentation and deployment of new ranking tasks that yield measurable production value.

</details>


### [84] [PermuFormer: Multi-Task Pretraining for Permutation Representation in Algebraic Combinatorics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25438)
*Henry Kvinge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PermuFormer，一个在28亿token的多任务、多编码语料上预训练的自回归Transformer，用于代数组合学中的排列相关任务。研究表明该模型作为微调起点，在下游任务上优于从头训练的相同架构、基线MLP和同等规模的语言模型，并分析了模型内部学习机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专门化数学模型通常从零开始训练，限制了其发展捕捉数学多面性领域感知表示的能力。本文探索通过多样化预训练为排列相关任务学习可复用、领域感知的表示，以解决专用模型从零训练的性能瓶颈。

Method: 引入PermuFormer，一个基于自回归Transformer的模型，在28亿token的多任务、多编码语料上进行预训练。采用多任务和多编码策略覆盖排列对象的不同数学特征，然后对下游任务进行微调，并分析内部表示的可线性解码性。

Result: PermuFormer在预训练未见的简单任务和复杂研究级任务上均表现优异，经常优于从零训练的相同架构、基线MLP以及同等大小的微调通用语言模型。机制分析显示部分任务可从提示的内部表示直接线性解码，而其他任务需要多轮生成后才能解码答案。

Conclusion: 多样化预训练对专门数学模型同样有效，PermuFormer证明了域感知预训练表示能作为排列任务的有效起点，并揭示了模型通过不同内部机制解决不同类型任务的特性。

Abstract: Diverse pretraining has been shown to be an effective method for learning reusable, domain-aware representations that provide a starting point for fine-tuning on downstream tasks. While much of the excitement in AI for math has been concentrated in the use of frontier reasoning models to solve well-specified problems through the medium of language, narrow, specialized models remain an important component of the AI for math ecosystem. In contrast to large language models, specialized models are usually trained directly on the mathematical objects themselves (e.g., graphs, sequences of numbers) rather than the textual descriptions that characterize these objects. However, the common practice of training specialists from scratch may limit their ability to develop domain-aware representations that capture the multifaceted nature of mathematics. In this paper, we describe an approach to pretraining for permutation-focused tasks in algebraic combinatorics. We introduce PermuFormer, an autoregressive transformer trained on a 2.8 billion token multi-task, multi-encoding corpus. We show that PermuFormer is an effective starting point for fine-tuning on basic tasks unseen during pretraining and more complex research-level tasks, frequently outperforming the same architecture trained from scratch, baseline MLPs, and a fine-tuned generic language model of comparable size. We also analyze some of the internal mechanisms by which PermuFormer learns to solve training tasks. For example, we show that while some tasks can be linearly decoded directly from the internal representation of the prompt, other tasks require multiple rounds of generation before the answer can be decoded.

</details>


### [85] [Mean Velocity Matching: Rethinking Generative Dynamics in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25444)
*Yunhong Zhang,Changjie Cao,Zhihua Zhang,Bingli Liu,Zongjie Cao,Zongyong Cui,Ying Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出均值速度匹配（MVM），通过构造高斯扰动过程，使条件期望的恢复导向速度(x0-xt)/t直接构成反向SDE的漂移项，从而仅需学习单一速度场即可参数化随机反向扩散过程，无需额外估计或重建分数。引入√t缩放参数化解决t→0时回归目标无界的问题，同时该场也诱导确定性概率流ODE，统一了随机与确定性采样。在ImageNet 32×32和256×256上取得具有竞争力的FID结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的基于速度的生成模型虽然简化了单一传输场的学习，但其标准公式是确定性的；随机扩展通常需要额外的分数信息或中间的速度到分数重建步驟。本文旨在保持单场预测的同时直接支持随机反向动力学，消除对额外分数估计或重建的需求。

Method: MVM构建一个高斯扰动过程，其中条件期望E[(x0-xt)/t|xt]直接构成反向SDE的漂移项。由于直接回归该速度在t≈0时无界，引入√t缩放参数化（即学习√t·(x0-xt)/t），在保持反向动力学不变的同时产生有界训练目标。学习到的单一场既参数化随机反向SDE，也诱导出确定性的概率流ODE，实现统一的公式化。

Result: 基于Transformer生成模型，MVM在ImageNet 32×32上达到特定FID（给定NFE），在ImageNet 256×256上达到特定FID（给定NFE）。受控SDE-ODE比较表明：在极低NFE下ODE表现更好，而在充足函数评估次数下随机反向过程达到更低的FID。

Conclusion: MVM提供了一种直接的单场参数化方式来描述随机反向动力学，同时保持竞争性的生成质量，统一了随机与确定性采样的研究框架。

Abstract: This work studies prediction parameterization for stochastic generative dynamics in diffusion models. Existing velocity-based generative models provide the simplicity of learning a single transport field, but their standard formulation is deterministic, whereas stochastic extensions generally require additional score information or an intermediate velocity-to-score reconstruction. To retain single-field prediction while directly supporting stochastic reverse dynamics, this paper introduces Mean Velocity Matching (MVM). MVM constructs a Gaussian perturbation process for which the conditional expectation of a restoration-oriented velocity, $(x_0-x_t)/t$, directly forms the reverse-SDE drift. Consequently, a single learned field is sufficient to parameterize the stochastic reverse process without separately estimating or reconstructing the score. Because direct regression of this velocity becomes unbounded near $t=0$, MVM further introduces a $\sqrt{t}$-scaled parameterization that preserves the reverse dynamics while yielding a bounded training target. The same learned field also induces a deterministic probability-flow ODE, enabling stochastic and deterministic sampling to be studied within a unified formulation. Experiments with Transformer-based generative models achieve an FID of $\MVMImageNetThirtyTwoFID$ at \MVMImageNetThirtyTwoNFE\ NFE on ImageNet $32\times32$ and $\MVMImageNetTwoFiftySixFID$ at \MVMImageNetTwoFiftySixNFE\ NFE on ImageNet $256\times256$. Controlled SDE--ODE comparisons further show that the ODE performs better under very low NFE, whereas the stochastic reverse process achieves lower FID when sufficient function evaluations are available. These results demonstrate that MVM provides a direct single-field parameterization of stochastic reverse dynamics while maintaining competitive generation quality.

</details>


### [86] [A Practical Recipe for Semi-Supervised Federated ASR: Online Pseudo-Labels with Server Update Stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25471)
*Wonho Bae,Zakaria Aldeneh,Martin Pelikan,Jan "Honza" Silovsky,Tatiana Likhomanenko,Sheikh Shams Azam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究半监督联邦学习（SSFL）在自动语音识别（ASR）中的应用，指出伪标签错误会在输出序列与训练轮次间累积导致发散，与全监督FL差距大。论文提出闭合这一差距取决于两个耦合的设计轴：教师（生成伪标签的模型）与锚（服务端基于标注数据的更新）。研究发现逐客户端在线教师一旦稳定可匹敌或超越广播全局教师，而过渡型教师在两者间最优；服务端需在轮次间持续训练标注数据以稳定在线教师，且教师选择与锚稳定化密不可分。最终在11对任务中9对优于最强基线，域内平均提升20.8%、跨域提升10.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSFL在ASR领域尤为脆弱，因为伪标签错误会在输出序列和训练轮次间复合累积，导致模型发散，与全监督FL之间存在显著性能差距。作者旨在理解并闭合这一差距，探究其背后起决定作用的设计因素。

Method: 论文系统剖析两个耦合的设计轴：(1) 教师轴——对比逐客户端在线教师（各客户端自身演化模型）与广播全局教师（服务端单模型，轮内固定），并提出过渡型教师（在特定轮次r从全局切换到在线）；(2) 锚轴——服务端在轮次间持续以标注数据训练以稳定训练。通过系统实验考察数据增强、批大小、种子数据分散度及其与客户端数据重叠度等因素对训练稳定性的影响。

Result: 逐客户端在线教师虽自身会发散，但一旦稳定可匹敌乃至超越广播全局教师（域内决定性优势，域偏移下具有竞争力）；过渡型教师在种子更强、在线教师优势收窄时匹敌或超越两者。服务端轮间持续标注训练（交错训练）比种子模型本身更主导收敛。教师激进选择仅在锚稳定化后才奏效，锚的稳定对数据增强与批大小高度敏感。所需稳定化程度取决于域分布，由种子数据分散度及与客户端数据重叠度决定。最终在11对评价中9对超越最强先验方法，域内平均提升20.8%，跨域提升10.0%，缩小了与全监督FL的差距。

Conclusion: SSFL在ASR中的性能与全监督FL的差距由教师与锚两个耦合设计轴共同决定，二者不可分割；据此提出设计准则，显著提升SSFL在ASR上的性能并大幅缩小与全监督FL的差距。

Abstract: Semi-supervised federated learning (SSFL) trains models on clients' unlabeled data using a teacher to generate pseudo-labels, with a small labeled seed dataset on the server. Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) is particularly fragile here: pseudo-label errors compound across the output sequence and across training rounds into divergence, leaving a large gap to fully-supervised FL. We show that closing this gap turns on two coupled design axes -- the teacher (which model generates the pseudo-labels) and the anchor (the server-side updates on labeled data that stabilize training). On the teacher axis, a per-client online teacher (each client's own evolving model) diverges on its own, but once stabilized it matches or beats the broadcast global teacher (one server model, fixed within a round) -- decisively in-domain and competitively under domain shift. As the seed grows stronger and the online teacher's advantage narrows, a transitioning teacher (global $\rightarrow$ online at round $r$) matches or beats both. On the anchor axis, the server must keep training on labeled data between rounds -- otherwise the online teacher drifts -- and this interleaving, more than the seed model, governs convergence. The two axes are inseparable: aggressive teacher choices pay off only once the anchor stabilizes training, which is highly sensitive to data augmentation and batch size -- the settings that govern how much input and gradient noise the server injects. How much stabilization is needed is domain-dependent, governed by the dispersion of the seed data and its overlap with client data. These findings yield guidelines for SSFL in ASR training, improving over the strongest prior method on 9 of 11 pairs, by $20.8\%$ on average in-domain and $10.0\%$ cross-domain, narrowing the gap to fully-supervised FL.

</details>


### [87] [Terminal Shrinkage Averaging Reveals a Schedule-Estimator Interaction in LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25482)
*Adam Ousherovitch,Yixin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出终端收缩平均（Terminal Shrinkage Averaging, TSA），将大语言模型预训练中的学习率调度与参数估计器解耦，在原始最终迭代与近期检查点平均之间插值，以在训练后期兼顾优化进展与终端方差，并通过受控实验和深度22的NanoChat实验验证其能提升验证质量并加速基准测试收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM预训练直接返回原始最终迭代，将两个设计选择耦合在一起：生成参数轨迹的学习率调度，以及用于构造部署模型的估计器（如原始最终迭代或检查点平均）。能够促进优化进展的调度可能与能最小化原始最终迭代方差的调度不同。分离这两个选择创造了在训练后期保持进展同时降低返回模型方差的机会。

Method: 提出TSA方法，在原始最终迭代与近期检查点的平均值之间进行插值，以平衡近期进展与终端方差。在局部二次近似下分析TSA如何改变优选的学习率终端调度，并通过一系列受控NanoChat实验检验二者的交互作用。最后在深度22的NanoChat上验证结合了调度与估计器的方案能提升验证质量。

Result: 受控NanoChat实验验证了TSA与终端学习率调度的交互；在深度22的NanoChat上，组合的调度与估计器改善了验证质量；一次符合资格的time-to-GPT-2运行也快于实验中使用的公开基线，为基准加速提供了初步证据。

Conclusion: 将学习率调度与参数估计器解耦是有价值的，TSA作为估计器能够在不牺牲训练后期优化进展的前提下减少终端参数方差，并且该收益可以迁移到更大深度的模型，具有加速基准训练的潜力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) pretraining conventionally returns the raw final iterate. This couples two design choices: the learning-rate schedule that generates the parameter trajectory and the estimator that constructs the deployed model (e.g. the raw final iterate or a checkpoint average). A schedule that promotes optimization progress may differ from one that minimizes variation in the raw final iterate. Separating these choices creates an opportunity to maintain progress late in training while reducing variation in the returned model. To this end, we propose \emph{Terminal Shrinkage Averaging (TSA)}, which interpolates between the raw final iterate and the average of recent checkpoints to balance recent progress against terminal variation. We analyze how TSA changes the preferred terminal learning-rate schedule under a local quadratic approximation and test this interaction through a sequence of controlled NanoChat experiments. Finally, we demonstrate that the resulting gains transfer to depth-22 NanoChat, where the combined schedule and estimator improve validation quality. A qualifying time-to-GPT-2 run also finishes faster than the public baseline used in our experiments, providing preliminary evidence of benchmark acceleration.

</details>


### [88] [Learning Defensive Policies against Diverse Inference Attacks for Smart Meter Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25484)
*Ruichang Zhang,Mustafa A. Mustafa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于代理引导的分层强化学习框架，通过电池负载整形策略向智能电表聚合信号注入真实但具有误导性的家电级签名，以抵御未知和异构的NILM推断攻击，在UK-DALE和REDD数据集上实现跨模型、跨家电的鲁棒隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能电表数据细粒度地暴露家庭能耗信息，易受NILM推断攻击恢复家电级行为模式，侵犯用户隐私。实际场景中攻击者模型未知且异构，现有防御方法缺乏对不同攻击者的泛化能力，因此需要在黑盒场景下构建鲁棒的隐私保护机制。

Method: 提出代理引导的分层强化学习框架：1) 学习基于电池的负载整形策略，注入真实但误导性的家电级签名以扰乱NILM可利用的结构化模式；2) 自监督聚合结构隐私探针提供基于重构误差的替代奖励，用于评估可恢复负载结构的破坏程度；3) 签名库确保扰动具有家电相关性和物理可实现性（通过电池控制实现）；4) 提供理论论证表明代理引导优化在攻击者多样性下提升推断鲁棒性。

Result: 在真实数据集UK-DALE和REDD上的实验覆盖6种未见过的NILM攻击者，分别涵盖4种和5种家电。防御方法使平均家电级RMSE分别提升107%和166%，F1分数分别降低79%和80%，展示出强跨模型和跨家电泛化能力。

Conclusion: 代理引导的分层强化学习框架能有效解决黑盒场景下的智能电表隐私保护问题，通过生成物理可实现的误导性负载签名，在应对多样化和不可见的NILM攻击者时表现出显著且鲁棒的防御效果。

Abstract: Smart meter (SM) data provides fine-grained visibility into household energy consumption, but also exposes users to privacy risks. Inference attacks, known as non-intrusive load monitoring (NILM), can perform appliance-level inference from aggregate signals and recover sensitive behavioral patterns. In practice, attacker models are unknown and heterogeneous, making robust defense challenging. We formulate SM privacy protection as a black-box inference defense problem, aiming to reduce the recoverability of appliance-level information while generalizing across diverse and unseen attackers. We propose a proxy-guided hierarchical reinforcement learning framework that learns battery-based load-shaping policies to inject realistic but misleading appliance-level signatures into the aggregate signal, thereby disrupting the structured patterns exploited by NILM. A self-supervised aggregate-structure privacy probe provides a reconstruction-error-based surrogate reward for disrupting recoverable load structure, while a signature library makes the perturbations appliance-relevant and physically realizable through battery control. We provide theoretical rationale showing that proxy-guided optimization improves inference robustness under attacker diversity. Experiments on real-world datasets UK-DALE and REDD demonstrate strong cross-model and cross-appliance generalization. Across six unseen NILM attackers, covering four appliances on UK-DALE and five on REDD, our proposed defense increases average appliance-level RMSE by 107% and 166%, respectively, while reducing F1 score by 79% and 80%.

</details>


### [89] [Continuous Optimization for p-adic Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25501)
*Julian Salazar,Dimitri Kanevsky,Matt Harvey,Pascal Getreuer,Lucas Dixon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出针对p-adic参数机器学习模型的原生连续梯度下降方法，通过在p-adic数的Berkovich仿射线上进行优化，实现连续优化与反向传播，并展示了在模运算、线性回归及层级分类等任务上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: p-adic数在机器学习中具有潜在价值，但由于其完全不连通性（totally disconnected），标准损失函数在其最小值附近是平坦的，现有原生优化器仅为离散的、多为组合搜索的方法，无法进行连续的梯度下降优化。

Method: 提出通过Berkovich仿射线来扩展Q_p：这是一种规范的、路径连通的Q_p扩张，保持其等距性并唯一扩展其解析映射。该凸包（hull）是一个具有可解释点和局部导数的度量树，从而支持有效的优化器和反向传播。基于此设计了梯度下降算法，并进一步提出了动量（momentum）和Adam变体。

Result: 近似梯度下降能高效学习Q_p中系数的线性模型以完成模运算（一种在R中线性模型无法表达的XOR类任务），并在线性回归和二进制编码层级（Quillian语义网络）分类任务上展示了有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了Martins (2025)提出的开放问题，为p-adic参数的机器学习模型提供了首个原生连续优化框架，并开源了代码库。

Abstract: We present the first method for native, continuous gradient descent for machine learning models with $p$-adic parameters. Existing native optimizers are discrete, mostly combinatorial searches, as the $p$-adic numbers $\mathbb{Q}_p$ are totally disconnected, with standard losses that are flat away from their minima. To enable continuous optimization, we propose working with $\mathbb{Q}_p$ via its Berkovich affine line: a canonical, path-connected expansion of $\mathbb{Q}_p$ that preserves its isometries and uniquely extends its analytic maps. This hull is a metric tree with interpretable points and local derivatives, which we show enables effective optimizers and backpropagation. We formulate gradient descent and show that its approximations efficiently learn linear models with coefficients in $\mathbb{Q}_p$ to do modular arithmetic, an XOR-like task not expressible by linear models in $\mathbb{R}$. We also demonstrate momentum and Adam variants, linear regression, and classification on binary-encoded hierarchies (Quillian semantic networks), addressing open problems posed by Martins (2025). Library at https://github.com/google-deepmind/padic-ml

</details>


### [90] [Hill Sampling for Test-Time Scaling: A Simple and Better Alternative to Repeated Sampling, Evolution, and Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25510)
*Jacob Beck,Philip V. Ogren,Ari Kobren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Hill Sampling，一种极简的测试时计算策略：从冻结的LLM中反复采样候选程序编辑，保留当前最优解并基于该解进行后续采样。该方法在圆填充、集合和/差、Erdős最小重叠问题等可验证科学问题上取得了优于更复杂进化搜索方法（如AlphaEvolve）的结果，并发现直接对LLM权重进行进化策略（ES）训练反而不如将学习率设为零的效果好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM提升可验证问题求解能力主要依赖复杂的进化搜索框架或测试时训练（更新模型参数）。作者质疑这些复杂机制是否必要，希望通过最简单的策略来探索测试时计算分配的下限，以理解哪些机制真正贡献了性能提升。

Method: Hill Sampling：在每次迭代中，冻结LLM基于当前最优程序采样一个编辑候选，评估其适应度（可验证的目标函数），若更好则替换当前最优解，否则保留原解。这是一个类似爬山法（hill climbing）的随机搜索过程。此外，作者还进行了大规模进化策略（ES）直接作用于LLM权重的实验，并对比了零学习率与正学习率的搜索效果。

Result: Hill Sampling在圆填充问题上创下新的最优结果（SOTA），在Erdős最小重叠问题上的表现优于AlphaEvolve参考结果，在有限集合和/差问题上也取得了强结果，且仅需8块NVIDIA H100 GPU运行数小时。大规模ES实验发现，零学习率（即仅保留固定随机扰动）的效果优于正学习率，但该方法的探索能力仍弱于Hill Sampling。

Conclusion: 最简单的方法——反复采样对当前最优解的编辑——在可验证科学问题上就已足够强大。测试时计算的合理分配策略应优先采用"贪心编辑+最优解保留"的方式，然后再考虑构建档案（archives）、多样性机制、进化脚手架或测试时参数学习等额外复杂性。此外，权重空间中随机扰动虽有助于探索，但其效果弱于token采样带来的随机性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can improve solutions to verifiable scientific and algorithmic problems by spending additional computation at test time. Recent systems achieve strong results with increasingly elaborate evolutionary search harnesses or by updating model parameters during test-time training. We ask how much of this machinery is necessary. We introduce Hill Sampling, a simple procedure that repeatedly samples candidate program edits from a frozen LLM, retains the best program found so far, and conditions all subsequent samples on that program. We evaluate the method on circle packing, sums/differences of sets, and Erdos' minimum-overlap problem using three open-weight models. Hill Sampling sets a new state of the art on circle packing among published methods, improves over the AlphaEvolve reference on Erdos' minimum-overlap problem, and achieves strong results on sums and differences of finite sets. The circle-packing and Erdos results require only hours of wall-clock time on eight NVIDIA H100 GPUs. To our knowledge, we also conduct, the largest study, by parameter count, of evolution strategies (ES) applied directly to LLM weights at test time. Surprisingly, learning the weights is worse than setting the ES learning rate to zero: at zero learning rate, the method is still searching in weight space through fixed random perturbations. Those perturbations can help exploration, but randomness from token sampling is stronger still, and repeated sampling remains substantially weaker than Hill Sampling. These results suggest a simple test-time compute allocation strategy: repeatedly sample edits to the best verified solution found so far, before introducing additional complexity such as adding archives, diversity mechanisms, evolutionary scaffolds, or test-time parameter learning.

</details>


### [91] [A JEPA Recipe for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25541)
*Mingyu Jeon,Suwan Cho,Jae Young Suh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究表格基础模型中的世界模型自监督（JEPA）训练问题。在之前的实验中，JEPA的潜在项发生坍塌（collapse），编码器退化为常数映射。作者提出一种配方使潜在项在收敛时与值目标共存：值头直接读取编码器场而非预测器，且采用指数移动平均（EMA）差异作为目标。为公平对比，两臂均按平台期规则停止训练而非固定步数（固定视野曾将减速混淆为天花板）。收敛时（每臂一次运行），JEPA臂在147个真实数据集上落后于仅值目标臂：分类32:70胜败、回归8:24，分类差距小、回归差距大。JEPA臂达到平台期需1.42倍步数和1.66倍墙钟时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型在上下文中预测单元格值，而世界模型自监督要求在表示空间预测（LeCun 2022; Assran等2023）。此前在表格基础模型先验上，JEPA的潜在项发生坍塌并拖垮编码器至常数映射，作者意图寻找让潜在项在值目标之外存活至收敛的训练配方，并公平量化其相对仅值目标臂的代价。

Method: 提出防坍塌配方：值头读取编码器场而非预测器，目标采用EMA差异。为约束成本，两臂均训练至平台期规则停止，而非固定步数预算（固定视野会混淆减速与天花板，因仅值目标臂在常规预算之后仍在改进）。每臂各运行一次直至收敛。

Result: 收敛时，JEPA臂在147个真实数据集上落后于仅值目标臂：分类32:70胜败（按数据集名去重后29:63），回归8:24；分类差距小、回归差距大，各分层与各基准中计数方向一致。JEPA臂达到平台期需1.42倍步数、1.66倍墙钟时间。

Conclusion: 在表格基础模型先验下，所提出的配方能让JEPA潜在项存活至收敛，但相对于仅值目标基线，JEPA臂在准确性和收敛成本上均处于劣势（尤其回归任务），且该劣势在各分层各基准中方向一致。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models learn to predict cell values in context, whereas world-model self-supervision asks for prediction in representation space (LeCun, 2022; Assran et al., 2023). On a tabular foundation-model prior, the latent term of a joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) collapsed in our earlier runs and took the encoder with it to a constant map. We report a recipe under which the latent term survives to convergence beside the value objective: the value head reads the encoder field rather than the predictor, and the target is an exponential moving average (EMA) difference. To bound its cost against the value-only arm, both arms train until a plateau rule stops them, with no fixed step budget. A fixed horizon had confounded a slowdown with a ceiling, since the value-only arm was still improving well past the usual budget. At convergence, in one run per arm, the JEPA arm trails the value-only arm across 147 real datasets, 32:70 wins to losses on classification (29:63 with one entry per dataset name) and 8:24 on regression, the margin small on classification and wider on regression, and the count leans the same way in each stratum and each benchmark. The JEPA arm (jepa) needs 1.42 times as many steps as the value-only arm (ds), and 1.66 times its wall-clock, to reach its plateau.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [92] [Do Synthetic Personas Predict Real Audience Response? A Sim-to-Real Study Where a No-Persona Baseline Beats Persona-Based Copy Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25010)
*Alexandre Cristovão Maiorano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过Upworthy Research Archive中数千个头条A/B测试（以真实点击率为地面真值）进行sim-to-real效度研究，比较了基于真实受众人口统计构建的十人设面板与无人物设定的零样本基线。核心结论是：人设条件化会降低预测效度——无人物基线（Kendall τ=0.361，top-1准确率49.2%）显著优于人设面板（τ=0.084，top-1 34.6%）。并且该结果在三个独立分割、其他领域数据集、不同种子/提示措辞/模型（Gemini三级与OpenAI gpt-4.1）上均稳健复现。地面真值可靠性是约束瓶颈（仅n=399可靠）。结论：预测聚合参与度时，朴素LLM排序器优于人设模拟——合成人物不仅预测力弱，反而比不使用更差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 营销人员越来越多地使用LLM作为"合成人物"（synthetic personas）来预测受众对文案的反应，受证据表明条件化人物LLM能模仿人类样本。然而，尚无研究验证这种预测对真实行为是否有效，以及人物机制是否真正有帮助。本文旨在对人物模拟的核心假设进行实证检验。

Method: 采用sim-to-real效度研究设计，以Upworthy Research Archive（数千个共享真实流量的头条A/B测试及实测点击率）作为留出地面真值。对比两种方法：①十人设面板，锚定于真实受众的人口统计数据；②无人物零样本基线，直接询问模型典型读者点击的可能性。仅在统计上可区分优胜者的可靠子集（n=399）上测量效度。通过三个独立Upworthy分割、跨域新闻数据集、多种种子/提示/模型（Gemini三档及OpenAI gpt-4.1）进行稳健性检验。

Result: 两个关键发现：第一，地面真值可靠性是约束瓶颈——多数A/B测试无统计可区分的获胜者，效度只能测量于可靠子集。第二，与人物模拟前提相反，人物条件化降低了预测效度：无人物基线排序显著更优（Kendall τ=0.361，中等效应；top-1准确率49.2%），远超人物面板（τ=0.084；top-1 34.6%），置信区间不重叠。结果在三独立分割上复现，跨域数据集方向一致，并对种子、提示措辞、模型选择（Gemini三级及gpt-4.1显著配对差距）稳健。

Conclusion: 直接询问模型可触及准确的人群级先验；强迫其扮演特定人物会注入偏差与噪声。因此，对于预测聚合参与度，朴素LLM排序器优于人物模拟——合成人物不仅是弱预测器，而是比不使用更糟糕。所有数值可由公开的、以工件为先的复制包重新生成。

Abstract: Marketers increasingly use large language models (LLMs) as "synthetic personas" to predict how an audience will react to a piece of copy before it ships, encouraged by evidence that profile-conditioned LLMs mimic human samples. But is that prediction actually valid against real behaviour - and does the persona machinery help? We present a sim-to-real validity study using the Upworthy Research Archive - thousands of headline A/B tests on shared real traffic, with measured click-through - as held-out ground truth. We compare a ten-persona panel, grounded in the real audience's demographics, against a no-persona zero-shot baseline that simply asks the model how likely a typical reader is to click. Two findings stand out. First, ground-truth reliability is the binding constraint: most A/B tests have no statistically distinguishable winner, so validity can only be measured on the reliable subset (n = 399). Second, and counter to the persona-simulation premise, persona conditioning degrades predictive validity: the no-persona baseline ranks variants markedly better (Kendall τ = 0.361, a medium effect; top-1 accuracy 49.2%) than the persona panel (τ = 0.084; top-1 34.6%), with non-overlapping confidence intervals. Asking the model directly taps an accurate population-level prior; forcing it to role-play specific personas injects bias and noise. The result replicates across three independent Upworthy splits, holds in direction on a different-domain news dataset, and is robust to seed, prompt phrasing, and model choice - across three Gemini tiers and a different model family (OpenAI gpt-4.1, significant paired gap). The takeaway: for predicting aggregate engagement, a plain LLM ranker beats persona simulation - synthetic personas are not merely a weak predictor, they are worse than not using them. All numbers regenerate from a public, artifact-first replication package.

</details>


### [93] [Do Existing Preconditioners Improve Biomedical Tabular Foundation Learning? An Empirical Study on TabPFN Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25013)
*M. Sajid,Pinki Khatun,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对TabPFN v2.5在59个生物医学数据集上的微调进行了系统性实证研究，比较了五种基于AdamW的预条件策略，发现原始AdamW优化器在预测性能和统计排名上始终表现最佳，而曲率感知的预条件器未能带来可靠的提升，提出了开发面向生物医学的专用预条件器的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管TabPFN在低数据表格分类任务中展现出强大潜力，但优化和预条件策略对生物医学微调的影响尚未被充分探索。现有曲率感知预条件器在通用场景可能有优势，但其在多样化的生物医学学习场景中是否有效仍不明确，因此需要系统性的实证研究来填补这一空白。

Method: 在59个生物医学数据集（涵盖阿尔茨海默病、乳腺癌、精神分裂症、显著性记忆关注、KEEL生物医学数据集和UCI生物医学基准）上，对TabPFN v2.5的微调过程采用五种基于AdamW的预条件策略进行对比实验，从预测性能、计算效率和统计显著性三个维度进行全面评估。

Result: 原始AdamW优化器在整体性能和统计排名上一致优于其他预条件策略；现有的曲率感知预条件器在多样化的生物医学学习场景中未能提供可靠的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通用预条件方法可能无法充分捕捉生物医学表格学习中的优化特征，未来应开发专门针对医疗保健场景设计的生物医学感知预条件器，以更好地适配生物医学表格基础模型的微调需求。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models have recently shown strong potential for structured biomedical data analysis. Among them, TabPFN has emerged as an effective approach for low-data tabular classification tasks. However, the impact of optimization and preconditioning strategies on biomedical fine-tuning remains largely unexplored. In this work, we present a comprehensive empirical investigation of five AdamW-based preconditioning strategies for fine-tuning TabPFN v2.5 on 59 biomedical datasets spanning Alzheimer's disease, breast cancer, schizophrenia, significant memory concern (SMC), KEEL biomedical datasets, and UCI biomedical benchmarks. The evaluation considers predictive performance, computational efficiency, and statistical significance analysis. Experimental results demonstrate that the original AdamW optimizer consistently achieves the best overall performance and statistical ranking, while existing curvature-aware preconditioners fail to provide reliable improvements across diverse biomedical learning scenarios. The findings suggest that generic preconditioning approaches may not adequately capture the optimization characteristics of biomedical tabular learning, motivating the development of biomedical-aware preconditioners specifically tailored for healthcare-oriented tabular foundation models.

</details>


### [94] [4DGS-JEPA: Temporally Compositional Joint-Embedding Prediction for Dynamic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25036)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出4DGS-JEPA，一种基于高斯原生的联合嵌入预测架构，用于动态高斯场景的因果多时域预测。该模型采用场景-运动组-高斯三级分层表示，配合时域条件转移算子，支持直接预测和递归展开。核心原则是时间组合性：到达同一未来端点的不同时间演化路径应产生兼容的预测状态。通过端点和多时域路径监督锚定预测，选择性几何解码器和几何级组合将学习到的动力学扎根于一致的组运动和高斯几何中，无需重建完整未来外观。还引入混合对应机制，结合持久规范身份与残余最优传输匹配，以应对重排序和拓扑变化。理论表征了零损失路径一致性和有限误差递推累积，并通过三个受控实验验证了时间组合性、几何级组合和混合对应的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态高斯泼溅方法主要针对重建、未来状态生成或渲染进行优化，而非学习可复用的预测性动力学。缺乏一种既能保持高斯显式表示优势又能进行因果多时域预测的统一架构，因此需要设计一种高斯原生的预测性动力学学习框架。

Method: 4DGS-JEPA采用：1）层次化表示（场景级、运动组级、高斯级）；2）时域条件转移算子，支持直接预测和递归 rollout；3）时间组合性原则，强制不同时间演化路径在同一端点产生兼容状态；4）端点与多时域路径监督，锚定未来目标嵌入；5）选择性几何解码器与几何级组合，在无需完整外观重建的情况下确保运动一致性；6）混合对应机制，融合持久规范身份与残余最优传输匹配，应对重排序和拓扑变化；7）理论上分析零损失路径一致性与有限误差递推累积。

Result: 三个受控实验提供了机制层面的证据：时间组合性降低了潜在路径依赖性同时保持预测精度，几何级组合提高了解码运动的连贯性，混合对应在对应关系模糊时仍能保持可靠身份的同时保持鲁棒性。理论分析刻画了零损失路径一致性和有限误差递推累积的性质。

Conclusion: 4DGS-JEPA提出了一种预测性、时间组合性的动态高斯世界建模公式，弥合了高斯泼溅在高质量重建/渲染与可复用预测动力学学习之间的鸿沟，为动态3D场景的未来状态预测提供了系统性方案。

Abstract: Dynamic Gaussian Splatting provides an explicit representation of evolving 3D scenes, but existing approaches are primarily optimized for reconstruction, future-state generation, or rendering rather than for learning reusable predictive dynamics. We propose 4DGS-JEPA, a Gaussian-native joint-embedding predictive architecture for causal multi-horizon prediction over dynamic Gaussian scenes. The model uses a hierarchical scene-, motion-group-, and Gaussian-level representation together with a horizon-conditioned transition operator that supports both direct prediction and recursive rollout. Its central principle is temporal composition: different chronological transition paths reaching the same future endpoint should produce compatible predictive states. Endpoint and multi-horizon path supervision anchor these predictions to future target embeddings, while a selective geometry decoder and geometry-level composition ground the learned dynamics in consistent group motion and Gaussian geometry without requiring complete future appearance reconstruction. We further introduce a hybrid correspondence mechanism that combines persistent canonical identity with residual optimal-transport matching under reordering and topology change. We characterize zero-loss path agreement and finite-error rollout accumulation theoretically. Three controlled experiments provide mechanism-level evidence that temporal composition reduces latent path dependence while retaining predictive accuracy, geometry-level composition improves consistency of decoded motion, and hybrid correspondence preserves reliable identity while remaining robust when correspondence becomes ambiguous. Together, 4DGS-JEPA provides a predictive, temporally compositional formulation of dynamic Gaussian worlds.

</details>


### [95] [An Accurate and Interpretable Hyper Graph Neural Network for GBM Survival Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25088)
*Mushahid Intesum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个针对胶质母细胞瘤（GBM）生存预测的多模态框架，通过结合她束超图神经网络（高阶关系建模）、概念瓶颈层（前置可解释性）和扩展充分性检验（EST）正则化项（训练期解释保真约束），同时实现高准确率与高可解释性。在UPenn-GBM数据集593例患者、5折交叉验证下取得0.643的一致性指数（C-index），且折间方差为所有对比模型最低（std=0.015）。这是首个统一她束超图卷积、概念瓶颈监督与EST正则化用于脑MRI可解释生存预测的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GBM生存预测模型将准确性与可解释性视为相互竞争的权衡目标：高性能模型牺牲透明度，可解释模型则降低预测能力。作者认为这种权衡并非固有属性，且当前方法多依赖单一影像模态，未能充分利用多模态MRI与临床元数据的互补信息。因此需要一种能同时优化两个目标并整合多模态信息的框架。

Method: 提出一个由三部分构成的多模态框架：(1) 她束超图神经网络，通过有向、非对称消息传递捕获组织斑块间的高阶关系；(2) 概念瓶颈层，将学习到的表征压缩为临床可理解的概念，实现前置（ante-hoc）可解释性；(3) 扩展充分性检验（EST）正则化项，在训练期间惩罚不忠实的解释，确保模型解释真实反映内部决策过程。临床与基因组特征通过门控融合引入，既保留分子标记物的主导预后信号，又保持概念级可追溯性。

Result: 在UPenn-GBM数据集593例患者上采用5折交叉验证评估，所提框架取得0.643的一致性指数（C-index），并且折间标准差为0.015，是所有对比模型中最小的折级方差，表明其在预测精度与稳定性两方面均具优势。

Conclusion: 该工作证明准确性与可解释性在GBM生存预测中并非不可兼得，通过她束超图卷积、概念瓶颈监督与EST正则化的统一设计，可在保持竞争力的判别性能的同时提供符合临床语义的可解释表征。就作者所知，这是首个将这三类机制整合用于脑MRI可解释生存预测的研究。

Abstract: Survival prediction for glioblastoma multiforme (GBM) demands models that are both accurate and interpretable, yet existing approaches treat these objectives as com- peting, where performant models sacrifice transparency, while interpretable models accept degraded predictive power. We argue that this trade-off is not inherent. Graph neural net- works offer a structural foundation for extracting interpretable, explainable representations without compromising discriminative ability. Furthermore, current methods typically rely on a single imaging modality, underutilizing the complementary information available across multi-modal MRI and clinical metadata. We propose a multi-modal framework that inte- grates three components to address both objectives simultaneously: (1) a sheaf hypergraph neural network that captures higher-order relationships among tissue patches through direc- tional, asymmetric message passing; (2) a concept bottleneck layer that compresses learned representations into clinically grounded concepts, enforcing ante-hoc interpretability; and (3) an extension sufficiency test (EST) regularizer that penalizes unfaithful explanations during training, ensuring that model explanations genuinely reflect the internal decision process. Clinical and genomic features are incorporated through gated fusion, preserving the dominant prognostic signal of molecular markers while retaining concept-level traceabil- ity. Evaluated on 593 patients from the UPenn-GBM dataset under 5-fold cross-validation, our framework achieves a concordance index of 0.643 with the lowest fold-level variance among all compared models (std = 0.015). To our knowledge, this is the first work to unify sheaf hypergraph convolution, concept bottleneck supervision, and EST regularization for interpretable survival prediction from brain MRI

</details>


### [96] [Ovis-Embedding: Pushing the Frontiers of Universal Omni-Modal Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25165)
*Ovis-Embedding Team*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Ovis-Embedding，一个基于共享多模态骨干网络的全模态嵌入模型家族，通过原生全模态初始化、数据驱动的全模态训练和嵌入专属的优化策略，在MMEB-v3、MMEB-v2、MVEB、MAEB和RTEB等基准上实现最先进性能，支持文本、图像、视频和音频的统一嵌入表示与任意到任意检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入模型通常将不同模态通过独立的塔式架构分开处理，导致模态碎片化，无法在统一的表示空间中对齐文本、图像、视频和音频，限制了跨模态检索和通用嵌入模型的发展。

Method: (1) 原生全模态初始化：采用预训练的Qwen-omni模型作为嵌入骨干，并通过低秩初始化的对比训练进行适配；(2) 数据驱动的全模态训练：构建涵盖文本、图像、视频、音频及交错多模态数据的高质量语料库，引入同源采样以形成任务一致的批次和具有信息量的批内负样本；(3) 嵌入专属的训练与推理优化：使用焦点损失强调困难样本，利用基于相似度的嵌入蒸馏从互补专家迁移细粒度相似度结构，推理时通过低秩特征分解实现紧凑嵌入和灵活维度。

Result: Ovis-Embedding在MMEB-v3、MMEB-v2、MVEB、MAEB和RTEB五个基准上均达到最先进的性能，证明了其在文本、图像、视频和音频各模态上的有效性。

Conclusion: 统一的omni-modal训练能够克服模态碎片化问题，为构建通用嵌入模型以实现任意到任意检索提供了可行路径。

Abstract: In this report, we introduce \textbf{Ovis-Embedding}, a state-of-the-art omni-modal embedding family built on native integration of text, image, video, and audio. Instead of assembling separate modality towers, Ovis-Embedding uses a shared multimodal backbone to encode different modalities in a common representation space. Specifically, we make \textbf{three key advances}: (1) \textbf{native omni-modal initialization}: we adopt a pretrained Qwen-omni model as the embedding backbone and adapt it through contrastive training with low-rank initialization; (2) \textbf{data-centric omni-modal training}: we construct a broad, high-quality corpus spanning text, images, video, audio, and interleaved multimodal data. To improve data efficiency, we introduce homogeneous-source sampling to form task-consistent batches with informative in-batch negatives; and (3) \textbf{embedding-specific training and inference optimization}: we use focal loss to emphasize hard examples and similarity-based Embedding Distillation to transfer fine-grained similarity structure from complementary experts. At inference time, low-rank feature decomposition enables compact embeddings with flexible dimensionality and minimal performance loss. Empirical evaluations show that the \textbf{Ovis-Embedding} family achieves state-of-the-art performance on \textbf{MMEB-v3}, \textbf{MMEB-v2}, \textbf{MVEB}, \textbf{MAEB}, and \textbf{RTEB}, demonstrating its effectiveness across text, image, video, and audio modalities. These results highlight the potential of unified omni-modal training to overcome modality fragmentation and advance universal embedding models for any-to-any retrieval.

</details>


### [97] [X-Planner: Event-Structured Task Planning for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25187)
*Howard Lu,Shalfun Li,Porter Pan,Cris,Lumen,Cyril,Eric Hu,Lily Li,Maeve Zhang,Robert Wang,KZ Zheng,Viggo Chen,Tim Ding,Regsis Cheng,YJ Xiao,Kian,Hai Lin,Alan Song,Elise Ma,Gody Li,Victor Yao,Yohann Tang,Ingrid Yu,Jason He,James Wang,Ryan Yu,Ping Yang,Chris Pan,Vincent Chen,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: X-Planner提出一种面向长程操作任务的规划前端，通过层级标注数据和双接口（离散事件态与潜在连续链式思维态）结构，结合阶梯式解码和潜在到文本重建损失，提升VLA系统的中间规划表示质量，并在离线评估和真实机器人实验中取得较好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代VLA系统常将高层指令与可执行行为之间的规划结构隐式化，现有CoT规划器要么依赖粗粒度任务注释，要么逐token序列化冗长推理，缺乏对监督粒度和推理表示两方面的系统性处理。

Method: X-Planner作为规划前端，构建结合Ego、UMI和遥操作数据的分层粒度规划数据，按数据源设置不同注释深度，并在接管时刻注释和人为失败样例中监督错误识别。模型侧使用共享VLM骨干，输出两种事件化规划形式：离散接口发出可解释事件状态，潜在接口通过阶梯式解码在不同Transformer深度间传递连续CoT状态；同时用固定的潜在到文本重建目标作为语义锚点。

Result: 在离线两步规划评估中，X-Planner在BERTScore-F1和基于裁判的Overall得分上均位列四个评估模型中的第二名；在真实机器人实验中，分别优于所评估的基线方法。

Conclusion: X-Planner通过层级监督和双接口表征有效平衡了规划文本的可解释性与连续推理的保持，改善了规划文本质量和下游执行性能，为VLA系统提供了更显式的规划中间结构。

Abstract: Task planning bridges high-level instructions and executable behavior in long-horizon manipulation, yet modern Vision-Language-Action (VLA) systems often leave this intermediate structure implicit. Existing chain-of-thought (CoT) planners also tend to rely on coarse task-level annotations or serialize long reasoning traces token by token. We present X-Planner, a planning front-end that addresses both the supervision and representation of embodied reasoning. Our planning data combine Ego, UMI, and teleoperation under a hierarchy granularity with source-dependent annotation depth. Takeover-time annotations and human-designed failures supervise ongoing error recognition. On the model side, a shared VLM backbone exposes two event-structured plan forms: a discrete interface that emits interpretable event states and a latent interface that relays continuous CoT states across staggered Transformer depths through Staircase Decoding. A frozen latent-to-text reconstruction objective provides a semantic anchor for the latent representation. Offline two-step planning evaluation places X-Planner second among four evaluated models on both BERTScore-F1 and a judge-based Overall score. In real-robot experiments, respectively, outperforming the evaluated baselines. These results characterize planning-text quality and downstream execution.

</details>


### [98] [Lean Pool: An AI-Maintained Archive of Formalized Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25199)
*Vasily Ilin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Lean Pool 是一个由 AI 智能体持续维护、培育和优化的形式化数学知识库（repository），汇集了形式化验证的数学内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着形式化数学（如 Lean 证明助手）的不断发展，需要一种可持续的机制来收集、维护并优化大量的形式化数学知识，而 AI 智能体可承担这一职责，提升知识库的生长效率与质量。

Method: 构建 Lean Pool 作为集中式形式化数学仓库，利用 AI 智能体对其内容进行持续的扩充（grown）、维护（maintained）以及优化（optimized）。

Result: 形成了一套由 AI 智能体驱动、可持续演进的形式化数学知识库 Lean Pool。

Conclusion: Lean Pool 展示了借助 AI 智能体自动培育和优化形式化数学知识库的可行性，为形式化数学的规模化发展提供了基础设施支撑。

Abstract: Lean Pool is a repository of formalized mathematics. It is grown, maintained and optimized by AI agents.

</details>


### [99] [The AI Neuroscientist: An Interactive Agentic Interface for Neuroimaging Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25254)
*Aakash Patel,Panos Ketonis,Shreya Saxena,Smita Krishnaswamy,David van Dijk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AI Neuroscientist，一个将大语言模型与神经影像工具集成的语言智能体，支持通过自然语言进行交互式数据探索，包括质量控制、建模和可视化，并在fNIRS数据上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经影像数据分析需要专门的编码和统计专业知识，这限制了没有计算背景的研究人员的使用。现有的脚本化流程对小型数据探索不够灵活和透明，需要一个更易访问的交互式替代方案。

Method: 系统将大语言模型（LLM）与神经影像工具集集成，支持质量控制、建模和可视化等功能。用户可直接通过自然语言查询数据质量并指定分析参数。作者使用功能近红外光谱（fNIRS）数据演示能力，并构建了自定义fNIRS基准测试套件来评估智能体，与具有代码沙箱的通用LLM智能体进行对比。

Result: 该系统在fNIRS数据上展示了质量控制、建模和可视化的能力，并通过自定义基准套件进行了评估，与通用LLM智能体相比表现出竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 未来工作将把架构扩展到其他模态（如fMRI数据），并扩展基准测试套件以涵盖更多的fNIRS任务，进一步提升系统的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: Analyzing neuroimaging data requires specialized coding and statistical expertise, which limits accessibility for researchers without computational backgrounds. We present the AI Neuroscientist, a language agent for interactive data exploration. The system integrates a large language model (LLM) with a neuroimaging toolset to perform quality control, modeling, and visualization. This allows researchers to query data quality and specify analysis parameters directly in natural language, providing a transparent and interactive alternative to conventional scripted pipelines for small-scale data exploration. We demonstrate these capabilities using functional near-infrared spectroscopy (fNIRS) data, and evaluate the agent on a custom fNIRS benchmarking suite against general-purpose LLM agents with code sandboxes. Future extensions will generalize the architecture to additional modalities, including functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) data, and expand the benchmarking suite to additional fNIRS tasks.

</details>


### [100] [MedGate-Fusion: Integrating First-Encounter Semantic Narratives and Physiological Biomarkers for Prospective Stroke Risk Stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25272)
*Hemn Khdr,Mohammad Noaeen,Karim Keshavjee,Aziz Guergachi,Zahra Shakeri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MedGate-Fusion，一种多模态门控架构，用于初级医疗场景下的前瞻性卒中风险分层。该模型整合了首次就诊临床叙述的Transformer嵌入与十项常规风险标记，基于加拿大初级医疗哨点监测网络（CPCSSN）的电子病历数据构建队列，最终纳入102,736名患者评估五年卒中结局，并通过词典脱敏减少诊断提及导致的标签泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 初级医疗中的前瞻性卒中风险分层具有挑战性，因为早期风险信号分散在常规生物标志物和非结构化的临床叙述中，亟需一种能够整合多模态信息进行有效风险预测的方法。

Method: MedGate-Fusion采用多模态门控架构，将首次就诊叙述的Transformer嵌入与十项常规记录的风险标记相融合；使用CPCSSN数据，从808,921条就诊记录中构建首次就诊队列，保留102,736条包含非空叙述且有足够数据的患者记录；在语义编码前应用基于词典的卒中相关术语脱敏以减少目标泄漏。

Result: 构建了包含102,736名独特患者记录的首次就诊队列，用于评估五年卒中结局。

Conclusion: MedGate-Fusion为初级医疗环境中的卒中风险分层提供了一种多模态融合方法，通过门控机制整合结构化风险标记与非结构化临床叙述，并注意控制标签泄漏问题。

Abstract: Prospective stroke risk stratification in primary care is challenging because early risk signals are distributed across routine biomarkers and unstructured clinical narratives. We propose MedGate-Fusion, a multi-modal gated architecture that integrates transformer-based embeddings of first-encounter narratives with ten routinely recorded risk markers. We used electronic medical record data from the Canadian Primary Care Sentinel Surveillance Network (CPCSSN). Starting from 808,921 encounter-level observations, we constructed a first-encounter cohort and retained 102,736 unique patient records with non-empty narratives and sufficient data to evaluate a five-year stroke outcome. To reduce explicit target leakage from diagnostic mentions in notes, we applied dictionary-based redaction of stroke-related terms prior to semantic encoding.

</details>


### [101] [When LLM Agents Fail to Read the Room: ReAdapt for Relational Social Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25284)
*Jianzhe Lin,Xiaolin Li,Yunda Liu,Fei Wang,Jubin Chheda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出ReAdapt框架，通过在ReAct循环中引入显式结构化社交状态与类型化Adapt步骤，帮助LLM智能体利用关系证据（如亲密度、互惠性、共同好友）来修正初始直觉决策，在反应选择和温暖引荐两个任务上分别提升8和14个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交智能体的基本决策（是否回复帖子、应联系谁）本质上是关系问题而非纯内容问题。标准LLM智能体循环未显式表示新获得的关系证据应如何修正当前社交假设，导致在关系和内容线索相矛盾时容易做出表面直觉性的错误选择。

Method: （1）构建RelationshipBench基准：500个合成社交世界，包含友谊、关注、反应历史和动态流，产生1000个查询覆盖两个任务；（2）提出ReAdapt框架：在ReAct循环基础上增加显式结构化社交状态z=(G,B,R,N,D)，分别表示目标、信念、关系、规范和披露；每次工具观察后执行类型化Adapt步骤来更新状态，并输出策略操作（继续、切换、放弃或澄清），再选择下一步行动。

Result: 使用Gemini-3-Flash在每任务n=150查询的分层子集上，ReAdapt将温暖引荐准确率从37%提升至51%（+14点），反应选择准确率从69%提升至77%（+8点）。Oracle遗憾分别从0.260降至0.152，从0.095降至0.053。

Conclusion: 在模型、工具和环境保持不变的情况下，显式关系状态适应能帮助LLM智能体将检索到的社交证据转化为修正后的决策，表明结构化的关系建模对社交智能体的决策质量具有显著价值。

Abstract: A social agent's most basic decisions (should I react to this post? who should I reach out to?) are not purely content problems. The right action often hinges on the latent relationship between people -- tie strength, reciprocity, mutual connections -- rather than on which content is most salient. Standard LLM agent loops do not explicitly represent how new relational evidence should revise the agent's current social hypothesis, leaving them prone to surface-obvious choices when relational and content cues diverge. We formalize this failure mode with a relationship-reasoning benchmark: 500 synthetic social worlds with friendships, follows, reaction histories, and feeds, yielding 1,000 queries over two tasks, reaction selection and warm introduction (finding the best bridge to a target person). By construction, the surface-obvious candidate differs from the relationship-grounded oracle in about 53% of queries, forming an overturn subset where the agent must use relational evidence to revise an initially plausible choice. We propose ReAdapt (Relationship-Adaptive Agent with Policy-driven sTate), which augments the ReAct loop with an explicit structured social state z = (G, B, R, N, D) capturing goal, belief, relationship, norm, and disclosure. After each tool observation, ReAdapt runs a typed Adapt step that updates this state and emits a policy operation (continue, switch, abandon, or clarify) before choosing the next action. With Gemini-3-Flash on a stratified subset of n = 150 queries per task, ReAdapt improves warm-introduction accuracy from 37% to 51% (+14 points) and reaction-selection accuracy from 69% to 77% (+8 points). Oracle regret drops from 0.260 to 0.152 and from 0.095 to 0.053, respectively. Holding the model, tools, and environments fixed, these results suggest that explicit relational-state adaptation helps LLM agents turn retrieved social evidence into revised decisions.

</details>


### [102] [Attention as a Routing Graph: Live Circuit Extraction from a Single Forward Pass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25285)
*Ash Manvi,Samreena Tajreen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种廉价的语言模型电路发现方法：将一次前向传播中的注意力视为路由图，仅保留指向答案的小规模路径集合，并通过消融实验验证这些路径是否真正具备因果作用。实验表明，在induction和IOI等已知电路的任务上，消融提取出的边比随机消融同等规模的边对模型伤害更大；提取仅需一次前向传播，而逐头patch扫描的成本高出约两个数量级。作者不声称提供完整电路图谱，而是提供一个携带真实因果信号且存在明确失效模式的廉价草图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，在语言模型中发现电路需要执行大量精细干预，成本高昂且繁琐。作者试图简化这一过程：不进行大量因果干预，而是仅从单次前向传播的注意力权重中提取路由图，并考察这些指向答案的路径是否真的在因果上起作用，以低成本换取可用的因果信号。

Method: 将一次前向传播的注意力视为路由图，保留一小部分指向答案方向的路径（边），然后对这些提取出的边进行消融，比较其与随机消融同等规模边的效果差异。评估采用成对差距检验（paired gap tests）与bootstrap置信区间，在GPT-2 Small、GPT-2 Medium和Pythia-410M三个模型上各用n=100个提示进行测试。提取步骤成本为一次前向传播，而逐头patch扫描的成本高出约两个数量级。

Result: 在induction和IOI这两个已知"正确"电路的任务上，消融提取出的边对模型的伤害显著大于消融随机同等规模的边，说明提取到的路由确实携带真实的因果信号。同时该方法具有明确失效模式，且作者明确不声称其提供完整电路图谱。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种廉价（单次前向传播）的电路草图画法，能在已知任务上携带真实因果信号，但仍存在清晰的应用边界与失效场景；代码与评估工件已公开在GitHub上。

Abstract: Finding circuits in language models usually means running many careful interventions. We try something simpler: treat attention as a routing map from one forward pass, keep a small set of routes that point toward the answer, and ask whether those routes actually matter.
  They often do. On induction and IOI (tasks where the "right" circuit is already known), ablating our extracted edges hurts the model much more than ablating a random set of the same size. We evaluate n=100 prompts per cell on GPT-2 Small, GPT-2 Medium, and Pythia-410M, with paired gap tests and bootstrap confidence intervals. The extract step costs one forward; a head-by-head patch sweep costs about two orders of magnitude more.
  We are not claiming a complete circuit atlas. We are claiming a cheap sketch that carries real causal signal on known tasks, with clear failure modes when it does not. Code and evaluation artifacts are at https://github.com/Aquinf03/live-circuit-routing.

</details>


### [103] [Learned Enterprise Data Comprehension: Compression and Routing for Data Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25286)
*Ethan Torres,Eric Mills*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"潜在等价学习"(latent equivalence learning)框架，将智能体擅长的语义推理与学习系统擅长的结构预测相结合。通过将持久任务相关身份与其数据集相关的实现分离，使用支持/反对证据引导的高斯原型和软成员分布来学习数据环境中的身份表达，并通过学习到的查询-原型兼容函数将查询映射到持久身份结构，实现查询条件化的证据路由。在Data Agent Benchmark上达到94.67%的Pass@1，远超基准的55.51%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级结构化数据智能体需要在复杂数据环境中推理，相关证据分散在schema、关系、策略和业务角色中。现有智能体系统虽通过可复用的markdown风格记忆或技能文件减少重复发现结构的需求，但这掩盖了智能体与学习系统之间的自然分工：智能体擅长语义推理，而学习系统擅长预测和组织重复出现的结构。作者希望弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 提出潜在等价学习框架，将持久任务相关身份与数据集相对实现分离。具体实现包括：(1) 支持与反对证据塑造"支持实现的高斯原型"(support-realized Gaussian prototypes)，学习特定数据环境中身份的表达方式；(2) 软成员分布(soft-membership profiles)保留硬分配下丢失的区别；(3) 单独的学习查询-原型系统表示重复出现的证据需求，通过学习到的兼容函数映射到相同的持久身份结构；(4) 身份因子化、查询条件化的路由将相关数据集特定证据物化给下游推理。

Result: 在Data Agent Benchmark上（12个异构数据集、54个查询），完整实现达到94.67%的数据集宏分层Pass@1（5次完整试验），258/270次成功的原始查询尝试，相比之下基准Claude Opus 4.6参考智能体仅为55.51%，提交时在40个排行榜条目中排名第一。

Conclusion: 身份因子化、查询条件化的证据路由方法显著优于传统智能体记忆/技能文件方案，通过将结构预测交给学习系统、将语义推理留给智能体，实现跨schema证据的高效组织，使智能体能够在已组织好的证据状态下操作而非每次查询都重新构建跨schema结构。

Abstract: Structured-data agents in enterprise settings must reason over complex data environments whose relevant evidence is distributed across schemas, relationships, policies, and recurring business roles. Modern agentic systems often address this burden through reusable markdown-style memory or skill files that preserve previously discovered information for later queries, reducing the need to rediscover the same structure repeatedly. This is useful, but it obscures a natural division of labor: agents are well suited to semantic reasoning, while learned systems are well suited to predicting and organizing recurring structure. We introduce latent equivalence learning to bridge this gap. The framework separates persistent task-relevant identities from their dataset-relative realizations. In our realization, supporting and opposing evidence shape support-realized Gaussian prototypes that learn how those identities are expressed in a particular data environment, while soft-membership profiles retain distinctions lost under a hard assignment. A separate learned query-prototype system represents recurring evidential requirements and maps them through a learned compatibility function into the same persistent identity structure. This identity-factorized, query-conditioned routing materializes the relevant dataset-specific evidence for downstream reasoning, allowing the agent to operate over an already organized evidential state rather than reconstructing cross-schema structure at every query. On the Data Agent Benchmark, spanning 54 queries across 12 heterogeneous datasets, our full implementation achieves 94.67% dataset-macro stratified Pass@1 over five complete trials and 258/270 successful raw query attempts, compared with 55.51% for the benchmark's Claude Opus 4.6 reference agent, ranking first among 40 leaderboard entries at submission.

</details>


### [104] [Making Agents More Consistent: Skills Should Form Habits for Repeat Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25299)
*Travis Weber,Rohit Taneja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"技能习惯形成"（skill habit formation）方法，让AI智能体从自身执行历史中挖掘可复用的确定性技能变体，通过四级筛选门控将常见路径固化为脚本执行，而非每次都重新推理。在text-to-SQL任务中，该方法将重复问题的输出一致性从38%-74%提升至100%，同时减少14%-56%的token消耗，并以可量化的准确性代价换取可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在重复任务上表现不一致（38%-74%的输出不一致率），这是买家、审计师和监管者所不能接受的。此外，智能体生成的95.3%-97.2%内容是在重复推导系统已知的计划，存在巨大的计算浪费。因此需要一种机制将已知的推理路径固化，保证可重复性和可审计性，同时降低推理成本。

Method: 提出技能习惯形成机制：智能体从自身执行历史中挖掘候选技能（确定性变体），与现有做法竞争而非直接替换。候选技能声明其声明的输入空间范围，使常见情况按脚本运行，其余情况回退到推理。通过四级递增成本的筛选门控来接纳候选技能，核心门控将候选的执行轨迹与保留的参考轨迹对比，容差由参考轨迹自身的运行间变异性度量。

Result: 在42个重复问题×多次执行的text-to-SQL实验中，四个推理臂分别仅在11-26/42的问题上能复现自身输出，而技能习惯形成后的变体在456次重复调度中全部一致复现，且对每个被替换的推理臂非劣（p<0.0001）。token使用减少14%-56%，在7-53次复用后实现净收益。代价方面：守卫在2.6%的自然改写和26%的边界输入上错误放行，11/13的此类失败对轨迹一致性门控在任何阈值下都不可见。将路由与参数提取分离使端到端准确率从0.888提升至0.952，且成本仅为原来的43%。

Conclusion: 技能习惯形成能以极小的准确性代价获得系统的可审计性和输出一致性，但需要认识到其根本权衡：确定性错误会精确重复，坏习惯与好习惯一样可靠，这正是可审计系统的代价。同时，将路由决策与参数提取分离能够显著提升端到端准确率和成本效率。

Abstract: On repeated work, agents are inconsistent. We ran 42 tasks three times each and found that, depending on the model, 38% to 74% returned answers that did not agree. Consistency is what a buyer, an auditor, or a regulator requires, and agents do not have it. They are wasteful too: 95.3% to 97.2% of what an agent generates goes to re-deriving a plan the system already knows.
  We propose skill habit formation. An agent mines its own execution history for candidate skills, deterministic variants that compete against the incumbent rather than replacing it. A candidate declares the region of input space it claims, so the common case runs as a script and the rest falls through to reasoning. Four gates of ascending cost admit candidates; the central one tests a candidate's execution trace against a retained reference, within a tolerance measured from that reference's own run-to-run variability.
  On text-to-SQL, three of four reasoning arms reproduced their own output on 11 to 13 of 42 repeated questions and the fourth on 26 of 42, while a habit-formed variant reproduced on all 456 dispatches we repeated and was non-inferior to every arm it replaced (p<0.0001). It also used 14% to 56% fewer tokens, turning net positive after 7 to 53 reuses.
  We measured what this costs in accuracy. The guard admitted work it should have deferred on 2.6% of natural paraphrases and 26% of inputs near its boundary, and 11 of 13 such failures were invisible to the trace-conformance gate at any threshold. Deterministic errors repeat exactly: a bad habit is as reliable as a good one, and that is the price of the property that makes the system auditable. Separating routing from parameter extraction raised end-to-end accuracy from 0.888 to 0.952 at 43% of the cost.

</details>


### [105] [Potential for Enhanced Learning in Machine Learning Classes by Using Wiki LLM Indexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25303)
*Brian Wright*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对两种知识表示方式——向量RAG检索增强生成与LLM编译的wiki百科（Karpathy框架）——在真实机器学习课程语料上进行头对头对比。结果显示，两种方法在单事实问答上表现相当，但在跨单元概念链接、综合解释及课程修订后的时效性问题上，wiki结构在准确性和可追溯性上显著优于向量RAG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型作为课程专属导师的实用性依赖于经过审核的教学材料，而这些材料常会在学期中修订。先前工作发现固定检索策略并非最优，这引发了一个新问题：语料在摄取时的结构组织是否比查询时的检索量更重要？本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 在同一真实课堂语料（Foundations of Machine Learning）上进行受控头对头比较：(A) 向量RAG——复现先前研究的最佳配置；(B) LLM编译wiki（Karpathy框架）——在摄取时将语料合成为带交叉引用和源材料引用的链接概念页。使用59个问题（涵盖单事实回忆、跨单元概念链接、综合解释和课纲修订后的时效性）进行评估，由LLM裁判依据人工编写的评分标准打分。

Result: 两种方法在单事实问答上表现接近（9.33 vs. 9.96/10），但在需要跨课程单元链接的问题上差异显著：编译wiki保持高准确性和完全可追溯性（9.93分；100%引用来源），而向量RAG得分较低且可追溯性明显不足（8.14分；64%）。wiki的引用机制使学生和教师能将任何主张追溯至引入该主张的讲座，提供了动态检索能力。

Conclusion: 尽管仍需进一步测试，但使用AI支持机器学习课程学习教学者应考虑采用wiki式结构，因其在支撑最佳实践基础要素方面具有潜力，特别是在跨单元知识链接和内容可追溯性方面。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as course-specific tutors, but their usefulness depends on grounding in vetted instructional materials that are often revised mid-semester. Our prior work built a multimodal retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system over an authentic machine learning course corpus (Foundations of Machine Learning) and found that retrieval improved contextual grounding, but that fixed retrieval strategies were suboptimal. That motivates a different question: whether how a corpus is structured at ingest time matters more than how much is retrieved at query time. We present a controlled head-to-head comparison of two knowledge representations over an identical classroom corpus: (A) vector RAG, replicating the best-performing configuration from our prior study, and (B) an LLM-compiled wiki (Karpathy framework), in which the corpus is synthesized at ingest into linked concept pages with explicit cross-references and citations back to source materials. We evaluate 59 questions spanning single-fact recall, cross-unit concept linking, synthesis and explanation, and currency after a syllabus revision, scored by an LLM judge against a human-authored rubric. Both representations answered single-fact questions about equally well (9.33 vs. 9.96 of 10), but diverged sharply on questions requiring links across course units. The compiled wiki remained accurate and grounded (9.93; 100% grounded in cited sources), while retrieval scored lower and was markedly less grounded (8.14; 64%). The wiki's citations let students and instructors trace any claim back to the lecture that introduced it, adding a layer of dynamic retrieval that machine learning courses require. While further testing is needed, instructors using AI to support learning in ML courses should consider wiki-based structure for its potential to support foundational elements of best practice.

</details>


### [106] [Clarification Is Not Correction: LLMs Fail to Let Go](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25337)
*Jianzhe Lin,Xiaolin Li,Fei Wang,Robert Douglas,Rajeshkumar Golani,Jubin Chheda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"早期后验坍缩"（early posterior collapse）概念，指出对话失败并非仅因记忆问题，更根本的原因是模型在歧义未消除前过早将未解决的用户意图锁定为单一任务状态。通过写作、规划、编码三类受控对话任务实验发现，即使最终对话包含等价信息，不同信息顺序会导致不同结果，且编码任务更易受影响。标准提示与记忆策略无法可靠解决此问题，从而呼吁转向不确定性保持的状态管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点将语言模型对话失败归因于记忆问题（上下文过长、摘要丢失、约束遗忘），但作者认为这忽略了更深层的问题：模型在对话中并非遗忘，而是过早承诺（over-commit）。早期歧义性轮次被坍缩为单一隐藏解释，后续澄清被这一承诺过滤，导致无法纠正。

Method: 使用Gemini-2.5-Pro和Gemini-2.5-Flash，在写作、规划、编码三类受控对话任务上进行数千次试验。系统性地改变信息呈现顺序，使最终对话包含等效的任务相关信息，观察输入顺序对输出结果的影响。

Result: 相同信息在不同顺序下产生不同结果，证实了顺序效应：后续澄清被当作额外上下文而非纠正信号，只会细化过时任务状态而不会使其失效。编码任务尤其脆弱，早期假设会嵌入结构化工件（接口、控制流）。标准提示策略（摘要、链式思维）均无法可靠解决：摘要可能坍缩歧义，链式思维虽能减少推理轨迹中的显式错误承诺，却未能提升最终任务成功率。

Conclusion: 稳健性不能仅依赖事后修正。交互式LLM需要不确定性保持的状态管理：在歧义存在时保持暂定假设，高影响力歧义持续时先询问再执行，当后期证据使早期解读失效时从修正后的状态重建。研究方向应从"保留更多上下文"转向"保留不确定性"。

Abstract: Dialogue failures in language models are usually framed as memory failures: context too long, summaries lossy, a constraint forgotten. We argue this misses a deeper problem: in many conversations the model does not forget, it commits too early. An ambiguous early turn collapses into a single hidden interpretation, and later clarification is filtered through that commitment. We call this early posterior collapse: unresolved user intent collapsing into a committed task state before ambiguity is resolved. We study it with controlled dialogue tasks in writing, planning, and coding using Gemini-2.5-Pro and Gemini-2.5-Flash. Across thousands of trials, the same information in different orders yields different outcomes, even when the final dialogue contains equivalent task-relevant information. This order effect suggests later clarification is treated as extra context rather than a corrective signal: it refines a stale task state without invalidating it. Coding tasks are especially vulnerable, suggesting early assumptions get embedded in structured artifacts such as interfaces and control flow. Standard prompting and memory strategies do not reliably help: summaries can collapse ambiguity, and chain-of-thought can reduce explicit wrong commitment in reasoning traces without improving final task success. These findings motivate uncertainty-preserving state management. If assistants cannot let go of early interpretations, robustness cannot rely on post hoc correction alone; it must keep ambiguous early turns from hardening into one task state. Assistants should hold tentative hypotheses while ambiguity remains, ask before executing when high-impact ambiguity persists, and rebuild from a revised state when later evidence invalidates an earlier reading. Rather than one prompting fix, we aim to redirect research for interactive LLMs from retaining more context toward preserving uncertainty.

</details>


### [107] [From Decorative to Load-Bearing: Task Difficulty Shapes the Causal Role of Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25366)
*Renee Jia,Di Mu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"基于延续的因果测试"方法评估思维链（CoT）推理是否真正因果约束答案，并发现CoT的负载承载度随任务难度变化：简单任务中模型"静默绕过"自身推理，困难任务中则跟随并传播被篡改的推理错误。虽然推理特定RL能抑制错误传播，且错误传播/非传播标签对评测提示具有不变性且标注者间一致性完美（κ=1.00），但该梯度构成CoT监管的结构性问题——易于阅读的轨迹信号弱，而关键处错误在监控介入前已传播。行为模式可读但不可靠地可控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链（CoT）监控只有在书面推理对答案具有因果约束力时才有意义，但现有方法缺乏对这种"负载承载性"（load-bearingness）的量化评估；这一行为层面上的因果关系有别于机制的忠实度，需要新的测试范式来揭示模型推理是否真正依赖其CoT。

Method: 提出延续式因果测试（continuation-based causal testing），一种消融-补丁干预：扰动单个推理步骤、截断思维链、强制模型从损坏前缀继续生成。在Gemma-2-9B-IT、Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct、DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B上，对GSM8K、MMLU、BIG-Bench Hard进行配对2x2分析，并对28,584个延续进行方差分解；辅以推理特定RL训练、四变体评判灵敏度分析、盲法双标注研究（n=500）及线性探针与加性激活引导实验。

Result: CoT负载承载度随模型相对任务难度变化：简单任务中模型静默绕过推理，困难任务中跟随损坏步骤并传播错误。错误传播从GSM8K到BBH多步算术上升16倍，方差分解中任务难度解释98.8%的方差而扰动类型仅占0.8%；推理特定RL抑制错误传播并压缩梯度；错误传播标签对评判提示不变且标注者间一致性完美（Cohen's kappa=1.00）。线性探针可分离静默绕过、自我修正与错误传播，但加性激活引导仅能翻转约25%的错误传播案例。

Conclusion: 存在一条随任务难度变化的错误传播梯度，这为基于CoT的监督与AI安全监控带来结构性难题：推理轨迹易于阅读时携带的信号很少，而在关键任务中错误在监控器介入之前就已传播。行为模式（静默绕过、自我修正、错误传播）可被线性探针读取，但通过激活引导难以实现可靠的因果控制。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is only meaningful if written reasoning causally constrains the answer. We introduce continuation-based causal testing, an ablation-patch intervention that perturbs one reasoning step, truncates the chain, and forces the model to continue from the corrupted prefix. It measures how load-bearing a CoT is for the final answer, a behavioral notion distinct from mechanistic faithfulness. Across Gemma-2-9B-IT, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, and DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B on GSM8K, MMLU, and BIG-Bench Hard, CoT load-bearingness tracks model-relative task difficulty: on easy tasks models silently bypass their own reasoning; on hard tasks they follow corrupted steps and propagate errors. A matched 2x2 analysis shows task difficulty dominates perturbation type: error propagation rises 16x from GSM8K to BBH multistep arithmetic, and a variance partition over 28,584 continuations attributes 98.8% of explained deviance to task difficulty versus 0.8% to perturbation type. Reasoning-specific RL suppresses error propagation and compresses the gradient. A four-variant judge-sensitivity analysis and blind two-annotator study (n=500) show the error-propagation vs. non-propagation label is invariant to judge prompt, with perfect inter-annotator agreement (Cohen's kappa = 1.00). This gradient creates a structural problem for CoT-based oversight and AI safety monitoring: where the trace is easy to read it carries little signal, and where it matters errors propagate before a monitor can intervene. Linear probes on hidden states separate silent bypass, self-correction, and error propagation, but additive activation steering provides limited causal control, flipping only about 25% of error-propagation cases at best. Behavioral mode is readable but not reliably controllable.

</details>


### [108] [Robust Failure, Conservative Repair: Textual Knowledge Distillation from Cross-Model Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25400)
*Andrew Ren,Haokun Liu,Chenhao Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RFCR（稳健失败、保守修复）文本知识蒸馏框架，通过跨模型共享失败样本提取规则、用边界案例收紧适用边界、无有效规则时放弃注入，在BIG-Bench Hard上使基线备忘单从68.50%提升至71.25%（+2.75 pp），且不损害先前正确案例的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于失败的文本知识蒸馏旨在通过分析模型任务错误来发现知识缺口，并将蒸馏出的规则注入文本输入以辅助推理。然而这种知识迁移不稳定：单一模型失败导出的规则（规则原子）可能携带模型特异的推理补丁，干扰其他模型；且注入的规则可能被错误应用于无关案例，导致模型基于无关信息误翻转答案。早期实验表明单模型失败蒸馏的规则在非源模型上低于基线备忘单性能，故需更稳健的蒸馏机制。

Method: RFCR包含三个关键环节：(1) 从跨模型共享的失败中导出规则（避免模型特异补丁）；(2) 利用边界案例（boundary cases）收紧规则的应用边界，明确规则适用的条件范围；(3) 在未发现有效规则时选择弃权（abstain），不盲目注入。整体构建于将训练样本蒸馏为任务特定备忘单的流水线之上。

Result: 在400项BIG-Bench Hard任务集上，RFCR将基线备忘单从68.50%提升至71.25%（+2.75个百分点；95%置信区间[+1.25, +4.50]），且在先前正确案例上无性能退化。消融实验与跨模型诊断表明精度增益来源于新知识注入与严格的规则应用控制两者共同作用。

Conclusion: 规则蒸馏的稳定性关键在于两方面：知识来源需跨模型共享以滤除模型特异信息，规则应用需严格的边界约束与弃权机制以防止误用。RFCR验证了"稳健失败+保守修复"策略能在增益新知识的同时避免规则误用带来的性能退化。

Abstract: Failure-based textual knowledge distillation aims to discover gaps in a model's knowledge by examining its task errors. The distilled knowledge can be useful for the reasoning of both this model ("source model") and other models. However, this transfer of knowledge may not be stable. We define a rule atom to be a standalone rule injected into a model's textual input at inference time. A rule atom can encode transferable task knowledge or model-specific reasoning patches that can confuse other models. Also, the injected rule atoms can be misapplied to unrelated cases, causing the model to incorrectly flip its answer based on irrelevant information. Building on a pipeline that distills training examples into task-specific cheat sheets that aid model reasoning, we examine when failure-derived rules can improve these cheat sheets. Our early experiment shows rule distillation from a single model's failures underperforms the baseline cheat sheet on non-source model families. This motivates Robust Failure, Conservative Repair (RFCR), a textual distillation procedure that derives rules from failures shared across models, sharpens their application boundaries using boundary cases, and abstains when no useful rule is found. On a 400-item BIG-Bench Hard task set, RFCR improves the baseline cheat sheets from 68.50% to 71.25% (+2.75 pp; 95% CI [+1.25,+4.50]) without performance degradation on previously correct cases. Ablations and cross-model diagnostics support that accuracy gains come from both new knowledge injection and strict rule-application control.

</details>


### [109] [Efficient Iterative Retrieval with Heterogeneous Batching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25405)
*Dohyun Park,Hubertus Franke,Daniel G. Waddington,Swaminathan Sundararaman,Yongjoo Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Orthrus 是一个统一推理循环的服务系统，通过异构批处理（chunked embedding + 增量池化 + 负载感知的批次组合）在同一管道中同时容纳嵌入模型与生成模型，解决了传统系统将两者隔离执行导致的吞吐低下与GPU利用率差的问题，在4块A100上对比基线获得了1.28x–4.52x的吞吐提升及最高55.8%的p99延迟下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代信息检索系统日益同时依赖嵌入模型（检索）与生成模型（生成），但现有服务系统将这两类模型隔离在独立GPU上执行，导致吞吐低、GPU利用率差。粗粒度的静态划分（如专卡专用）无法适应动态负载，并产生计算"气泡"（资源空闲时段），因此需要一种能统一调度这类异构工作负载的机制。

Method: Orthrus 在统一的推理循环中执行异构批处理，核心包含两项机制：(1) chunked embedding 与增量池化，用于弥合嵌入（内存/IO密集、请求短）与生成（计算密集、请求长）之间计算模式的冲突；(2) 负载感知的批次组合调整，动态优化批次的构成以适配实时工作负载并最大化性能。

Result: 在4块A100 GPU上的评估显示：相比基线部署，Orthrus 在受控负载下实现了1.28x–4.52x的吞吐提升，并在迭代式RAG基准上将端到端p99延迟降低最高达55.8%。

Conclusion: 通过将嵌入与生成工作负载统一到同一推理循环并采用异构批处理，Orthrus 有效消除了静态划分带来的计算气泡，同时显著提升了吞吐与延迟表现，证明了负载感知的异构批处理是解决混合检索-生成服务瓶颈的有效路径。

Abstract: Modern information retrieval increasingly employs both embedding and generative models to handle complex queries. However, current serving systems suffer from low throughput and poor GPU utilization because they execute these models in isolation. Coarse-grained partitioning, such as dedicating GPUs to specific tasks, fails to adapt to dynamic workloads and creates computational "bubbles". To address these, we present Orthrus, a serving system that performs heterogeneous batching within a unified inference loop. The primary challenge lies in unifying embedding and generation workloads with conflicting computational patterns while optimizing batch composition for high performance. Orthrus addresses these challenges through chunked embedding with incremental pooling and by adjusting batch composition in a workload-aware manner. Evaluation on four A100 GPUs shows that, relative to baseline deployments, Orthrus achieves 1.28$\times$--4.52$\times$ higher throughput on controlled workloads and up to 55.8% lower end-to-end p99 latency on an iterative-RAG benchmark. We release our code at https://github.com/illinoisdata/Orthrus .

</details>


### [110] [From Offline Proxies to Online Decisions: A Layered Engagement Evaluation Framework for Conversational AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25408)
*Xuanyi Li,Vaskar Nath,Hossein Amirkhani,Jay Li,Alex Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种离线信号（composite proxy）来预测在线A/B实验的效果，以缓解在线实验流量和读取时间对对话式AI测试的限制。该离线信号由三个对齐环节构成：行为标签到产品结果、学习分类器到候选助手行为、聚合离线信号到实验效应。通过区间感知决策一致性和实验内排序的审计协议验证，在27个实验、489个配对对比上达到81.1%的F1分数（对比原始分类器34.3%），并能避免错误方向判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线A/B实验是用户参与度的决策标准，但流量和读取时间的限制使得对话式AI的改动难以充分测试。作者希望探索一种无需治疗组用户暴露即可计算的离线信号，看其是否与在线实验结果一致，从而在稀缺实验流量分配前优先排序候选方案。

Method: 构建离线代理的复用性框架，将离线代理视为三个对齐环节的链条：(1)行为标签到产品结果的对齐；(2)学习分类器到候选助手行为的对齐；(3)聚合离线信号到实验效应的对齐。配套评估协议通过区间感知决策一致性（比较离线与在线置信区间而非点估计）和实验内排序进行审计。实例化包括：固定评估套件、预测会话/提示级参与度的分类器、以及将样本级分数差映射到在线模型级参与度变化的校准层。

Result: 在部署的多轮助手上进行了27个实验、489个配对离线—在线对比（涵盖模型检查点到系统提示调优）。主要测试使用冻结映射后的8个实验中的113个对比，复合信号达到81.1%的F1分数（原始分类器为34.3%），无错误方向判断（原始分类器有31个）。所有离线预测均在实验运行前计算以防过拟合。

Conclusion: 证据支持使用复合离线信号在稀缺实验流量分配前对候选方案进行优先级排序，在实际部署中选择训练检查点和调优系统提示词时有效。

Abstract: Online A/B experiments are the decision standard for user engagement, but traffic and readout time limit how many conversational-AI changes can be tested. We ask whether an offline signal designed to be computable without treatment-arm user exposure agrees with the outcomes of those experiments. We contribute a reusable construction and diagnosis checklist that treats an offline proxy as a chain of three alignments: behavioral label to product outcome, learned classifier to candidate-assistant behavior, and aggregated offline signal to experiment effect. A companion evaluation protocol audits the whole composite by interval-aware decision agreement, which compares offline and online confidence intervals instead of point estimates, and by within-experiment ranking. The instantiation we evaluate comprises a fixed evaluation suite on which candidate behavior is scored, an engagement classifier trained to predict session/prompt level engagements, and a calibration layer mapping sample-level score differences to online model-level engagement deltas. We then report the audit: 489 paired offline-online contrasts (one candidate arm against its control) from 27 experiments on a deployed multi-turn assistant, spanning model checkpoints to system-prompt tuning. Our primary test uses the 113 contrasts from eight experiments that ran after the map was frozen: on these the composite reaches 81.1% F1, against 34.3% for the raw classifier score it is built on, and makes no wrong-direction calls where that raw score makes 31. Every offline prediction was computed before its experiment ran to prevent overfitting. The evidence supports using the composite to prioritize candidates before scarce experiment traffic is allocated---in our deployment of the experiment, selecting among training checkpoints and tuning system prompts.

</details>


### [111] [ZeroGate: Trust-Preserving Fast Paths for Governed AI Agent Runtimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25443)
*Zexun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ZeroGate系统，将授权前置化（提前准备授权），将精确动作审批与本地持久化准入分离。签发者签署短时效ActionPass，运行时适配器重建最终动作后由本地门检查绑定并消耗nonce，SQLite事务耦合nonce消耗、配额更新与受理回执。实验表明预先执行模式在worker准入到分发阶段延迟更低（p95 9.8-11.4ms vs 同步25-334ms），但完整生命周期平均延迟更长，说明边界改善并非净加速。贡献在于显式的再验证契约、持久化参考边界及授权成本位置的审计对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提前授权可以缩短agent的分发边界（dispatch boundary），且无需移除授权工作，但存在动作载荷、权限或相关状态在授权后发生变化的语义风险。作者希望在不引入新密码学原语的前提下，通过分离精确动作批准与本地持久化准入来获得更低的准入延迟，同时保持可验证性。

Method: ZeroGate实现了一个双层授权架构：(1) 签发者（issuer）签署短时效的ActionPass（精确动作批准）；(2) 可信运行时适配器（runtime adapter）在本地重建最终动作，经本地门（local gate）检查动作绑定并消耗nonce。SQLite事务将nonce消耗、配额更新和受理收据原子地耦合。作者提出条件性决策保持命题：若批准是健全的、所有策略依赖被表示且最新、观察是忠实的、消耗是原子的，则本地准入成功意味着指定的同步策略会在准入点授权同一动作。

Result: Azure Blob云实验（4800次尝试）显示：预先执行模式下worker准入到分发的p95延迟为9.802-11.374 ms，同步模式为25.018-334.000 ms（各并发级别下）。但预先执行模式的完整生命周期平均延迟在所有并发级别均更长，表明边界改进不是净加速。实现本身不能单独保证当前世界新鲜性或精确一次远程效果。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是一个显式的再验证契约（revalidation contract）、一个持久化的参考边界（durable reference boundary），以及关于授权成本在何处支付的审计式比较。作者明确声明这不是新的密码学原语或通用性能前沿，而是对授权前置化语义与成本权衡的系统性分析。

Abstract: Moving authorization earlier can shorten an agent's dispatch boundary without removing authorization work. It can also admit an action whose payload, authority, or relevant state has changed. ZeroGate separates exact-action approval from durable local admission: an issuer signs a short-lived ActionPass, and a trusted runtime adapter reconstructs the final action before a local gate checks its binding and consumes its nonce. A SQLite transaction couples nonce consumption, applicable quota updates, and an admission receipt. We state a conditional decision-preservation proposition: successful local admission implies that a specified synchronous policy would authorize the same action at the admission point, provided approval is sound, all policy dependencies are represented and current, observations are faithful, and consumption is atomic. The implementation alone establishes neither current-world freshness nor exactly-once remote effects. Evaluation separates authored semantic fixtures, controlled concurrency and crash experiments, and an Azure Blob study comparing synchronous and prepared execution through the same issuer and gate. Both modes mint an exact-action pass; lifecycle latency includes preparation and prepared-batch dwell. Across 4800 cloud attempts, prepared worker-admission-to-dispatch p95 ranges from 9.802 to 11.374 ms, versus 25.018 to 334.000 ms synchronously, across the tested concurrency levels. Prepared mean complete lifecycle is longer at every level: the boundary improvement is not a net speedup. The contribution is an explicit revalidation contract, a durable reference boundary, and an auditable comparison of where authorization cost is paid, not a new cryptographic primitive or a universal performance frontier.

</details>


### [112] [Rollout Efficiency in Reinforcement Learning for Reasoning Large Language Models: A Taxonomy and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25463)
*Niloofar Gholipour,Marcos Assuncao,Gursimran Singh,Timothy Yu,Rajkumar Buyya,Julien Gascon-Samson,Zhenan Fan,Yong Zhang,Xiaojie Xu,Yaqiang Yao,Xiaolong Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于面向推理的强化学习（reasoning-oriented RL）中 rollout 效率的系统性综述。作者从机制和瓶颈两个视角建立了对现有 rollout 效率研究的分类体系，分析了不同技术族如何解决各类 rollout 低效来源，并探讨了组合应用的机会与冲突、评估报告中的不足以及未来的开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向推理的强化学习使大语言模型能够解决数学、编程等多步任务，但将大量训练成本转移到 rollout 阶段（即生成轨迹以更新策略）。因此需要高效的 rollout 机制来降低该成本，同时保证训练数据的新鲜度、一致性和统计有效性。现有研究分散且缺乏系统的梳理视角，亟需一个从机制和瓶颈双重角度出发的分类框架。

Method: 以综述形式提出系统化的分类法（taxonomy），从两个互补视角（机制视角与瓶颈视角）对面向推理 RL 的 rollout 效率研究进行分类，并据此分析不同技术族各自解决的特定低效来源，以及它们之间的组合可行性与潜在冲突。

Result: 作者归纳了不同技术族如何针对不同 rollout 低效根源进行优化，指出了多技术组合应用的机会与潜在冲突，识别了效率提升评估与报告环节的空白，并汇总了当前研究中的关键开放问题。

Conclusion: 本文为面向推理 RL 的 rollout 效率研究提供了系统、全面的参考框架，凸显了该方向的未解挑战，并指明了未来研究方向，有助于推动低成本、高数据质量的推理强化学习训练。

Abstract: Reasoning-oriented reinforcement learning enables large language models to solve mathematical, coding, and other multi-step tasks, but shifts a substantial portion of the training cost to rollout, where trajectories are generated for policy updates. Efficient rollout mechanisms are therefore essential to reduce this cost while maintaining the freshness, consistency, and statistical validity of training data. This survey provides a systematic taxonomy of recent research on rollout efficiency for reasoning-oriented reinforcement learning, classifying existing approaches from both mechanism and bottleneck perspectives. Based on this taxonomy, we analyze how different technique families address distinct sources of rollout inefficiency, examine opportunities and potential conflicts for combining them, identify gaps in the evaluation and reporting of efficiency gains, and discuss open challenges and future research directions.

</details>


### [113] [Real-Time Hand Gesture Recognition for OpenXR Using Transformer-Based Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25466)
*Salar Rezayani,Russell Butler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过Unity中的OpenXR标准获取手部追踪数据（手部关节点位置与手腕旋转角度），整合基于Transformer的机器学习模型实现实时手势识别。系统利用Transformer的序列建模能力，在短手势窗口内捕捉时间依赖关系，并能跨手部朝向和尺寸进行鲁棒识别，显著提升了手势分类准确率，并为未来扩展到动作流（手势间转换）检测奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手势识别是人机交互（HCI）中的关键组成部分，可为游戏、虚拟现实（VR）、机器人等领域提供直观交互界面。现有方法往往难以在跨手部朝向和尺寸变化时保持鲁棒性，也未能充分利用手势动作中的时序依赖信息，因此需要一种能够捕捉时间序列特征且泛化能力强的识别方案。

Method: 将基于Transformer的机器学习模型集成到实时手势识别系统中。数据采集环节采用Unity中OpenXR标准进行手部追踪，提取手部关节的位置数据以及手腕旋转角度作为训练特征。核心思路是利用Transformer的序列建模能力，在短时间手势窗口内捕捉时序依赖关系，从而实现对不同手部朝向和大小的手势进行鲁棒分类。

Result: 实验结果表明，该系统的手势分类准确率相比基线获得了显著提升，验证了Transformer模型在手势识别中捕捉时序特征的优越性以及对不同朝向和手型差异的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了基于Transformer并结合OpenXR标准手部追踪数据的方法能有效提升手势识别准确率与鲁棒性。未来工作将在此基础上拓展至检测运动流，即识别手势与手势之间的过渡转换，从而支撑更复杂的连续交互场景。

Abstract: Hand gesture recognition is a key component in human-computer interaction (HCI), enabling intuitive interfaces for applications in gaming, virtual reality (VR), robotics, and more. This study integrates transformer-based machine-learning models for real-time hand gesture recognition, using hand-tracking data captured through the OpenXR standard in Unity. We leverage positional data of hand joints and wrist rotation angles to train a custom gesture recognition system. By utilizing the sequential modeling capabilities of transformers, the system captures temporal dependencies within short gesture windows and classifies gestures robustly across hand orientations and sizes. The results show a significant improvement in gesture classification accuracy. Building on this, we outline how the approach can be extended toward detecting the flow of movement, i.e., the transitions between gestures, as future work.

</details>


### [114] [ShowTellArena: Evaluating Business Workflow Understanding from Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25467)
*David Garg,Ritobrata Sarkar,Ehsan Azarnasab,Siddhartha Borah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ShowTellArena，一个用于评估智能体从商业演示教学中理解程度的基准协议与公开数据集。v1.0包含50个业务流程任务（涵盖财务、招聘、采购等六大领域），包含录屏、截图、旁白、种子数据及502个问题，并通过218次试点尝试分析展示了现有系统的回答错误及教学体验完成失败等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏系统化的方法验证智能体（agent）从他人演示教学（show-and-tell）中真正理解了什么。教学不仅是展示工作过程，还包括决策解释，而如何客观评估智能体对这些叙述性商业演示的理解程度仍是一个未解决的问题。

Method: 提出ShowTellArena基准协议：固定业务场景和测试问题，允许不同产品通过各自的教学接口捕获课程内容；构建包含50个业务流程任务的多模态数据集（录屏、截图、旁白、种子数据），设计502个覆盖操作规则、边界条件、异常处理和自动化错误检测的问题；选取218次试点尝试（39个流程案例，其中28个被三个系统全部尝试）进行分析。

Result: 试点结果暴露了两类主要问题：1）回答错误——系统对规则、边界和异常的理解不准确；2）教学体验完成失败——部分系统未能完成教学流程。数据集的覆盖不均衡、排除标准和评分来源存在验证缺口，且试点并非受控产品排名。

Conclusion: 该工作的贡献在于提供了一个可检查、可扩展的数据集和评估工作流，供社区后续使用和改进；但试点结果仅具有探索性，不应被解读为产品之间的受控对比排名。

Abstract: We often teach a colleague by showing the work and explaining the decisions as we go. How can we check what an agent understood from the same lesson? We introduce ShowTellArena, a benchmark protocol and public dataset for comprehension after narrated business demonstrations. The v1.0 release contains 50 business workflow tasks, with recordings, screenshots, narration, fixture seeds, and 502 questions. Tasks span finance, hiring, procurement, customer decisions, inventory, and logistics.
