The protocol holds the business scenario and quiz fixed while allowing each product to capture the lesson through its own teaching interface. Questions test operational rules, boundaries, exceptions, and errors in proposed automations. We analyze 218 selected pilot attempts across 39 workflow cases, including 28 cases attempted by all three evaluated systems. These exploratory results expose both answer errors and failures to complete the teaching experience. We describe the release's verification gaps and the pilot's uneven coverage, exclusions, and grading provenance. The contribution is an inspectable dataset and assessment workflow that others can extend; the selected pilot is not a controlled product ranking.

</details>


### [115] [RAG-NAROK: Retrieval-Aware Knowledge Corpus Poisoning in RAG with Source-specific Refutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25469)
*Abdullahil Kafi,Alvi Ataur Khalil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RAG-NAROK，一种针对检索增强生成（RAG）系统的自适应对抗攻击框架。与传统静态知识库投毒攻击不同，RAG-NAROK利用RAG管道的透明性，先提取合法来源身份，再生成"锚定特定驳斥文档"，通过明确贬低检索到的来源并利用时效性与权威性偏差，将文本生成引导至目标答案。实验表明该方法在多个领域显著优于静态基线，揭示了RAG透明性与AI安全之间的根本矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG已成为将LLM输出锚定在可验证外部知识上的主流架构，但其动态检索管道的结构性依赖引入了尚未被充分探索的对抗漏洞。现有知识库投毒攻击本质上是静态的，预计算的对抗文档对被攻击系统实际检索到的内容一无所知，盲于生成器上下文窗口中围绕其载荷的竞争性文档环境，攻击效果受限。

Method: RAG-NAROK分两步：首先利用RAG管道的透明性提取合法来源的身份信息；然后生成"锚定特定驳斥文档"，这些文档明确指名并贬低被检索的来源，同时利用时效性偏差和权威性偏差来操纵文本生成，使其偏向目标答案，从而攻击能够自适应查询文本。

Result: 实验结果显示，RAG-NAROK在多个不同领域均显著优于静态攻击基线，证明了自适应攻击的有效性，并揭示出RAG自身的透明特性成为可被利用的安全弱点。

Conclusion: 本研究表明RAG系统的透明性与其安全性之间存在根本性张力：使RAG可解释、可验证的透明机制，恰恰为这类自适应对抗攻击提供了便利条件，需要在RAG架构设计中重新权衡透明性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Retrieval augmented generation (RAG) systems have emerged as the dominant architecture for grounding large language model (LLM) outputs in verifiable external knowledge, yet their structural reliance on a dynamic retrieval pipeline introduces a largely unexplored class of adversarial vulnerability. Existing knowledge-base poisoning attacks are fundamentally static. Adversarial documents are pre-computed and injected without any awareness of what the victim system will actually retrieve for a given query, leaving the attack blind to the competitive documentary landscape that surrounds its payload in the generator's context window. Unlike traditional static poisoning attacks that are blind to the retrieved context, we introduce RAG-NAROK (Retrieval-Anchored Generation Negation And Response Quality Collapse), a RAG attack framework that adapts to the query text. RAG-NAROK exploits the transparency inherent in RAG pipeline to first extract the legitimate source identities, then generate Anchor-Specific Refutation documents that explicitly name and devalue retrieved sources while leveraging recency and authority biases to steer the text generation toward a target answer. Our results demonstrate that RAG-NAROK significantly outperforms static baselines across diverse domains, revealing a fundamental tension between RAG transparency and AI security.

</details>


### [116] [Spectra: A Rules-Driven LLM Pipeline for Automated KYC Document Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25474)
*Miray Wahib,Ethan Tran,Rea Mourad,Mira Muti,Nikita Dvornik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对资本市场KYC开户流程分析发现其由可重复组件构成，适合AI自动化。作者提出将传统四方流程整合为两方共享工作并消除交接延迟。引入Spectra平台：将合规规则编码为可查询数据库（结构化规则引擎）结合LLM分类/抽取/验证代理，将文档处理分解为独立、可审计的阶段。实测分类准确率100%、抽取准确率89.4%，人工审查负担降低96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KYC开户在资本市场的合规流程耗时巨大：分析师需手动分类文档、从异构来源抽取结构化数据、并对照复杂监管政策进行验证。端到端开户常因顺序递交接力而拖延数周。作者旨在通过分析流程、识别可自动化组件并重构工作流，从而显著缩短开户周期并降低人工负担。

Method: 首先对现有KYC开户流程进行剖析，识别可重复、适合AI自动化的环节。随后重构工作流：把传统的四方参与流程合并为两方，由双方共享大部分工作并协同自动化，从而移除造成延迟累积的中间交接。在剩余步骤中引入Spectra：该平台将合规政策编码为可查询数据库（结构化规则引擎），实现聚焦性上下文注入以降低token用量并提升抽取精度；同时摒弃单一巨型prompt，将文档处理拆分为相互隔离、可审计的独立阶段，每个阶段独立优化且可追溯至具体政策条款。

Result: 在真实KYC文档上的评估显示：Spectra实现100%的分类准确率和89.4%的抽取准确率，人工审查负担（工作量）下降96%。

Conclusion: KYC开户流程包含高度可自动化的重复组件；通过流程重构（四方合并为两方）结合规则引擎与LLM代理的混合架构（Spectra），可大幅提升分类与抽取精度、降低token消耗，并将人工审查负担削减96%，验证了该方案在实际合规场景中的可行性与高效性。

Abstract: Know Your Client (KYC) onboarding in capital markets requires analysts to manually classify documents, extract structured data from heterogeneous sources, and validate compliance against complex regulatory policies. This process requires significant analyst time per client, with end-to-end onboarding often stretching to multiple weeks due to sequential handoffs. In this work, we analyze an on-boarding process and find that it comprises repeatable components well-suited to AI automation. We therefore propose a restructured workflow to be amenable to automation: we consolidate the traditional four-party process into two parties that share most of the work and can be automated together, eliminating intermediate handoffs that compound delays. To automate the remaining steps, we introduce Spectra, an AI-assisted document processing platform that combines a structured rules engine with LLM-based classification, extraction, and validation agents. The rules engine encodes compliance policy as a queryable database, enabling focused context injection that reduces token usage while improving extraction precision. Rather than a single monolithic prompt, the system decomposes document processing into isolated, auditable stages, each optimized independently and traceable to specific policy clauses. In evaluation on real KYC documents, Spectra achieves 100% classification accuracy and 89.4% extraction accuracy. Human review burden dropped by 96%.

</details>


### [117] [Queer inclusion in speech datasets: An audit and taxonomy of practical tensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25491)
*Brooklyn Sheppard,Anaelia Ovalle,Adina Williams,Levent Sagun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审视了语音数据集中对LGBTQIA+（酷儿）群体的代表性，通过审计六个多样化语音数据集发现酷儿说话者占比极低（0-1.4%），不足以进行可靠的差异度量。作者以该群体为案例研究，探讨从边缘化社区收集语音数据所面临的挑战与张力，并额外审计了两个由酷儿社区创建、为酷儿社区服务的语音科学数据集进行对比，最终提出了描述这些张力的分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语音技术数据集中缺乏对LGBTQIA+群体的充分代表性，这阻碍了语音技术对这一群体的公平性和鲁棒性研究。作者旨在通过审计和分类学构建，理解为何此类数据稀缺，并探索与边缘化社区合作收集数据时存在的系统性张力。

Method: 对六个多样化语音数据集进行审计以量化酷儿说话者代表性；额外审计两个由酷儿社区参与创建的语音科学数据集作为对比；基于审计结果提出一个描述语音数据收集实践中与参与式方法价值观冲突张力的分类体系（taxonomy）。

Result: 审计发现六个数据集中酷儿说话者占比仅为0-1.4%，远不足以支撑可靠的差异测量。对比数据集显示由社区主导创建的数据集在代表性上具有不同特征。研究识别出AI和语音技术研究中的常见数据收集惯例与边缘化社区参与式方法所强调的价值观之间存在多重冲突，并形成了一套张力分类体系。

Conclusion: 语音数据集的构建实践需要在社区参与、数据主权与AI研究需求之间重新平衡。作者提出的张力分类体系为未来在边缘化社区中开展语音数据收集提供了指导框架，强调应从社区出发、以社区为中心进行数据创建。

Abstract: In this paper, we examine speech datasets for their inclusion of LGBTQIA+, or queer, voices and provide a taxonomy of tensions to better understand why there is a lack of such voices in current speech technology datasets. Through an audit of six diverse speech datasets, we find that measurable queer representation is low (0-1.4% of speakers) - insufficient for robust disparity measurement. We take this community as a case study to consider what challenges and tensions are associated with collecting speech data from marginalized communities. For comparison, we audit an additional two datasets from the speech sciences that were created by, for, and with the queer community. We note that many customs in speech dataset collection efforts in AI and speech technology research may conflict with values emphasized in participatory approaches with marginalized communities, and provide a taxonomy describing these tensions.

</details>


### [118] [Towards participatory speech dataset curation: A queer case study and conceptual framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25496)
*Brooklyn Sheppard,Anaelia Ovalle,Adina Williams,Levent Sagun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过LGBTQIA+（酷儿）社区的案例研究，论证了建立参与式语音数据集创建框架的必要性，并提出了一个由边缘化社区共同设计、包含社区定义、项目制定、参与模式和个人自主性四个重叠双向过程的语音数据策展概念框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者旨在解决语音数据收集中对酷儿等边缘化社区的伦理问题，特别是回应针对该社区AI技术（如所谓"gaydar"识别技术）造成的实际伤害，指出常见的语音数据采集方法不适合酷儿说话者参与，因此需要一种更具包容性和参与性的框架。

Method: 本文采用综述与概念建构的方法：首先回顾常见语音数据收集实践及其对酷儿社区的不适用性；其次梳理参与式AI与该社区的协作努力，以及其他边缘化社区在语音数据收集中参与式实践的尝试；最终基于co-design（协同设计）和知识共享的洞见，开发出包含"社区定义、项目制定、参与模式、个人自主性"四个重叠且双向流程的概念框架。

Result: 提出了一套由边缘化社区"由社区主导、为社区服务、与社区共建"的参与式语音数据策展框架，其核心特征是通过四个相互重叠、双向互动的过程实现真正的参与式数据治理。

Conclusion: 参与式语音数据创建应超越单纯的采集手段，通过让社区深度参与定义过程，确保数据收集尊重个人自主性，从而减少AI对边缘化社区造成的潜在危害，为更具伦理性的语音数据实践提供理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we motivate the need for a participatory speech dataset creation framework through a case study of the LGBTQIA+, or queer, community - a community with documented concerns about AI and reported harms, including attempts to develop 'gaydar' technologies that purportedly identify individuals as queer. We review common speech data collection practices, why these methods may be unsuitable for engaging with queer speakers, and discuss previous efforts in participatory AI with queer community engagement, as well as participatory endeavours specific to speech data collection for other marginalized communities. From this review, we develop a conceptual framework for participatory speech data curation by, for, and with marginalized communities drawing on insights from co-design and knowledge sharing. We propose a framework comprising overlapping and two-way processes of defining a community, project formulation, modes of participation, and personal autonomy.

</details>


### [119] [SMTB: Fast Structure-Mapping with Tight Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25508)
*Daniel Weitekamp,Christopher MacLellan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种新的结构映射算法SMTB（Structure-Mapping with Tight Bounds），该算法比现有SME引擎快5-15倍，在大型嵌套领域中的映射查找能力提升约50%。SMTB隶属于更广泛的认知推理引擎（CRE）项目，提供Python接口并兼容多种语言。与SME偏向树状谓词逻辑中的高阶对应不同，SMTB最大化关系连通性而不优待高阶关系，因此能同时适用于任意关系图和树状嵌套谓词逻辑领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的结构映射引擎（SME）在处理大型嵌套领域时性能不佳，且其偏向高阶关系（higher-order relations）的设计在非树状结构或任意关系图中会产生失败模式。作者希望提出一个更高效、更通用的结构映射算法，同时构建一个灵活的认知系统框架。

Method: SMTB采用紧密边界（tight bounds）策略来加速结构映射过程，通过最大化关系连通性而非偏好高阶关系来实现映射。该方法设计为适用于任意关系图，而非仅限于树状谓词逻辑。同时构建了支持多语言、提供Python接口、底层为C++实现的CRE框架。

Result: SMTB比SME快5-15倍；在大型嵌套领域中的映射查找能力比SME提升约50%；在5845个来自SME语料库的领域对上进行了对比评估，结果表明SMTB能避免SME在偏好高阶关系时遇到的失败模式。

Conclusion: SMTB通过不偏向高阶关系的设计，实现了对任意关系图结构映射的广泛适用性，同时在性能和映射质量上均优于SME。该算法与CRE框架的结合为认知系统提供了更灵活、更高效的结构映射解决方案。

Abstract: Structure-mapping forms analogies by aligning systems of relationally connected elements based on shared structure instead of surface features. We introduce a new structure-mapping algorithm: Structure-Mapping with Tight Bounds (SMTB) that is 5--15x faster than the structure-mapping engine (SME) and about 50\% better at finding mappings in large nested domains. SMTB is part of the broader Cognitive Rule Engine (CRE) project, a flexible multi-language-compatible framework with an accessible Python interface to state-of-the-art C++ implementations of core algorithms commonly used in cognitive systems such as pattern matching, planning, and structure-mapping. CRE and SMTB are designed to work with a wide range of representation choices. Unlike SME, which biases higher-order correspondences in tree-like predicate logic, SMTB maximizes relational connectivity without privileging higher-order relations. This allows SMTB to work just as well over arbitrary relational graphs as it does in tree-like domains of nested predicate logic. We discuss situations where privileging "higher-orderness" in structure-mapping can cause issues, and illustrate how SMTB avoids failure modes that SME would encounter in these situations. We also provide an evaluation comparing SMTB to SME v4 over 5845 domain pairs from the SME corpus.

</details>


### [120] [Weakly Supervised Quantum Error Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25555)
*Seyed Mohamad Ali Tousi,G. N. DeSouza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需理想标签的量子错误缓解方法。传统监督学习方法需要无噪声经典模拟生成理想输出，但在系统规模增大时代价指数增长。作者改用16种启发式标签函数（如稳定子/奇偶校验约束、弛豫与读出特性、局部深度、门计数、邻近活动等），通过概率标签模型融合其分歧，得到每量子位错误概率并作为读出信道的逆来缓解测量分布。在两个IBM设备上对147,000个五量子位电路进行了验证，消除了24.3%（Algiers）和28.8%（Hanoi）的KL散度，优于最强分析基线（15.4%和21.5%）。在有理想标签可用的场景中监督模型仍更强，但该方法针对的是无法计算理想标签的规模区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督式量子错误缓解方法依赖理想标签，而理想输出需通过无噪声经典模拟获得，其成本随系统规模指数增长——恰恰在错误缓解最关键的场景中，可靠监督标签不可用。作者旨在探索是否可以用廉价的、不完美但来自电路结构与硬件校准的信号替代理想标签，从而突破监督学习的规模瓶颈。

Method: 作者构建了16种启发式标签函数，涵盖稳定子与奇偶性约束、弛豫和读出特性、局部电路深度、门计数和邻近活动等信号来源。这些函数产生的分歧标签通过概率标签模型进行融合，输出每量子位的错误概率，并将其解释为读出信道，取该信道之逆来缓解测量得到的分布。整个过程无需任何理想输出来参与训练。

Result: 在147,000个五量子位电路上，分别于IBM的Algiers和Hanoi两台设备上执行。该方法消除了24.3%（Algiers）与28.8%（Hanoi）相对于理想分布的KL散度，显著优于最强已发表分析基线（15.4%和21.5%），优势在两个设备上均成立且远超bootstrap置信区间。

Conclusion: 该方法明确针对监督标签不可计算的规模区域——即错误缓解最需要的场景；在理想标签可得的情况下，监督神经模型仍更强，作者量化了这一差距而非回避。本文方法的价值在于填补监督学习在无标签大规模场景中的空白。

Abstract: Supervised approaches to quantum error mitigation learn a map from noisy circuit outputs to ideal ones, and therefore require the ideal outputs. Producing those ideal outputs demands noiseless classical simulation, whose cost grows exponentially with system size, so supervision is unavailable in exactly the regime where mitigation matters most. We ask whether cheap, individually unreliable signals drawn from circuit structure and hardware calibration can take the place of ideal labels. We assemble sixteen heuristic labeling functions (stabilizer and parity constraints, relaxation and readout characteristics, local depth, gate counts, and neighboring activity), reconcile their disagreements with a probabilistic label model, and read the resulting per-qubit error probabilities as a readout channel whose inverse mitigates the measured distribution. No ideal output enters the training path. On $147{,}000$ five-qubit circuits executed on two IBM devices, the method removes $24.3\%$ (Algiers) and $28.8\%$ (Hanoi) of the Kullback-Leibler divergence to the ideal distribution, against $15.4\%$ and $21.5\%$ for the strongest published analytical baseline, a margin that holds on both devices and lies far outside its bootstrap interval. Supervised neural models trained on ideal distributions remain stronger where such labels exist, and we quantify that gap rather than setting it aside; the method's claim is to the regime where they do not, since the labels they require cannot be computed for the circuits mitigation is needed for. The codes will be released shortly.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [CogenPVG: Cognitive-Enhanced Reflective Multi-Agent Framework for Persuasive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25821)
*Yuntian Xiao,Shoulong Zhang,Wenfeng Song,Yan Wang,Yi Chen,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出CogenPVG，一个基于认知增强的反思式多智能体框架，用于说服性视频生成任务，通过将生成过程解耦为论证推理、故事板规划、资产创建和后期编辑四个阶段，并在每个阶段采用生成器-评论家对进行反思式细化，结合精细加工可能性模型（ELM）与批判性思维理论，实现高效的说服性视频自动生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 说服性视频生成（PVG）虽有价值但研究不足，现有多模态生成技术难以自动生成具有显著说服力、类似人工制作的视频，且缺乏针对通用说服主题（非仅限于商业目的）的系统性方法。

Method: 构建多智能体框架，将复杂的说服视频生成过程分为四个顺序阶段：论证推理、故事板规划、资产创建和后期编辑，模拟人类视频制作流程。每个阶段配备生成器和评论家智能体对，基于心理学说服理论（ELM）进行反思式细化：论证推理阶段利用批判性思维理论生成高逻辑性和可信的推理，通过ELM中心路径实现认知增强；其余三个阶段依据启发式理论生成和优化多模态资产并组装成视频，作为ELM的外围路径。

Result: 通过大量实验和全面分析，证明该框架在说服性能上达到最佳，验证了所提多智能体框架在PVG任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: CogenPVG是首个关注通用说服主题（不限于商业用途）的PVG工作，成功将认知心理学理论与多智能体协作机制结合，为说服性视频自动化生成提供了有效且可推广的解决方案。

Abstract: Persuasive video generation (PVG) is a valuable yet under-explored research topic. Despite the significant advances in multimodal content generation, AI-empowered automated creation of human-made-like videos with substantial persuasiveness remains a formidable challenge. In this paper, we propose CogenPVG, a novel Cognitive-Enhanced reflective multi-agent framework tailored for Persuasive Video Generation task. Given the topic and stance from the user, we decouple the sophisticated generation process into four sequential stages: argument reasoning, storyboard planning, asset creation, and post-editing, imitating the workflow of human video producers. To ensure high persuasiveness, each stage is equipped with a pair of generator and critic agents, following a reflective refinement scheme grounded in a solid psychological theory of persuasion, the Elaboration Likelihood Model (ELM). In the argument reasoning stage, we generate highly logical and credible reasoning thoughts under the guidance of critical thinking theory, enabling cognitive enhancement via the central route of the ELM. For the other three stages, we generate and optimize multimodal assets, assembling them into a persuasive video guided by theories of heuristics, as the peripheral route of the ELM. To the best of our knowledge, CogenPVG is the first work focused on general persuasive topics, without being confined to commercial purposes. Extensive experiments and comprehensive analysis demonstrate that our framework achieves the best persuasion performance, thereby proving the effectiveness of our proposed multi-agent framework for the PVG task.

</details>


### [122] [TV-AudioRemover: Joint Text-Visual Guided Sound Removal with Multi-Task Hard-Mixture Curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25864)
*Xinyue Guo,Jianxuan Yang,Daiguo Zhou,Jiagao Hu,Yuxuan Chen,Fei Wang,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出TV-AudioRemover，一种文本-视觉联合引导的目标声音移除框架。它利用视觉编辑后的视频和自然语言指令，从原始音频混合中抑制与已移除视觉对象关联的声音，解决视频物体移除后声轨残留造成的视听不一致问题，并在自建百万级数据与AV-Remove-Bench基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频修复模型仅对像素进行处理，而音频编辑模型（尤其声音移除任务）通常由文本驱动，依赖单一模态控制。相比多模态引导（提供更强的语义基础和时序同步线索），单模态控制效果有限，导致视觉物体被移除后其声学痕迹仍保留在声轨中，产生明显视听不一致。

Method: 1) 数据构建：设计流水线构建单物体视听对齐的百万级样本数据集，并合成混合-目标对用于训练；2) 架构增强：引入任务令牌、泛化指令建模和模态特定全局引导，以利用视觉上下文并遵循指令意图；3) 训练策略：采用多任务训练强化任务角色理解，微调阶段使用硬混合课程（语义相近的声学混合物）增强细粒度源区分；4) 评测：提出AV-Remove-Bench基准、专用客观指标和基于MLLM的评估协议。

Result: 在主观和客观指标上均达到当前最优（state-of-the-art）性能，实验验证了文本-视觉多模态引导相比单一文本控制的有效性。

Conclusion: TV-AudioRemover证明多模态（视觉+文本）引导相结合能显著提升目标声音移除的效果，为视听一致的视频物体编辑提供解决方案，同时贡献了大规模数据集与系统化评测基准，为后续研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Visual object removal can eliminate a target from video frames, yet its acoustic trace persists in the soundtrack, causing obvious audio-visual inconsistency. Existing video inpainting models operate solely on pixels, while audio editing models, especially for the sound removal task, are typically driven by text and therefore rely on limited single-modal control, which is less effective than multimodal guidance that provides stronger semantic grounding and temporal synchronization cues. In this paper, we present Text-Visual Guided Sound Removal (TV-AudioRemover), a target sound removal framework that leverages the visually edited video together with a natural-language instruction to suppress the sound associated with the removed visual object from the original audio mixture. To acquire high-quality training data, we devise a pipeline to construct a million-scale dataset of single-object audio-visual aligned samples, from which we synthesize mixture-target pairs customized for model training. To effectively leverage visual context and follow instruction intent, we augment the model architecture with task tokens, generalizable instruction modeling, and modality-specific global guidance. We further adopt multi-task training to strengthen task-role comprehension, and employ a hard-mixture curriculum that leverages semantically similar acoustic mixtures during fine-tuning to enhance fine-grained source discrimination. To support evaluation, we present AV-Remove-Bench, a comprehensive audio-visual object removal benchmark, along with dedicated objective metrics and an MLLM-based evaluation protocol. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on both subjective and objective metrics. Project page: https://yjx-research.github.io/TV-AudioRemover/.

</details>


### [123] [KeyBound: Keyed and Host-Bound Learned Audio Watermarking for Speech Provenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26235)
*Bangshuo Zhu,Yuxin Cao,Weifei Jin,Fusen Guo,Huadong Mo,Jingling Xue,Wei Song*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: KeyBound 是一种基于学习的音频水印方法，通过引入秘密密钥（secret key）和宿主感知载波（host-aware carrier）两个经典水印要素，解决了现有学习式音频水印在溯源（provenance）场景下的三大缺陷：被未授权方读取、可被移植到无关音频、以及在现代生成模型重合成后失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习式音频水印仅以"在固定信号失真目录（噪声、压缩、滤波、重采样）下的载荷恢复"作为评判标准，这对溯源而言必要但不充分。作为溯源证据的水印不应被未授权方读取、不应可被移植到无关音频、也不应在经过现代生成模型重合成后消失。因此需要一种密钥化、宿主绑定的水印方案。

Method: KeyBound 用秘密密钥对载荷（payload）进行掩码，再通过由冻结的宿主频谱表示调制的载波嵌入被掩码的比特。秘密密钥控制载荷的访问权限，而宿主条件化的载波能抵抗直接的载波移植攻击。此外设置一个与密钥无关的存在性检测头（presence head），允许任何一方检测水印的存在，但只有持有密钥者才能读取归属（attribution）信息。

Result: 在 LibriSpeech 上对比 WavMark、AudioSeal 和 Timbre：KeyBound 在频谱降噪器（该降噪器使所有基线检测失效）下保持 1.00 的检测准确率和 0.98 的比特准确率；无密钥时解码仅达随机水平；能拒绝移植载波。检测能力可迁移到持出集（held-out）的 DAC 和 BigVGAN 重合成，但精确载荷恢复有所下降。同时，在单样本均匀性假设下，错误密钥通过验证规则的概率至多为 2.1×10⁻³。

Conclusion: 语音溯源应被重新定义为一种密钥化、宿主绑定的归属（attribution）问题，而非在预先固定的信号失真目录下恢复载荷的问题。

Abstract: Audio watermarking is a proactive route to attributing synthetic speech to its source. Learned audio watermarks are typically judged by payload recovery after a fixed catalog of signal distortions such as noise, compression, filtering, and resampling. That test is necessary but not sufficient for provenance. A mark offered as evidence of origin should not be readable by an unauthorized party, should not be transferable to unrelated audio, and should not vanish when the recording is re-synthesized by a modern generative model. We present KeyBound, a learned audio watermark that restores the two ingredients classical watermarking supplied and learned schemes set aside, a secret key and a host-aware carrier. KeyBound masks the payload with a secret key and embeds the masked bits through a carrier modulated by a frozen spectral representation of the host, so the key governs payload access while the host-conditioned carrier resists direct transplantation. A key-independent presence head lets any party detect a mark, whereas only a key holder reads its attribution, and under the single-sample uniformity assumption a wrong-key decode clears our verification rule with probability at most $2.1\times10^{-3}$. On LibriSpeech against WavMark, AudioSeal, and Timbre, KeyBound holds 1.00 detection accuracy and 0.98 bit accuracy under a spectral denoiser that costs every baseline its detection, decodes at chance without the key, and rejects transplanted carriers. Detection further transfers to held-out DAC and BigVGAN re-synthesis, though exact payload recovery degrades. Speech provenance is thus better posed as a keyed, host-bound attribution problem than as the recovery of a payload under a catalog of signal distortions fixed in advance.

</details>


### [124] [ROAM-ASD: Robust Open-World Active Speaker Detection with Flexible Multimodal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26648)
*Pu Wang,Yujun Wang,Hugo Van hamme*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出ROAM-ASD，一种鲁棒的视听主动说话人检测框架，通过统一联合自注意力机制联合建模音频、全脸和细粒度嘴部 representations，并采用模态dropout增强对缺失输入的鲁棒性，在五个基准上均达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASD系统在跨域场景或观测不完整（如面部或音频缺失）时性能显著下降，需要更鲁棒的视听关联建模方法。

Method: ROAM-ASD采用统一联合自注意力机制处理音频、全脸和嘴部三路输入流，通过模态无关的查询token实现各模态间的直接交互；引入模态dropout策略增强对输入流缺失时的鲁棒性。

Result: 在WASD、UniTalk、AVA、ASW和Talkies五个基准上分别达到98.8%、87.9%、96.5%、99.3%和98.2%的mAP，较先前最优系统分别提升5.1、4.7、0.9、1.0和2.1个百分点；零样本跨数据集泛化能力显著提升，且对缺失观测保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: ROAM-ASD通过联合多模态建模与模态dropout训练，有效解决了ASD在挑战性场景和观测不完整条件下的性能退化问题，实现跨基准的SOTA性能和良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Active speaker detection (ASD) requires reliable association between visible faces and acoustic speech, yet existing systems often degrade under challenging domains or incomplete observations. We introduce ROAM-ASD, a robust audiovisual framework that jointly models audio, full-face, and fine-grained mouth representations. A unified joint self-attention mechanism processes all input streams together with modality-agnostic query tokens, enabling direct interaction among available modality inputs. Modality dropout further improves robustness when input streams are unavailable. ROAM-ASD achieves state-of-the-art performance across five ASD benchmarks: 98.8% mAP on WASD, 87.9% on UniTalk, 96.5% on AVA, 99.3% on ASW, and 98.2% on Talkies, improving over previous best systems by 5.1, 4.7, 0.9, 1.0, and 2.1 mAP points, respectively. ROAM-ASD also substantially improves zero-shot cross-dataset generalization and remains robust to missing observations.

</details>
